# Engine seams

A seam is an engine gap a card in the catalog (`server/internal/cards/effects`)
is waiting on: an event kind, a cost component, a prompt shape, or a
primitive that `server/internal/game` does not yet provide. A seam is not a
bug: bugs found by the roadmap sweep (issues #446, #478, #482, #489, #492)
are tracked and fixed on their own, separately from this list.

This file's open table is generated from the seam registry in
`server/internal/roadmap/registry.go` ([ADR 0092](decisions/0092-public-roadmap-and-site-portal.md),
issue #1386), which also feeds the public roadmap page. The table began as the
seam registry for the card-coverage roadmap (Discussion #559 item 6, issue
#580), replacing the seam lists that used to live only in agent prompts and in
scattered batch-issue comments.

The rule going forward: a batch PR that skips a card for a seam appends the
card's name to that seam's `Waiting` list **in the registry**, adding an entry
there if the seam has none, and regenerates the table with
`go test ./internal/roadmap/ -update`. Do not edit the table by hand:
`TestSeamsTableIsCurrent` fails a PR whose table and registry disagree. A
seam moves to Closed on the day the engine PR that closes it lands — flip its
`Status` to implemented in the registry and write its prose as a new fragment
under [`engine-seams/closed/`](engine-seams/closed/) (see
[Closed seams](#closed-seams) below; do not edit that list).
`TestWaitingCardsAreNotComplete` fails
the day a waiting card is registered complete, which is usually the first
sign that a seam has closed.

A seam that blocks exactly one card is still usually noted on that card's own
batch issue rather than given an entry; it earns one the moment a second card
waits on it, or when it has a `seam` issue of its own.

**Status** is the registry's verdict after checking the code: *partial* when
part of the seam has shipped, *missing* when nothing a player can use has.
**What is missing** is the entry's `EngineNotes`, written for engineers; the
public page shows its own one-sentence `Summary` and `Missing` instead.

**Cards waiting** are the cards batch issues and batch PRs recorded as
skipped for the seam that are still not in the catalog as complete.

**Unblocks** comes from the card-coverage audit of 2026-09-16, which
classified every gap card on the 60 batch issues (#294-#313, #383-#403,
#448-#467; 5,084 cards) and gave, for each seam, the number of cards where it
was the *only* core blocker. It is a classifier's attribution, not a skip
list: the verifiers sampled the only-blocker cards and found between about 5%
and 85% of them also blocked by something else or misattributed, so read it as
an upper bound. For a row tracked by one of #742-#765, that issue has the
verified estimate. **—** means the audit had no figure for the seam, or that
its figure predates a half of the seam that has since shipped (the entry's
notes say which). The public roadmap ranks "up next" by Unblocks plus the
number of cards waiting.

**2026-09-24 re-triage (#1386).** Every open row was checked against the code
when the table moved into the registry. Four rows had fully closed and moved
to Closed seams: dice rolls and coin flips; layer invalidation on hand, life,
attack, graveyard and spells cast; the pick-from-exile or pick-from-graveyard
prompt; and the post-departure "exiled with this" record. Several others had
shipped most of what they described and now say only what is left. Four open
`seam` issues that had no row got one (#1275, #1278, #1379, #1382). The old
Count column is gone: it tallied recorded skips, and Cards waiting is now that
list itself.

| Paradigm | missing | keyword: three clauses and no shape for any of them. The spell EXILES ITSELF instead of going to the graveyard; a per-NAME "have you resolved a spell with this name yet" record is kept for the rest of the game; and from then on, at the beginning of each of the controller's first main phases, they are offered a cast of a **copy** of the card out of exile for no mana. The third clause is the one with nothing behind it: every cast permission in the engine ([ADR 0066](decisions/0066-granted-cast-and-play-permissions.md)) opens the CARD, and "cast a copy of a card in exile" exists nowhere — `CreateTokenCopy` mints a permanent, not a spell on the stack. The other two are reachable on their own (`CastPermission.ExileOnResolution` is the self-exile's sibling, and a per-name record is a tally), but a card shipped with only those would be all drawback and no payoff, so both Lessons defer the whole keyword. Decorum Dissertation (S58 deck requests, #2077) waits for the same reason; its draw two and lose 2 life is ordinary. | Echocasting Symposium; Improvisation Capstone; Decorum Dissertation | — | #1866, #1112 (deck tracker) |
| Triggers once for each counter | missing | harvester: one `EventCounterPlaced` per placement (post-change total), and a trigger fires at most once per event. "Whenever one or more counters are put" fits (Herd Baloth, Scurry Oak); "whenever a counter is put" is one trigger per counter (CR 603.2c), so a placement of two must be two stack objects. Likely a per-event multiplicity on `TriggeredAbility`, fed by the delta `b33CountersPlacedDelta` reads. Found landing the evolve cards (#1805). Protean Hydra (ADR 0108 PR 8, #1906) is the removal side: "Whenever a +1/+1 counter is removed from this creature" triggers once per counter removed (its ruling), and nothing triggers on a counter's removal at all yet. | Fathom Mage; Protean Hydra | — | #1841 |

Savage Summoning's "can be cast as though it had flash" has to be read BEFORE the cast (a timing grant, `PlayerStatic.Timing`, that is spent by the cast it permits) and its "enters with an additional +1/+1 counter" after it (a mark on the spell read by the entry-counter replacement in `game/entry_counters.go`), and neither shape exists. Its "That spell can't be countered" is the promise that shipped. Saheeli, the Gifted's +1, "The next spell you cast this turn has affinity for artifacts" (S58 deck requests, #2077), is a cost promise spent by the next cast at CR 601.2f; affinity exists only as a spell's own cost clause (`game/cost_modifier.go`) and no one-use cost modifier rides on a player. Her Servo +1 and her −7 are expressible. | Savage Summoning; Saheeli, the Gifted | — | #1852 (S50 tracker #1784; moved off #1806 by ADR 0106 PR 3) |

Fiery Annihilation's damage and "if that creature would die this turn, exile it instead" shipped with ADR 0108 PR 1. Goblin Welder (S58 deck requests, #2077) targets "artifact a player controls and target artifact card in that player's graveyard", the second clause bound to the first's controller, and then swaps the two at once if both are still legal. | Fiery Annihilation; Breaking of the Fellowship; Goblin Welder | — | #1943 (found landing ADR 0108 PR 1, #1886) |

The key is a closed enum rather than a func, so the ADR 0041 closure ratchet gains no route. The announce gate refuses a set with two keys, the CR 608.2b re-check judges the survivors, `fillableCountLocked` answers the largest group (so Pestilent Cauldron's exact four is offered only when one graveyard holds four), the retarget offer narrows to the staying slots' graveyard while the gate judges the final set (CR 115.7e), the enumerator builds sets inside one group, and the view ships `same: {label, keys}` for the picker. 21 cards shipped. **Still open:** four cards of the family are held for their OTHER text, each with no seam row of its own yet: Martyr of Bones ("Reveal X black cards from your hand" as an activation cost — `AbilityCost` has no reveal component), Qutrub Forayer ("target creature that was dealt damage this turn" — nothing records damage dealt to a creature this turn), Feral Deathgorger // Dusk Sight (an Omen, CR 720 — Scryfall lays it out as an Adventure, the Adventure path always exiles the resolved half, and CR 720.3d shuffles an Omen into its owner's library instead) and Spellweaver Helix ("copy the other. … cast the copy" — casting a copy of a card in exile). The discard goes through `discardCardsLocked` with `DiscardCauseEffect` and the sacrifice through `SacrificeAllThenForEffect`; either may pause, and the paused entry rides a frozen copy of the event (cloned again when the continuation runs). A redirected entry is now MOVED, by `moveRedirectedEntryLocked` inside the one entry finisher, where three sites used to treat it as a cancel and two put the card onto the battlefield anyway; a window that cannot ask runs a shockland's, reveal-land's or Mox's decline (`declineIsReplace`); and the apply-loop and `ResolveReplacementOrder` share one question dispatcher (`offerOwnQuestionLocked`). Shipped on Mox Diamond and the seven "sacrifice … instead" lands. One predicate, `canAttackTargetWithLocked(attacker, target)` — CR 506.2's controller check plus the creature's own list, with the owner read live (CR 108.3, so a copy uses its own owner) and battles never refused — is called by both declaration verbs and by the CR 508.1d search (`attackRequirementCandidatesLocked`), so with only its owner to attack the creature owes nothing. The enumerator walks a per-attacker `AttackTargetsForAttackerForEffect`, so the ADR 0105 digest's `attack_targets` and the client's per-attacker gate follow; `Turn.AttackTargets` stays per seat for prices and limits. CR 508.7b's reselection and CR 508.4c's entry attacking keep the controller-only check. The card view carries `attack_target_restrictions` (owner resolved to a seat), which draws the "Can't attack <name>" chip. "Spend only mana produced by basic lands / creatures to cast this spell" (Imperiosaur, Myr Superion; Security Rhox's alternative cost from Treasures) restricts by SOURCE, which `ManaToken.SourceKinds` records and no cost reads. Emblazoned Golem's "Spend only colored mana on X. No more than one mana of each color may be spent this way" is a distinct-colours cap with no shape. One candidate walk (`ExilePermanentsOptionsForEffect`) feeds the view's `exile_permanent_options`, the enumerator and the validator; the picks ride `exile_permanent_ids` on both actions; the payment is the one exit primitive with `MustSettleNow` (leaves-the-battlefield triggers fire, dies and sacrifice triggers do not; a commander is asked CR 903.9 first) and lands on `PaidCost.Exiled`. `PermanentInfo.ManaValue` answers "the exiled creature's mana value" as it last existed (Food Chain). Each is a field on the filter plus a line in `Matches` (the type says it grows a field when a card needs one), and the filter is snapshot data, so the field is additive. | Counters put on a permanent together, by kind | missing | harvester: one `EventCounterPlaced` per kind per permanent, so a placement of two kinds would be two triggers that could each target a different creature, stronger than printed. Attributing a placement to "you" is best effort (All Will Be One's caveat). Aragorn's first ability works with today's option pick and keyword counters. Found landing ADR 0114 PR 5. | Aragorn, Company Leader | — | #2150 |
| Controlling another player | missing | decision routing: every prompt, priority pass and action in `game/` is answered by the seat it belongs to, and nothing records a player-controlling effect (CR 723.1) or lets one seat make another's decisions with that seat's resources (CR 723.5a) while seeing what that seat sees (CR 723.4). Emrakul's "you gain control of target opponent during that player's next turn" is CR 723.1's turn-long form, followed by an ordinary extra turn for that player; Word of Command is one of CR 723.2's two limited forms, during which the controlled player must play the chosen card using only their lands' mana abilities. The rest of Emrakul (its discount for each card type in your graveyard, flying, trample, protection from instants) exists. | Emrakul, the Promised End; Word of Command | — | #2164 (S58 tracker #2077) |
| One spell of each card type | missing | cast permission: `game.CastPermission` (`game/cast_permission.go`) spends one flat `CastsLeft` (#1729) and its `PermissionFilter` matches a card by fixed type flags; nothing records which card type a cast through the permission used, so "one of each type" can't be enforced, and a card with two permanent types has to choose which one it uses as it is played (Muldrotha's reminder text). Muldrotha's is a standing graveyard permission renewed each of your turns, with a land play beside it; Aminatou's Augury's is made as it resolves, over the eight exiled cards, free, for each nonland card type, until end of turn (its land half already works). `budgeted-free-casts` (#2017) is the mana value sibling. | Aminatou's Augury; Muldrotha, the Gravetide | — | #2167 (S58 tracker #2077) |
| Draws replaced by a choice | missing | replacement: the `RepEventDraw` replacements (`effects/draw_replacements.go`, `drawCardLocked` in `game/mutations.go`) rewrite the count or the drawing player, or cancel the draw, inside a `Replace` that can't pause for a card prompt, and a draw inside a sequence ("draw two cards") must finish its replacement before the next draw begins (CR 121.6b). Forbidden Crypt returns a card of your choice from your graveyard to your hand instead, and you lose if you can't; Underrealm Lich looks at three and keeps one. Dredge (#2127) needs the same pause. Forbidden Crypt's second ability is the static shape of `ModExileInsteadOfYourGraveyard` and works. | Forbidden Crypt; Underrealm Lich | — | #2168 (S58 tracker #2077) |
| Something that happens because you cast the card you were allowed to | missing | cast permission: `game.CastPermission` (`game/cast_permission.go`) carries a price, a timing, a lapse and a cast count, nothing runs when a cast through it happens, and the spell on the stack doesn't record which permission let it be cast. Conduit of Worlds' tap ability lets you cast the target card for its mana cost as the ability resolves (the `LapseOnPass` shape cascade uses, CR 608.2g), and only that cast is followed by a ban on casting more spells (`RestrictCasting`, `effects/cast_ban.go`); a ban written without knowing whether the card was cast would either stop that cast or bind a player who declined it. "If you haven't cast a spell this turn" reads the turn's cast tally, and the first line, "You may play lands from your graveyard", is a standing graveyard permission that works. | Conduit of Worlds | — | #2173 (S58 tracker #2077) |
| Blight X as an additional cost | missing | cost: `game.AdditionalCost.Blight` (`game/additional_cost.go`) is a fixed count paid through `CastSpellParams.BlightIDs`, and `effects/teamwork_blight.go` notes that "blight X" is not this component. Needs an X announced with the cost (CR 107.3a), capped by the card's own limit (Soul Immolation: no greater than the greatest toughness among creatures you control), paid onto one creature and read back by the spell as X on resolution. | Soul Immolation | — | #2174 (S58 tracker #2077) |
| Adventures on a land | missing | adventure: `grantAdventureCastFromExileLocked` (`game/adventure.go`) grants face 0 with `CastOnly: true`, and its comment reads CR 715.3d as "cast". The pinned text says "For as long as that card remains exiled, that player may play it", so for a land main half the grant has to be a play permission (a land play that spends the turn's land drop). Casting the Adventure half from the hand is untested for a card whose main face is a land. Final Fantasy's Town lands have this shape. | Jidoor, Aristocratic Capital // Overture | — | #2176 (S58 tracker #2077) |
| Opponents can't make you discard or sacrifice | missing | rules gate: the sacrifice path (`game/sacrifice.go`) and the discard path (`discardCardsLocked`) take a player and the cards and never ask who controls the instruction; Sigarda, Host of Herons ships without its sacrifice clause for this reason. Needs a gate keyed on the controller of the spell or ability that instructs, asked by every effect-driven discard and sacrifice and never by a cost the player chose to pay. Tamiyo's +1 (name a card, reveal four) and −3 work. | Tamiyo, Collector of Tales | — | #2178 (S58 tracker #2077) |
| Playing exiled cards you don't own that carry a counter | missing | permission filter: a standing exile permission reaches every exiled card (`permissionReachesPileLocked`, `game/cast_permission.go`), and `PermissionFilter` matches by card type only, with no owner clause and no counter clause. Tinybones needs both, during your turn (`TimingYourTurnOnly`) and with mana of any type (`AnyType`), which exist. A per-card grant made by its discard trigger would miss cards stashed while it wasn't on the battlefield, so that build would ship as Caveats; the standing filter is the faithful one. | Tinybones, Bauble Burglar | — | #2179 (S58 tracker #2077) |
| Abilities that resolve even if their target becomes illegal | missing | resolution: `spellAllTargetsIllegalLocked` (`game/mutations.go`) removes any item whose targeted slots are all illegal, with no exemption, so Gilded Drake's "If you don't or can't make an exchange, sacrifice this creature" would never run when its target is gone, stronger than printed. Needs a flag on the ability that skips the CR 608.2b removal while the effect still treats the illegal target as unaffected. `ExchangeControlForEffect` already reports whether an exchange happened. | Gilded Drake | — | #2182 (S58 tracker #2077) |

<!-- END GENERATED OPEN SEAMS -->

## Closed seams

This list is generated, one entry per file in
[`engine-seams/closed/`](engine-seams/closed/) (#1461, Discussion #1231). A
PR that closes a seam adds one file, `<issue>-<slug>.md`, and does not touch
this list: CI regenerates it on every push to `develop` and `main`, so two
PRs never edit the same lines. A fragment looks like this:

```
---
title: "Phasing"
date: 2026-09-23
issues: [1199]
pr: 1251
---
**Phasing** (#1199, [ADR 0084](decisions/0084-phasing.md)) — what closed, and what is still open.
```

`title` is double-quoted and must match the bold heading the entry starts
with; `issues` is a list (`[]` when the entry names none); `pr` is optional,
since the number is not known until the PR is open. Write links relative to
`docs/`, as they render here. Newer fragments list first, by date. The
entries migrated from the hand-written list keep their old order after them
(`legacy_order`), because many of them say "the row above"; a new entry
should name the issue it means instead. `TestClosedSeamsAreCurrent` fails a
PR whose fragment is malformed or that writes an entry into the list by hand.

<!-- BEGIN GENERATED CLOSED SEAMS — generated from docs/engine-seams/closed/*.md and refreshed by CI on every push to develop and main; add a fragment there instead of editing this list (manual refresh: go test ./internal/roadmap/ -run TestClosedSeamsAreCurrent -update-closed) -->

- **A guarded pay-unless with a custom decline** (#2365, CR 118.12 / CR 117.4, [ADR 0018 §6](decisions/0018-triggers-on-the-stack.md)) — "<do something to> target spell unless its controller pays {N}" where the consequence is not a counter. Only `CounterUnlessPaid` (#951) held the table while the guarded spell sat on the stack; Divert written with a plain `PayUnless` let the spell it targeted resolve before its controller answered. The halt was already derived from the guarded object (`PendingChoice.GuardsStackItem`, read live by the gate and by `internal/legal` through one predicate), so the fix is one field: `game.CounterUnlessPaidPrompt.OnDecline`. Nil is the printed counter and `CounterUnlessPaid` is unchanged; a non-nil effect runs on a decline (or an unaffordable "pay") under the same re-check, so it never runs for an object that has already left the stack. Card side: `effects.GuardedPayUnless{StackID, Cost, Question, OnDecline}`, whose decline is a `*Context` bound to the same stack item, so Divert's controller (not the payer) picks the new target through `ChangeTargets`. No wire, enumerator or protocol change. **1 card:** Divert (`full`). A sweep of the Oct 5 dump for "unless … pays" on a spell or ability found no other card whose consequence is not a counter; the uncatalogued counter-unless-pays cards (Temur Charm, Phantom Interference, Aether Spike) are different gaps and are not this shape.

- **How much life a player lost at once** (#2183, CR 603.2c, CR 120.3a) — the engine now keeps the life each player lost in the live event batch (`game/life_batch.go`), written where a life total actually moves (both damage arms and the CR 614 life change), so it is the total after every replacement and counts life paid. A `TriggeredAbility` with `AtBatchEnd` set is staged when its event arrives and asked once, with the batch's final totals, at the next priority grant or as the next batch opens; combined with `OncePerBatch` it fires once per batch. `effects.WheneverOneOrMoreOpponentsEachLoseExactly(n, …)` is the card-side shape. Two 1-damage sources into one opponent in one combat damage step are a loss of 2 and do nothing; one drain of 1 to each of two opponents is one trigger; separate resolutions and steps are separate occurrences. Undo carries the totals and staged triggers; a persisted snapshot drops them (weaker than printed, never stronger). **Cards:** Ob Nixilis, Captive Kingpin (Caveats: the engine has no "until your next end step" duration, so the exile-and-play window runs through that end step).

- **Protection from mana value** (#2181): "protection from mana value N or less" (Reaver Titan) is a new `game.ProtectionQualityKind`, `ProtectionQualityManaValueAtMost`, with its own parser token. A source's mana value is not a characteristic a layer computes, so the source snapshot gained `Characteristic.SourceManaValue` (and `SourceManaValueKnown`, so an unreadable cost matches nothing). CR 202.3e: a spell counts the X it was cast with, through `TargetSource.X` at announce and re-check and `damageSourceLKILocked` for damage; a token that is no copy is 0 and a copy has the copiable cost's value. Every protection reader (targeting, attachment, damage, block) already asked `ProtectionQuality.Matches`, so none changed. Wire token `mana_value_at_most`; the bot reads the cost it already sees. Shipped on **Reaver Titan**.

- **Populate** (#2180, CR 701.36) — `Populate` (`effects/populate.go`) is the keyword action as one primitive: the controller's creature tokens are the candidates (not a target, so hexproof and shroud do not matter), none means nothing happens and no prompt is asked (CR 701.36b), one candidate or several with the same copiable values copy without asking, and otherwise one `choose_cards` prompt over the battlefield. The copy is made by `CreateTokenCopy`, so Parallel Lives and Doubling Season double it and "whenever a token enters" watchers see it. The rest of the card's sentence is the primitive's `Then`, handed the tokens the populate created, because the choice can be a prompt and a following statement would run before the answer; `Except` is the copy's "except it …" clause. A new `3/3 green Centaur` row joins the token table. **Cards:** Determined Iteration (haste and a delayed sacrifice on the new token), Rootborn Defenses, Wake the Reflections, Growing Ranks, Sundering Growth and Vitu-Ghazi Guildmage are Full, and Druid's Deliverance drops its caveat. No wire, view or bot change: the prompt is the existing `choose_cards`. **Still open:** none for this seam; the other populate cards need only a card file.

- **The legend rule not applying to your permanents** (#2177, CR 704.5j) — `Spec.LegendRuleExemptions` declares a printed "the legend rule doesn't apply" static, player-scoped (`LegendRuleDoesntApplyToYours()`) or global (`LegendRuleDoesntApply()`). `legendRuleChoicesLocked` reads the exemptions off the battlefield on every state-based action check, keyed by `CatalogAbilityKey` and never stored, so one leaving cannot revoke another's and the next check after the last one leaves applies the rule with the controller choosing. A copy effect's "except it has" clause carries it as `AbilityGrant.LegendRuleExempt`. **Cards:** Mirror Box (loses its caveat), Mirror Gallery (Full) and Sakashima of a Thousand Faces (Caveats: Partner is deck construction and does nothing).

- **Devour** (#2175, CR 702.82): "as this creature enters, you may sacrifice any number of creatures; it enters with N +1/+1 counters for each." It is the sacrifice `EntryCardChoice` of ADR 0098 in an "any number" form (`AnyNumber`: floor zero, ceiling the candidate count, no "if you do" branch) plus `ThenEntering`, a hook handed the paused entry event so the choice can rewrite what the creature lands as. Devour uses it to add the counters to the entry (`ev.AddCounterAtETB`), so Doubling Season and Hardened Scales apply, and to record the number devoured (`ReplacementEvent.EntersDevoured`, copied onto the new per-instance `Card.Devoured` at every landing and cleared when the permanent leaves) for CR 702.82b's "each creature it devoured". The sacrifice goes through `SacrificeAllThenForEffect` before the creature enters, so sacrifice and dies triggers fire and nothing can respond in between; a token copy asks like any other entry. `effects.Devour(name, n)` declares it. **6 cards:** Ravenous Tyrannosaurus (with its attack trigger's excess damage to the creature's controller), Predator Dragon, Thunder-Thrash Elder, Gorger Wurm, Skullmulcher and Marrow Chomper.

- **Can't be blocked by one player's creatures** (#2172, CR 509.1b) — a new scoped block-rule kind, `ModCantBeBlockedByPlayer`, stores the chosen player in `Mod.Player` and its refusal clause in `Mod.Text`. It is pinned to the target at resolution (CR 611.2c), swept at end of turn, and adapted by the block-rule walk into a pair rule, so the block validator and the enumerator both refuse a barred blocker; the blocker's controller is compared live. The kind is new vocabulary and reuses existing mod fields. **Cards:** The Black Gate (Full).

- **A player it already attacked this turn** (#2171, CR 508.1c) — `AttackTargetRestriction` gains a `NotAlreadyAttackedThisTurn` clause that reads `TurnTally.Attacks` for the attacker's current object epoch and refuses a player it has already been declared attacking this turn. A flickered creature is a new object (CR 400.7) and may attack that player again; planeswalkers and battles are never refused by the clause. Both declaration verbs, the CR 508.1d search and the enumerator's per-attacker target list share the predicate, so only the players left are offered. The snapshot gains one additive field. **Cards:** Bloodthirster (Full).

- **Mana that can't pay generic costs** (#2170, [ADR 0040](decisions/0040-mana-pipeline.md)) — `game.ManaRestrictNoGeneric` is a symbol-level spend tag, read where the generic part of a cost is paid rather than where the object being paid for is matched: the pool solver (`ManaPool.attemptSpend`), the missing-mana breakdown, the distinct-colours pick and the auto-tapper's pool credit (`poolShortfalls`), so the view's castability, the bot's affordability probe and the payment agree. {N}, {X}, cost increases and commander tax are refused it; coloured, hybrid and Phyrexian symbols, the widened symbols of "spend mana as though it were mana of any color" and symbols a cast permission folded into the generic demand take it. Kruphix's conversion keeps the tag. **Cards:** Jegantha, the Wellspring (caveat: companion). **Not built:** The Rebellious Intelligence (not legal in Commander; its upkeep ability needs a random card from outside the game).

- **A delayed trigger that keeps triggering this turn** (#2169, CR 603.7a/b, CR 605.1b): an event-conditioned `DelayedTrigger` fired on its first match and was removed, so "whenever a creature you control deals combat damage to that player this turn" (Great Train Heist) had no shape. **`DelayedTrigger.Repeats`** keeps it queued after it fires; the end-of-turn duration sweep removes it (`clearExpiredDelayedTriggersLocked`, CR 514.2), the same one that ends a Full Throttle. It is not tied to its source, a match a suppressor stops neither fires nor ends it, and `dispatchEventDelayedTriggerLocked` now asks the trigger doublers like `harvestMatchLocked` does, for every event-conditioned delayed trigger. The additive snapshot keys are `repeats`, `manaTapSubtype` and `manaAdds`. The CR 605.1b twin is `ManaTapSubtype` / `ManaAdds` on the same record: `fireManaTriggersLocked` reads it beside the permanents' mana triggers and adds the mana at once, with no stack item (Bubbling Muck, High Tide). `effects.DelayedOnEvent{Repeats: true}` and `effects.TurnManaTrigger` are the catalog sides. Shipped on Great Train Heist, Bubbling Muck, High Tide, Glimpse of Nature and Benefactor's Draught. Still open: Dalkovan Encampment keeps its own trigger on the land, and Vizkopa Guildmage's "whenever you gain life this turn" is not built.

- **Mana you don't lose as steps and phases end** (#2166, [ADR 0040](decisions/0040-mana-pipeline.md) amendment 2026-10-05) — the step-boundary sweep (`sweepManaPoolLocked`, `game/mana_keep.go`) keeps or converts each unspent mana from three sources: a static over a player's pool derived from the battlefield (`Spec.ManaPool`: keep all, keep some colours, or Kruphix's becomes-colourless, which keeps the mana's restrictions), a granted player statement with a duration (`PlayerStatic.KeepManaColors`), and a per-mana mark (`ManaRiderKeepUntilEndOfTurn`, `effects.KeepManaUntilEndOfTurn()`) that expires when the cleanup step begins. The mark rides the token's rider slot, so it is additive in the snapshot and carried by every mint route. **Cards:** Upwelling, Kruphix, God of Horizons, Karn, Legacy Reforged, The Last Agni Kai and Savage Ventmaw (Full); Leyline Tyrant (caveat: its dies trigger). **Not built:** Omnath, Locus of Mana, whose +1/+1 per unspent green mana needs a layer 7 read of the pool.

- **Two kicker costs on one card** (#2153, CR 702.33b / 702.33d / 702.33f, [ADR 0073 amendment 2026-10-05](decisions/0073-optional-additional-costs-and-the-cast-gate.md)) — "Kicker {R} and/or {W}" is `effects.Kickers("{R}", "{W}")`: two `Kicker` entries in printed order, both keyed `game.KickerKey`, which `Register` now allows for that key alone (never three kickers, and the two must differ in mana). Each is a once-only toggle announced by index, so payment, price, the view's two offers, the client's toggles and the record needed no change, and every "was it kicked" reader counts both: both paid is "kicked twice". CR 702.33f's "if it was kicked with its [A] kicker" is `game.KickedWithPaid` / `game.CardKickedWith` / `ctx.KickedWith` / the `effects.ThisKickedWith` trigger condition, keyed by the printed cost; a CR 707.10 copy keeps which kicker was paid. The bot enumerator offers the pair together as one extra announcement. No wire change. **Cards:** Urborg Lhurgoyf drops its relabelled one-payment multikicker for the real shape; Archangel of Wrath, Thornscape Battlemage and Sunscape Battlemage ship, all Full. **Not catalogued here:** the other two-kicker cards (Ana, Nightscape, Stormscape, Thunderscape and Wastescape Battlemage, the five Volvers, Stronghold Arena, Temporal Firestorm, Vodalian Mindsinger, Illuminate) are card work on this shape plus whatever else each prints. Wastescape's "when you cast this spell, if it was kicked with its {G} kicker" asks `game.KickedWithPaid` of the spell's record on the stack (`StackItemPaidForEffect`), not of a permanent's.

- **Devoid** (#2152, CR 702.114a, [ADR 0034 amendment 2026-10-05](decisions/0034-multi-face-cards.md)) — a devoid card is colourless in every zone instead of the colour of its pips. Scryfall's `colors: []` for a devoid card had always read as "not stamped", so the colour fell back to the mana cost. `devoid` joins `canonicalKeywords` (`game.KeywordDevoid`, `game/devoid.go`), so the deck importer stamps it on the ~130 devoid cards with no catalog entry, and the printed-colour rule reads it as the colour-defining ability it is: a stamped colour list first, then nothing for devoid, then the cost (`printedColorsOf`, and `Card.EffectiveColors` off the battlefield). Being layer 0, it reaches every colour read — target predicates, "colorless spell" payoffs, protection, mana restrictions, the wire's `colors` — and a layer-5 colour effect still applies on top. A copy keeps it (Keywords and the oracle ID are copiable values); a copy "except it's black" is black, because a stamped colour wins (CR 707.9d). Colour identity (CR 903.4) is unchanged. The importer leaves a devoid face's colours unstamped rather than deriving them from its pips (Drowner of Truth). No wire or snapshot field changed; a restore point written before this keeps an uncatalogued devoid card coloured for the rest of its game. **Cards:** Ugin's Binding's caveat is gone (now Full); Fanged Flames, Unnatural Aggression, Touch of the Void, Brutal Expulsion, Depth Defiler, Hideous Taskmaster, Propagator Drone, Serpentine Spike and Flaying Tendrils declare the keyword, and `TestEveryCatalogDevoidCardIsColourless` holds every catalogued devoid card to it.

- **A permanent you controlled left the battlefield this turn** (#2148, revolt, CR 702.136) — `PlayerTurnTally.PermanentsLeft` is bumped from every `EventLTB` under the controller the permanent had as it left (CR 603.10a), whatever the route or destination: a sacrifice, a bounce, an exile, a death, a token that ceased to exist, a permanent that left and came back. An opponent's permanent leaving does not count for you, and the cell resets with the rest of the turn tally. Read through `Game.PermanentLeftThisTurn`. Card side (`cards/effects/revolt.go`): `Revolt(g, you)` for a spell's resolution, `WhenThisEntersIfRevolt` and `AtYourEndStepIfRevolt` for the intervening if (checked as the trigger fires and again as it resolves, CR 603.4) and `SelfEntersWithCountersIfRevolt` for "enters with counters if". Cards: Shortcut to Mushrooms, Fatal Push, Renegade Rallier, Hidden Stockpile, Narnam Renegade, Silkweaver Elite, Airdrop Aeronauts and Countless Gears Renegade. The table-wide nonland sibling, void (#2128), still wants its own cell.

- **Protection from Ring-bearers** (#2145): "protection from Ring-bearers" (Lord of the Nazgûl) is `ProtectionQualityRingBearer`. A Ring-bearer is a designation (CR 701.54b), so the source snapshot carries it as `Characteristic.SourceRingBearer`, set when the source is its controller's Ring-bearer (CR 701.54e) and, for a source that has left the battlefield, from `PermanentInfo.RingBearer`. Wire token `ring_bearer`. Shipped on **Lord of the Nazgûl**.

- **Alternative costs for every spell you cast** (#2163, CR 118.9, [ADR 0118](decisions/0118-strict-payment-by-default-and-alternative-costs-for-every-spell.md) §3) — `Spec.GrantedAlternativeCosts` declares a static that offers its controller one more alternative cost for each spell they cast. The offers are derived from the battlefield on every cast query (`grantedAlternativeCostsLocked`), so nothing is stored and an offer lasts exactly as long as its source is under its controller; a permanent that has lost its abilities grants nothing. They sit last in `CastOffersForLocked`, claimable only where the printed mana cost could be paid (CR 118.9a), and reach the cost picker through `card.alternative_costs`. Helpers: `PayWUBRGForSpellsYouCast` (`granted-wubrg`) and `CastFromHandWithoutPayingManaCost` (`granted-free`, hand only). **Cards:** Fist of Suns, Jodah, Archmage Eternal and Omniscience in full, and Leyline of Mutation with the other Leylines' caveat that it can't begin the game on the battlefield (CR 103.6a). Hunting Velociraptor's granted prowl stays on `ability-cost-modification`.

- **Commanders die** (#2085, CR 903.9a, [ADR 0115](decisions/0115-commanders-die.md)) — a commander that is destroyed, sacrificed, milled, discarded, countered or exiled now goes to its graveyard or to exile like any card, every trigger of that move fires, and only then does a state-based action (`commanderReturnSBALocked`) ask its owner, through the data-only `commander_return` prompt, whether it goes to the command zone. `commanderZoneReplacement` shrank to CR 903.9b, a move to a hand or a library, which is still a replacement and still pauses. Commander tax and commander damage follow the card across a new object, so a blinked commander keeps both. **Cards:** the caveats came off Sauron, Lord of the Rings (now Full: it sees an opponent's commander die whatever the owner answers), Rest in Peace (now Full: a dying commander is exiled and then offered) and Leyline of the Void (only its opening-hand caveat remains). The comments on Agent of the Iron Throne, Phyrexian Rebirth, The Battle of Bywater, God-Eternal Oketra, The Locust God, Underworld Cerberus, Liesa, Stone of Erech and Cosmic Intervention now describe the new order. **Still open:** showing the commander card in the prompt and an "X is deciding" line for the other seats (ADR 0115 PR 5, polish only).

- **The Ring tempts you** (#2076, CR 701.54, [ADR 0114](decisions/0114-the-ring-tempts-you.md)) — one keyword action, `game.RingTemptsForEffect`: the Ring emblem at the first temptation (`emblem:the-ring`), its count up by one, a Ring-bearer chosen (automatically with one creature, through the `ring_bearer` prompt with two or more), then `EventRingTempted`, even with no creature to choose. `Card.RingBearer` has one write site and is cleared by CR 400.7 and by a change of control. The emblem's four abilities are each switched on by `game.RingTempted(n)`: legendary and unblockable by greater power, the loot on attack, the end-of-combat sacrifice of a blocker, and "each opponent loses 3 life" on combat damage to a player. The table shows a Ring chip with its level and a marker on the Ring-bearer. Card side: `effects.TheRingTemptsYou{Then}`, `WheneverTheRingTemptsYou`, `WheneverYouChooseARingBearer`, `IfYouChoseAnotherRingBearer`, `ringTemptsYouNext` (the tempt after a destruction, bounce, damage or scry that can pause) and `AtYourEndStepIfACreatureDied`. **Cards:** 46 of the 56 that mention the Ring: five with Sauron's deck (PR 3) and 41 in PR 5's three pools. Three carry caveats: Sauron, Lord of the Rings (#2085), Boromir, Warden of the Tower (strict mana) and Sauron, the Necromancer (its Wraith always joins Sauron's attack). **Still open, each on another seam's row:** Breaking of the Fellowship (#1943), Frodo, Adventurous Hobbit (partner with, #2142), Galadriel, Elven-Queen (voting, #2143), Frodo, Sauron's Bane (setting creature types, #2144), Lord of the Nazgûl (protection from Ring-bearers, #2145), Rangers of Ithilien (a target relative to its source, #2146), Sauron's Ransom (face-down piles, #2147), Shortcut to Mushrooms (a permanent you controlled left the battlefield, #2148), Witch-king of Angmar (creatures that dealt combat damage to you, #2149) and Aragorn, Company Leader (counters put on together by kind, #2150).

- **Choosing a card from a revealed hand with a restriction** (#2078, [ADR 0116](decisions/0116-a-card-filter-on-the-revealed-hand-pick.md)) — `Game.QueueDiscardFromRevealedHand` now takes a `RevealedHandDiscard` with a card filter and its label. It reveals the whole hand to every player (`RevealForEffect`, CR 701.20a), runs the filter once over the hand and stores the matching cards on the prompt as `PendingChoice.DiscardOptions`, an additive v7 snapshot field with a new corpus board. It caps the count at the matches and queues nothing when there are none: the hand is revealed and nothing is discarded (CR 609.3). `ResolvePendingChoice` refuses a pick outside the list or named twice, `legal.EnumerateFor` offers only the list, and the wire sends it as `eligible` / `eligible_label`, so the prompt shows the whole revealed hand with the other cards greyed out. Card side: `effects.ChooseFromRevealedHand` and `TargetRevealsYouChooseDiscard` over an ordinary `CardPredicate`. **Cards:** Thoughtseize (Caveats to Full), Unmask and Pelakka Predation (Full). **Moved to other rows:** the exile, optional-pick and reads-the-chosen-card variants (#2115, `revealed-hand-pick-variants`).

- **Undying and persist** (#2075, CR 702.93 / 702.79, [ADR 0113 §4](decisions/0113-small-seams-for-the-s58-deck-requests.md#4-undying-and-persist-2075)) — the first dies keywords derived from the ability list. `undying` and `persist` join `canonicalKeywords`, both cumulative (CR 113.2c). The leaves-the-battlefield harvest used to read only catalog triggers and returned early for a permanent with no catalog key; `harvestLTB` now asks `ltbKeywordTriggersFor` for one trigger per token on the departed permanent's LAST-KNOWN ability list first (CR 603.10a), so a printed instance (stamped by the deck importer), a token's and a layer-6 grant all work with no catalog entry, and a creature that died having lost all abilities has none. The intervening "if" (CR 603.4) reads the counters it last had, as it dies (`lastKnownCounters`) and again on resolution (its `PermanentInfo`). The keyed bodies `undying/return` and `persist/return` return the card only while it is still the graveyard object the ability triggered with (CR 400.7e; the item's `SourceObject` is that object), under its owner's control (the trigger is the last controller's, CR 603.3a), with the counter on the entry event so a CR 614 counter replacement applies (CR 122.6). A token, or a card that left the graveyard, returns nothing and raises no error. **Also fixed:** the state-based actions ran the CR 704.5q +1/+1 / -1/-1 cancel before the destruction sweep, so a creature that died in the same check lost a counter it should have died with; the cancel now runs after the sweep (CR 704.3), which never changes power or toughness. Two new on-disk body keys and a v7 corpus board (`persist_trigger_pending.json`); no shape change. The bot values both tokens in its keyword table. **Cards:** Gleeful Arsonist and Persistent Constrictor, Full; Murderous Redcap drops its caveat and goes Full. The vanilla undying and persist creatures need no card file. **Still open:** the rest of the pool lands in follow-up card PRs.

- **Maximum hand size** (#2074, [ADR 0113](decisions/0113-small-seams-for-the-s58-deck-requests.md) §3) — a permanent's static can now set a player's maximum hand size, change it, or reach players other than its controller: `Spec.HandSize` declares `game.HandSizeStatic{Players, Kind, N, When}`, and `Spec.NoMaxHandSize` folds into the same list. `Game.EffectiveMaxHandSizeLocked` applies every entry that reaches a player in CR 613.11 timestamp order, starting from seven: each battlefield permanent's statics at its own timestamp, read through `CatalogAbilityKey`, and the player's "for the rest of the game" grant at `Player.MaxHandSizeAt`, the time it resolved (CR 613.7b, an additive snapshot field). "No maximum" stays unbounded under a later change and gives way to a later set. The result is never below zero (CR 107.1b). The cleanup discard still asks only the active player, against their own maximum (CR 514.1). The seat panel shows a badge when a player's maximum is not seven. **Cards:** Jin-Gitaxias, Core Augur, Null Profusion and Price of Knowledge (Full).

- **Annihilator** (#2073, CR 702.86, [ADR 0113](decisions/0113-small-seams-for-the-s58-deck-requests.md) §2) — the third keyword trigger the engine derives from the ability list, after prowess and evolve, and the second numbered keyword, after toxic. `annihilator` joins `canonicalKeywords` as a family key: the tokens are `annihilator N` (minted by `CanonicalAnnihilatorToken`), a bare `annihilator` is refused, and the token is cumulative (CR 702.86b), so a printed annihilator 4 and a granted annihilator 2 are two triggers. The deck importer reads the number off the oracle line, as it does for toxic, and no longer counts a keyword sitting between two commas inside a sentence (Flayer of Loyalties' cast trigger), which would have stamped a second, unprinted instance. Each trigger watches its creature being declared as an attacker (CR 508.3a, so a creature put onto the battlefield attacking does not trigger), records the defending player (CR 508.5, per attacker), and resolves through the new keyed body `annihilator/sacrifice`: the defending player, read again if the creature is still attacking and otherwise the recorded one, chooses N permanents they control in one prompt and sacrifices them together (fewer if they have fewer; nothing if they have left the game). Ulamog, the Defiler's "annihilator X" is the catalog row `AnnihilatorCounted`, which reads X as it resolves. The Eldrazi titans' "When ~ is put into a graveyard from anywhere" is the new `WhenThisIsPutIntoAGraveyardFromAnywhere`, one trigger per arrival whether the card died, was sacrificed, discarded, milled or countered. Snapshot: one new on-disk body key and a v7 corpus board with the trigger on the stack; while the defender is choosing, the table holds the same pick prompt Lotus Field's does. **Cards:** Kozilek, Butcher of Truth; Ulamog, the Infinite Gyre; Ulamog, the Defiler — all Full.

- **A spell reads what its sacrifice cost took** (#2072, CR 400.7j / 608.2h / 707.10, [ADR 0113](decisions/0113-small-seams-for-the-s58-deck-requests.md) §1) — `PaidCost.SacrificedObjects` names every permanent a sacrifice component took, as the battlefield object it was (instance ID and epoch), in the order named; it is written beside `PaidCost.Sacrificed` at all three payment sites (a spell's additional cost, an activated ability, a mana ability), read before the payment moves the permanents. `Context.SacrificedPermanents()` reads each one through `Game.PermanentForEffect`, so an effect sees the permanent as it last existed on the battlefield, anthem bonuses and counters included; `SacrificedPower()` floors the first one's power at zero and `SacrificedTotalPower()` sums them and floors the total (CR 107.1b). A CR 707.10 spell copy now carries both the list and the count, so a copied Tend the Pests makes the original's Pests and a copied Vicious Betrayal, Devouring Greed or Devouring Rage no longer reads zero; an ability copy carries the list beside the count it already carried. `b17PermanentSacrificedToPay` (Jarad, Greater Good, Birthing Pod and the rest) reads the record, keeping its event-log scan only for a restore point written before the field. Additive within schema v7 (`stackMeta[].paid.sacrificedObjects`, corpus board `sacrifice_cost_objects`). **Cards:** Fling, Kazuul's Fury, Momentous Fall, Tend the Pests and Corpse Cobble (Full). **Pool (owner decision 2), all Full:** Thud, Pyrrhic Blast, Life's Legacy, Carrion, Call for Blood, Ichor Explosion, Final Strike, Rite of Consumption, Tormented Thoughts, Severed Strands, Worthy Cause, Morbid Curiosity, Reckoner's Bargain, Nasty End, Forge Armor (#2096); Foundry Helix, Hellish Sideswipe, Splitting the Powerstone, Fatal Grudge, Eldritch Evolution, Anchor to Reality, Burnt Offering, Sacrifice, Metamorphosis, Mind Extraction, Endemic Plague and Desperate Plea. **Moved to other rows:** Extus, Oriq Overlord, whose magecraft also fires on copied spells (#2090); Soulblast, whose cost sacrifices all your creatures (#2097); Neoform, whose search puts a card onto the battlefield with a counter (#2098); Rescue from the Underworld, which returns the sacrificed card later (#2099).

- **Prevention that does something with what it prevented** (#1906, [ADR 0108](decisions/0108-turn-scoped-effects-object-history-and-damage-shields.md) §8, owner decision 2, Delivery PR 8) — a prevention static declares its CR 615.5 additional effect as `ReplacementEffect.Then` (a registered body) and the unit it runs once per as `ThenPer`: per recipient for "if damage would be dealt to X" (the Phantoms: blocked by three, one counter comes off), per source for "if a source would deal damage to X" (Nine Lives: three attackers, three counters). Every apply-loop path fires `Replace` through one helper that measures the damage event before and after and owes the difference to the shared follow-up queue, keyed on the static's object (CR 400.7), its slot and the unit, once per damage instance; under damage that can't be prevented the additional effect is still owed, with nothing prevented (CR 615.12). A follow-up is handed both "the damage prevented this way" and "that damage", because the rulings read them differently under CR 615.12 (Polukranos still removes that many counters; Phyrexian Hydra puts on none). Register refuses `Then` without `Prevention` or `ThenPer`, and a census test fails a prevention static whose `Replace` does more than change the event. The scoped `preventDamage` and `preventCombatDamage` shields read `Then` too, `Mod.To` names the object a follow-up deals its damage to, and every prevention of one shield joins one end-step trigger. **Cards** (32): Phantom Centaur, Phantom Flock, Phantom Nantuko, Phantom Nishoba, Phantom Nomad, Phantom Tiger, Phantom Wurm, Oathsworn Knight, Unbreathing Horde, Undergrowth Champion, Polukranos, Unchained, Ugin's Conjurant, Magma Pummeler, Anti-Venom, Horrifying Healer, Panther Habit, Stormwild Capridor, Phyrexian Hydra, Ironscale Hydra, Nine Lives, Gloom Surgeon, Angel of Suffering, Swans of Bryn Argoll, Phyrexian Vindicator, The Mindskinner, Test of Faith, Temper, Sacred Boon, Scars of the Veteran, Acolyte's Reward, Vengeful Archon and Inkshield (Full), and Immortal Coil (Caveats: which graveyard cards to exile is asked after the damage's spell or ability finishes, #2040). **Moved to other rows:** Hostility, Purity and Vigor (#2038), Jared Carthalion (#2039), Khârn the Betrayer (#2040), Weeping Angel (#2041), Bloatfly Swarm (#2042), Protean Hydra (#1841) and Candles' Glow (splice, #1989).

- **Damage dealt to something else instead** (#1905, [ADR 0108](decisions/0108-turn-scoped-effects-object-history-and-damage-shields.md) §9, owner decision 1, Delivery PR 9) — one primitive, `redirectDamageEventLocked` (`RedirectDamageEventForEffect` for a catalog static), rewrites a damage event's recipient and its damage tail's kind together, so redirected damage lands through the path its new recipient takes, and does nothing when CR 614.9 says so. The damage keeps its source, combat status, deathtouch, lifelink, infect, wither and commander tally (CR 120.4b), meets the new recipient's shields and protection (CR 616.2), and is never redirected by the same effect again (CR 614.5). A redirection is a replacement, not a prevention effect: "damage can't be prevented" leaves it alone, and "can't be dealt instead" (Lava Burst, Whippoorwill) stops it unspent. The scoped kind, `ModRedirectDamage`, reads the one source vocabulary, what it protects, "the next time" (spent per damage instance), a charge (CR 615.7, divided by the protected player when it meets several events at once — the Harm's Way ruling — and split off an event it only partly covers), or all turn, and a destination: `Mod.To` (one object, epoch-pinned) or `Mod.ToSourceController`. With no destination and a follow-up it is Eye for an Eye. Two static redirections are two effects the affected player orders (the Pariah ruling). A banner line names each live redirection. **Cards** (51): Aegis of Honor, Beacon of Destiny, Captain's Maneuver, Carom, Daughter of Autumn, Empyrial Archangel, Eye for an Eye, General's Regalia, Gideon's Sacrifice, Glarecaster, Goblin Psychopath, Harm's Way, Harsh Judgment, Hazduhr the Abbot, Jade Monolith, Karona's Zealot, Kor Chant, Kor Dirge, Lancers en-Kor, Martyrs of Korlis, Mirror Strike, Mirrorwood Treefolk, Nomads en-Kor, Opal-Eye, Konda's Yojimbo (with bushido), Oracle's Attendants, Outrider en-Kor and Zhalfirin Crusader (with flanking), Palisade Giant, Pariah, Pariah's Shield, Protector of the Crown, Razia, Boros Archangel, Reflect Damage, Refraction Trap (its follow-up's target carried on the shield, and its trap cost read from the turn's casts), Reverberation, Saving Grace, Shaman en-Kor, Shield Dancer, Shimian Night Stalker, Soltari Guerrillas, Spirit en-Kor, Treacherous Link, Turn the Tables, Vassal's Duty, Veteran Bodyguard, Ward of Piety, Warrior en-Kor, Weathered Bodyguards, With Great Power . . . and Zealous Inquisitor (Full); Shining Shoal (Caveats: its alternative cost isn't offered). **Moved to other rows:** Sivvi's Valor (#2030, its alternative cost taps a creature), Nova Pentacle (#2055, a target of an opponent's choice), Blood of the Martyr, Kjeldoran Royal Guard and Ascent of the Worthy (#2056), Heroic Sacrifice (#2057), Ancient Adamantoise (#2058), Personal Incarnation and Martyrdom (#1947).

- **Damage dealt as though its source had wither or infect** (#1889, [ADR 0108](decisions/0108-turn-scoped-effects-object-history-and-damage-shields.md) §10, PR 10) — "All damage is dealt as though its source had wither" and "all damage is dealt to you as though its source had infect" are a battlefield static, `Spec.DamageAsThough` (`game.DamageAsThoughStatic{Wither, Infect, ToYou, WhileAtOrBelowZeroLife}`, `game/damage_as_though.go`), read live through `game.CatalogDamageAsThough` keyed by `CatalogAbilityKey`, so a permanent that has lost its abilities stops (CR 613.1f). It is a rules effect (CR 613.11) read where the rule it changes is used: one gate, `damageResultAsThoughLocked`, ORs it into the result the source's own infect, wither and toxic gave the event's tail (ADR 0056 Decision 2) as the damage lands, in the two landing functions and nowhere else. So the damage has been settled first (CR 120.4b before 120.4c): a prevention shield prevents it and no counter is put for a prevented point, "damage can't be prevented" (ADR 0107 §5) makes every point a counter and leaves the shield's charge alone, a multiplier doubles the counters with the damage, and §7's charged and half shields take their part off first. The counters go through the CR 614 counter window, put or given by the source's controller (CR 120.3b, 120.3d). The source gains no ability (CR 609.4): a "whenever a creature with infect deals damage" trigger does not see it. Phyrexian Unlife's "as long as you have 0 or less life" is read once per damage instance, against the life total its controller had as the instance began (owner decision 4, its ruling): `nextDamageInstanceLocked` records every seat's life total, transient like the stamp, cloned with the game and never captured, so two attackers that take you from 3 to -1 in one combat damage step are all life loss, and the next instance is poison. `Register` refuses a static that names neither keyword, and wither narrowed to damage dealt to a player. **Cards** (both Full): Everlasting Torment, Phyrexian Unlife (its "you don't lose the game for having 0 or less life" is ADR 0057's `LossLife`-narrowed gate, as on Transcendence).

- **Abilities only some mana can pay, and mana for one-colour or gold spells** (#1600, CR 105.2a / CR 105.2b / CR 609.4b, [ADR 0040](decisions/0040-mana-pipeline.md)'s 2026-10-03 amendment) — the ability half and the token half of the "Costs that only some mana can pay" row. COST side: `game.AbilityCost.SpendOnly` (`game.ManaSpendOnly{Colors, ChosenColor, XOnly}`), built with `effects.SpendOnlyManaOfTheChosenColor()` ("Spend only mana of the chosen color to activate this ability") or `effects.SpendOnlyOnX(colors...)` ("Spend only black mana on X"), composed with `Plus` and checked at boot (`checkSpendOnlyClause`). The one ability pricer (`AbilityManaCostForTargetsForEffect`) resolves it against the source — the chosen colour read off `Card.ChosenColor` (#742) — and stamps it on the `ParsedCost`, whose `String()` ignores it, so the shown price stays printed. `costAsPaidByLocked` (now taking the announced X) FOLDS it into coloured requirements before the any-colour widening (`game/spend_only.go`): the activation and its auto-tap, `internal/legal`'s one affordability probe and the auto-tap preview all read it there, so the view's `legal_actions`, the bot's moves and the payment agree, and no solver changed. Under Chromatic Orrery any mana pays it — CR 609.4b, the restriction is about how the cost is paid, and the Celestial Dawn rulings say an as-though colour pays "an ability that can only be activated by spending another color of mana"; a cost with no chosen colour stays unpayable. TOKEN side: the restriction tags `monocolored` and `multicolored` (`game.ManaRestrictMonocolored` / `ManaRestrictMulticolored`, CR 105.2a–b; a hybrid spell is multicolored, CR 202.2d), which a mana restriction keeps under the Orrery. `effects.MonocoloredSpellsOfTheChosenColor()` and `effects.ProducedChosenColorAmount(n)` are Throne's mana. **Cards:** Throne of Eldraine, Crypt Rats, Crimson Hellkite, Pillar of the Paruns and Obsidian Obelisk (Full). **Not covered** (the row stays open as partial): the same clause on a SPELL (Drain Life, Consume Spirit, Soul Burn), "spend only mana produced by …" source restrictions (Imperiosaur, Myr Superion, Security Rhox), and Emblazoned Golem's one-of-each-colour cap. Atalya, Samite Master is writable now and not catalogued.

- **Exiling a creature you control as a cost** (#1600, CR 118.3 / CR 602.2b / CR 605.3a, [ADR 0020](decisions/0020-activated-abilities.md)'s 2026-10-03 amendment) — the ability half of the "Exiling a permanent you control as a cost" row. `game.ExilePermanentsCost` (`Count`, `CardType`, `ExcludeSource`, `Label`) is the battlefield sibling of `ExileCost` and `ReturnToHandCost`, on both owners: `AbilityCost.ExilePermanents` and `ManaAbilityShape.ExilePermanents`, built with `effects.ExileACreatureYouControl()` or `effects.ExileAPermanentYouControl(label, cardType)`. The clause is DATA (a permanent card type read off the permanent's current types), not a `TargetSpec`, so the ADR 0041 closure ratchet gains no route. One candidate walk, `ExilePermanentsOptionsForEffect`, feeds the view's `exile_permanent_label` / `exile_permanent_options` on both ability views, the legal enumerator (one move per creature) and `validateExilePermanentsCostLocked` (exactly the count, distinct, yours, of the type, never also sacrificed or returned by the same payment); the picks ride `exile_permanent_ids` on `activate_ability` and `activate_mana_ability`, and the client reuses `SacrificeCostModal` with the verb "Exile". The payment is the one exit primitive with `MustSettleNow`: leaves-the-battlefield triggers fire, dies and sacrifice triggers do not, a commander is asked CR 903.9 before anything is paid, and on a mana ability the triggers reach the stack after the mana is in the pool. The exiled permanents land on `PaidCost.Exiled`, and `PermanentInfo.ManaValue` (additive in snapshot schema v7) answers "the exiled creature's mana value" as it last existed. The auto-tapper never plans a mana ability with the component, and the heuristic prices an exiled permanent as it prices a sacrificed one. Proof cards, all `full`: The Soul Stone, Food Chain, City of Shadows and Altar of Bhaal // Bone Offering. Still open on the row: the casting-cost form (Lunar Hatchling's escape), a variable count (Fabrication Foundry), a clause a card type cannot say (Curie, Emergent Intelligence) and craft's two-zone clause.

- **Riot and unleash, and the entry look-ahead** (#1556, [ADR 0109](decisions/0109-rule-gates-land-types-mana-and-cost-components.md) §10, PR 9) — riot (CR 702.136a) and unleash (CR 702.98a) join `canonicalKeywords`, both cumulative (CR 702.136b; CR 113.2c for unleash). Both are entry replacements, and which ones apply is judged by the permanent "as it would exist on the battlefield" (CR 614.12): `entryLookAheadLocked` (`game/entry_lookahead.go`) runs a dry layer pass over a copy of the battlefield plus the entering card, under its would-be controller, as the copy it was told to enter as, and with the records pinned to it as a spell (CR 400.7a); nothing is written. The gather adds one replacement per instance it reports, so a printed riot under Rhythm of the Wild asks twice and a creature entering under an ability-removing effect is not asked. Riot is the new mandatory prompt `entry_riot` (`{apply}`: true the counter, false haste); haste becomes an indefinite `addKeywords` record pinned to the permanent (`RiotHasteLabel`), shown as the haste chip labelled "Riot" (`CardView.riot_haste`), and an entry that cannot pause takes the counter. Unleash is the existing optional entry counter (with `entry_keyword: "unleash"` on the prompt), and its "can't block as long as it has a +1/+1 counter on it" is folded into the restrictions after the layer pass (`foldUnleashLocked`). The bot takes riot's haste when the creature can still attack this turn and the counter otherwise, and always takes the unleash counter unless the creature would be its only untapped blocker on an opponent's turn. No snapshot change. **Cards:** Rhythm of the Wild, Gruul Beastmaster, Skarrgan Hellkite, Ravager Wurm, Clamor Shaman, Frenzied Arynx, Burning-Tree Vandal, Tesak, Judith's Hellhound, Grim Roustabout, Hellhole Flailer, Exava, Rakdos Blood Witch and Chaos Imps, all Full; Uncivil Unrest moves onto the keyword grant and drops its caveat. Rampaging Rendhorn, Zhur-Taa Goblin, Ghor-Clan Wrecker, Wrecking Beast and nine unleash creatures need no card file. **Still open:** Arcbound Slasher waits on modular (#2012), Gruul Spellbreaker on "you have hexproof" during your turn (#2013), Spider-Punk on abilities that can't be countered (#2014), and Domri, Chaos Bringer on a spend rider that grants a keyword to the spell (ADR 0109 PR 10).

- **The block declaration comes before priority, and a finished declaration is finished** (#1501, CR 509.1 / 509.1h / 117.3a, [ADR 0045 amendment 2026-10-03 (#1501)](decisions/0045-combat-restrictions.md) Decisions 68-70) — the two sandbox allowances #1279 kept on purpose. A block from a defender whose declaration is complete is refused with the new `illegal_block` reason **`blocks_declared`**, checked last so a block that is illegal anyway still names its own reason, so a ninja that entered attacking after the declaration cannot be blocked: an engine fact, not a table convention. And the declare-blockers step no longer hands the active player priority on entry: while any defender is still declaring, priority is **parked** (`priority_holder = -1`, `beginBlockDeclarationLocked`), the priority-gated verbs are refused for every seated player, and the last `finish_blocks` hands the active player the post-block window. A defender with nothing to block with is never waited on; one who concedes, the step being advanced, or a sandbox verb that empties combat (the dispatcher's `SettleBlockDeclaration`) all unpark it. `finish_blocks` is a new legal-move kind, AlwaysLegal, so a bot defender whose policy wants no block still finishes rather than holding the table; the heuristic prices it as the pass it stands in for. The client's dock line names the defenders the table is waiting on. Completion by `pass_priority` and the bot's block grace remain for the one shape in which a declaring defender holds priority (a restore point from before this change, or a player who became a defending player mid-step).

- **Shields against a chosen source that are not one-use** (#1904, [ADR 0108](decisions/0108-turn-scoped-effects-object-history-and-damage-shields.md) §7, owner decision 1, Delivery PR 6) — `ModPreventFromSource` is a scoped prevention kind of plain data that reads the next-damage shield's source fields (a chosen or targeted source pinned as an object, CR 400.7 / 609.7a, and the CR 615.9 recheck), protects a player, a permanent or anything, can be combat-only, carries a CR 615.5 follow-up run once per damage instance (CR 615.13), and has an `Amount`: zero is all damage this turn and the shield is never spent, N is CR 615.7's charge. A charged shield with no source stays `preventDamage`. `Mod.Half` is Dark Sphere's "prevent half that damage, rounded down" (CR 107.1a); a shield that would prevent nothing is not used up (CR 609.7b). **`divide_shield`**: when a charged shield — `preventDamage` included — meets two or more events of one damage instance for more than its charge, the protected player divides the charge before any of them is dealt (CR 615.7, the Refraction Trap and Harm's Way rulings), on resolve_choice's `distribution` payload; nothing is asked when the charge covers them, and an event whose damage can't be prevented is not offered. The instance's events are collected first: an Each walk is asked about before its first leg, a damage scope stages the events a charged shield could meet and deals them as it ends, and a combat damage step stages its damage while a charged shield is live and deals it all at once. The division is plain data, spent as the events land; the prompt blocks the table, a departed chooser's prompt settles in the engine's order, the heuristic shields the player first and then the creature closest to dying, and the client answers it in the action dock. A banner line names the live source shields. **Cards** (12): Pay No Heed, Auriok Replica, Burrenton Forge-Tender, Prahv, Spires of Order, Rith's Charm, Dark Sphere, Healing Grace, Samite Ministration, Shieldmage Advocate, Mourner's Shield and Hidden Retreat (a targeted spell as the source, with ADR 0109 PR 8's library cost) (Full), and Protective Sphere (Caveats: with strict mana off it never sees the mana spent). **Moved to other rows:** Consulate Surveillance (#1995, a new row: paying energy as a cost) and Refraction Trap (#1905: its follow-up deals damage to the spell's target). **The recipient and to-and-by families** (Delivery PR 7): "prevent all [combat] damage that would be dealt to and dealt by that creature" is one prevention effect, so it is one record — `Mod.AndDealtBy` makes the record's pinned objects both what it protects and the sources it stops, so an event between two of them meets one effect (CR 614.5) and an instance is one application (CR 615.13) — and one record can protect several permanents (`DamageShield.ProtectPermanents`: "up to two target creatures", "those creatures"). **Cards** (39): Indestructible Aura, Shielded Passage, Djeru's Resolve, Leap of Faith, Fleeting Flight, Harvestguard Alseids, Favored Hoplite (heroic, read off the spell's targets as it is cast), Brace for Impact, Redeem, Kitsune Healer, Oriss, Samite Guardian, Godtoucher, Wellgabber Apothecary, Riot Control, Morningtide's Light, Ultimate Magic: Holy, Endure, Safe Passage, Take the Bait, Trained Pronghorn, Avacyn, Guardian Angel, Oko, the Trickster, Maze of Ith, Maze of Shadows, Ebony Horse, Elvish Scout, Foxfire, Ith, High Arcanist, Cephalid Illusionist, Deftblade Elite, Moonlight Geist, Urborg Phantom, Soratami Cloud Chariot, Energy Arc, Delirium, Dovin, Hand of Control and Kiora, the Crashing Wave (Full), and Kurbis, Harvest Celebrant and Mutational Advantage (Caveats: mana spent with strict mana off; the game chooses what to proliferate). **Moved to other rows:** Gideon Jura and the other Gideons that become creatures (#2046), Selfless Squire (#2047), Oketra's Avenger (#2048), Enshrouding Mist (#2049), You Look Upon the Tarrasque (#2050), Goblin Snowman (#1863), Glyph of Destruction (#2027) and the recipient sets the record can't name (#2045).

  **The object-source and property families** (Delivery PR 7b) — a targeted source pinned as the spell or ability resolves, with no prompt, or a property shield with no source at all. A sentence about several sources (Soul Parry, Feint, Serene Sunset, kicked Falling Timber) makes one record per source: a damage event has one source, so no event meets two of them. **Cards** (37): Fend Off, Kor Haven, Warning, Restrain, Horn of Deafening, Safeguard, Lady Evangela, Resistance Fighter, Songstitcher, Subdue, Kry Shield, Soul Parry, Inquisitor's Snare, Hallow, Dromoka's Command, Azorius Ploy, Shieldmage Elder, Stonewise Fortifier, Ignoble Soldier, Zealot il-Vec, Feint, Falling Timber, Serene Sunset, Chain of Silence, Fighting Chance, Loafing Giant, Mtenda Lion, Heroism, Guard Dogs, Radiant Kavu, Luminesce, Ethereal Haze, Chant of Vitu-Ghazi, Scarecrow and Eerie Interference (Full), and Boros Fury-Shield (Caveats: with strict mana off it never sees the {R} spent) and Ajani's Aid (Caveats: its search looks in the library only). **Moved to new rows:** 28 shields whose sources a property list can't describe — "non-", power, attacking or blocked, colorless, and "you don't control" (#2026, Benalish Missionary's blocked target among them); Sewers of Estark, Suppressor Skyguard, Winter's Chill, Glyph of Destruction and Old Fat Spider Can't See Me (#2027, "this combat" and "while the Saga remains"); Gossamer Chains (#2028, returning itself as a cost); Telekinesis (#2029, the next two untap steps); and Prismatic Strands (#2030, a flashback cost that taps a creature).

- **Activation costs from your library, onto it, or discarded at random** (#1902, [ADR 0109](decisions/0109-rule-gates-land-types-mana-and-cost-components.md) §7 and owner decision 3, PR 8) — three cost components, validated with the others and paid in `activateCatalogAbilityLocked` (`game/library_cost.go`). **"Put a card from your hand on top of your library"** (`AbilityCost.PutFromHandOnLibraryTop`) names its card at announce in a new `top_ids` field: a card in the activator's hand, never the source and never one the same payment discards or exiles (CR 118.3). It goes to the top of the library through the exit primitive, the activator keeps knowing it, and it is not a discard. **"Exile the top N cards of your library"** (`AbilityCost.ExileFromLibraryTop`) chooses nothing: a library of fewer than N cards refuses the activation, and the cards are exiled last (CR 601.2h) and recorded on `PaidCost.Exiled`, top first, for "the exiled card" (CR 400.7j). **"Discard N cards at random"** (`DiscardCost.Random`, CR 701.9b) is drawn by the engine from the hand the other components leave, once per announcement, so a commander drawn is offered CR 903.9a before anything is paid and the re-made announcement discards the same card; it is paid last through the one discard door and joins `PaidCost.Discarded` for ADR 0109 §8's readers. Each move is asked CR 903.9 like every other cost move (#1397). The view stamps `top_cost_n`, `top_cost_label`, `top_cost_options` (private to the activator), `library_exile_cost_n` and `discard_cost_random`; the enumerator offers one payment and gates the other two on the library and the hand; the client asks for the card to put on top with the discard picker and confirms the library exile and the random discard. **Cards** (24, all Full): Penance, Leashling, Seasoned Tactician, Arc-Slogger, Whirling Catapult, Royal Herbalist, Storm Elemental and Phyrexian Devourer, and the random discarders Pyromancy, Stormscale Anarch, Mage il-Vec, Frenetic Ogre, Canyon Drake, Coral Helm, Meteor Storm, Pardic Swordsmith, Stormbind, Amok, Pyromania, Hell-Bent Raider, Dwarven Strike Force, Draconian Cylix, Ogre Shaman and Pardic Lancer. **Moved to other rows:** Hidden Retreat (#1904, its spell-keyed shield), Thought Lash (`cumulative-upkeep`, its upkeep paid from the library) and Barbarian Bully (#1903, an offer to every player).

- **Emblems that restrict players** (#1899, [ADR 0109](decisions/0109-rule-gates-land-types-mana-and-cost-components.md) §5, PR 6) — an emblem's abilities function in the command zone (CR 114.4), but the rule gates walked only the battlefield, so an emblem's "can't cast" was dead text. `effects.EmblemSpec` now carries `CastRestrictions`, `LandPlayRestrictions`, `GameEndGates` and `UntapCaps`, the `Spec` slots of the same names under the same Register guards, and each gate (`CastGateLocked`, `LandPlayGateLocked`, the game-end gates, the untap caps) walks every seat's emblems beside the battlefield with the emblem as the source, so "your opponents" is its owner's (CR 114.2). The action, the legal-move enumerator and the view already share each gate, so all three see an emblem with no caller changed. Narset's emblem is deliberately not a `CastBanRule`: that is a record stored on each banned player, invisible on the board, and nothing would end it when the emblem's owner leaves the game (CR 800.4a). Two delayed-trigger keys are new on-disk vocabulary: `cast/you-next-cast-from-hand` and `rebound/the-spell-you-just-cast`. **Cards:** Narset Transcendent, Dovin Baan and Gideon of the Trials (Full; Gideon's +1 is ADR 0108 PR 6's source shield until your next turn).

- **Players can't play lands** (#1895, [ADR 0109](decisions/0109-rule-gates-land-types-mana-and-cost-components.md) §4, PR 5) — a land play is a special action, not a cast (CR 305.1, CR 116.2a), so `CastGateLocked` never reached one. A land play now has its own gate, `Game.LandPlayGateLocked(player, card, fromZone)`, asked before the drop count because CR 101.2 makes "can't" beat "can" (an extra land drop does not lift it). It has two sources: **statics on permanents** (`Spec.LandPlayRestrictions`, a `game.LandPlayRestriction{Label, Forbids(LandPlayQuery), ActiveWhen}` read from the battlefield through `CatalogAbilityKey`, with constructors for "players", "you", "your opponents from a zone", "a land with a name" and "each opponent who controls more lands") and the **"this turn" ban** from a resolved spell or ability (`ModCantPlayLands`, a `ScopedEffect` naming one player, swept at cleanup, CR 514.2; a ScopedEffect and not a cast-ban kind so an older binary refuses the file instead of banning nothing). The gate has four callers, found by grep: the land branch of the play path, the land play a resolution instructs (hideaway's free play, CR 305.2a), the legal-move enumerator, and the view, so a bot is never offered a land the server refuses and the client greys it with the refusing clause on the land's own `cant_cast`. `PlayerView.cant_play_lands` names a seat's blanket ban. `game.ArabianNightsNames` is CR 206.3a's list. **Cards:** City in a Bottle, Territorial Dispute, Aggressive Mining, Pardic Miner, Turf Wound, Solfatara, Moonhold, Rock Jockey, Ward of Bones and Experimental Frenzy (Full). **Moved to other rows:** Tomik (waits on the graveyard-targeting row, ADR 0109 PR 6), Worms of the Earth (its own "lands can't enter" row), Damping Engine (ignoring an effect by sacrificing) and Shaman's Trance (playing from other players' graveyards).

- **Effects that last while a permanent stays tapped, and while it has a counter** (#1894, #1604, [ADR 0109](decisions/0109-rule-gates-land-types-mana-and-cost-components.md) §3 and §2's condition half, PR 2) — a `Duration` can now be a conjunction: `Duration.Also []DurationCondition` lists conditions that must hold as well as `Condition`, so Seasinger's "for as long as you control this creature and this creature remains tapped" ends the moment either stops (CR 611.2b). Three conditions were appended, all read in the one sweep (`durationConditionHoldsLocked`): `WhilePinnedHasCounter` with `Duration.CounterKind` ("for as long as it has a flood counter on it"), `WhilePinnedRemainsTapped` (Zygon Infiltrator's "for as long as that creature remains tapped") and `WhilePinnedPowerAtMostSource` (Old Man of the Sea's power comparison, re-checked after every layer pass). Every constructor answers CR 611.2b's "never starts". The restore check covers every stored duration (ADR 0109 Shared machinery 2, `game/snapshot_durations.go`): the routes to a `Duration` are reflected from the snapshot types, and an unknown key or a duration this binary cannot read (`Duration.Problem`) is refused with `ErrUnknownEffectKey` on a cast permission, a player static and an untap hold, not only on a scoped effect and a delayed trigger. Card side: `effects/remains_tapped.go` (`WhileYouControlThisAndItRemainsTapped`, `DurationWhileThisRemainsTapped`, `TargetGetsWhileThisRemainsTapped`, `GainControlOfTargetFor`, `TapTargetsAndHoldWhileThisRemainsTapped`) and `effects/counter_held.go` (`CounterThenWhileItHasIt`, `GrantWhileItHasCounter`, `FloodTargetLand`). A v7 corpus fixture holds a conjunction and a counter-held duration. **Cards**: Seasinger, Helm of Possession, Hivis of the Scale, Rubinia Soulsinger, Willow Satyr and Zygon Infiltrator; the counter-held Aquitect's Will, Xolatoyac, Aven Mimeomancer, Liege of the Tangle, Minas Morgul, Mathas and Obsidian Fireheart, and Makeshift Mannequin (with a caveat); and the single "for as long as this remains tapped" cards (owner decision 4) Ashnod's Battle Gear, Deserter's Quarters, Endoskeleton, Everglove, Flamestick, Frightshroud, Ghosthelm and Pearlspear Courier, Flowstone Armor, Hisoka's Guard, Ice Floe, Kill Switch, Mana Leech, Mole Worms, Phyrexian Gremlins, Sand Squid, Spirit Shield, Tawnos's Weaponry, Thalakos Dreamsower, The Blackstaff of Waterdeep, Vedalken Shackles and Zelyon Sword.

- **Damage doubled or tripled this turn by a spell** (#1890, [ADR 0108](decisions/0108-turn-scoped-effects-object-history-and-damage-shields.md) §3, PR 2) — "it deals double (triple) that damage instead" from a resolving spell or ability (CR 614.1a) is a `ScopedEffect` replacement kind, `multiplyDamage` (`game.ModMultiplyDamage`, `game/multiply_damage.go`), written by `Game.MultiplyDamageForEffect` until end of turn (CR 514.2) or until a player's next turn (CR 611.2b). `Mod.Amount` is the multiplier. Which damage is one source (`Objects` + `SourceZone`, read as the next-damage shield reads its chosen source), or a new `Mod.Sources` value, `yours` or `creatures`, read off the source's last-known information as the damage would be dealt (CR 611.2c, 608.2h), or any source. To what is a new `Mod.Recipients` value, `opponents`, `opponentsAndTheirPermanents`, `playerAndTheirPermanents` (with `Player`) or `creatures`, read live. `CombatOnly`, and a new `Mod.Next` for "the next time": one instance of damage (CR 615.8), spent through `SpentInstance` as the next-damage shield is, so a trampler's damage to its blocker and the player is doubled together and nothing after. A source that would deal 0 deals none (CR 120.8): nothing is multiplied and nothing is spent. It is not a prevention effect, so "damage can't be prevented" leaves it alone, and two multipliers commute, so no CR 616 order is asked between them (two Insults are ×4). `choose_source` gained a controller filter (`ChooseSourcePrompt.Controller`; a card nobody controls answers by its owner, CR 108.4a), and the next-damage shield a combat-only form, a kind of its own (`preventNextCombatFromSource`) so that a binary from before it refuses the restore point rather than reading an ordinary shield. Restore refuses a `sources` or `recipients` value this binary does not know, and the three fields on any other kind. The table sees a banner line per record (`GameView.damage_multipliers`). A v7 corpus fixture holds every shape. **Cards** (all Full): Insult // Injury, Isengard Unleashed, Desperate Gambit, Goblin Goliath, Blind Fury, Impulsive Maneuvers, Quest for Pure Flame, Lightning, Army of One. **Moved to other rows:** Jeska, Thrice Reborn (loyalty-cost-x, #1944: her −X), Overblaze (splice, #1989).

- **Echo, and pay-unless payments that are not mana** (#1888, CR 702.30a / CR 603.4 / CR 118.12a / CR 702.24a, [ADR 0108 §5](decisions/0108-turn-scoped-effects-object-history-and-damage-shields.md)) — "At the beginning of your upkeep, if this permanent came under your control since the beginning of your last upkeep, sacrifice it unless you pay [cost]." The fact is two counters: `Player.UpkeepsBegun` goes up as that player's upkeep begins, before any upkeep trigger is checked, and `Card.ControlledSinceUpkeep` is stamped with the controller's count as the permanent enters and at each control change the layer pass materialises.
