The enumerator reads `CardPaymentCount` / `AltCostCandidatesLocked` as for escape, and `CastAutoTapExclusions` now holds `alt_cost_ids` (a Thalia tax on a flashed-back Dread Return cannot be paid by cracking an Eldrazi Spawn the sacrifice names), with the enumerator checking affordability under the same exclusion. Constructors `effects.FlashbackSacrifice` and `effects.SacrificeInstead`; `Register` refuses a variable count and an offer with two card components. **4 cards, all `full`:** Dread Return (adopted; its `adoptableGaps` pin is gone), Lava Dart, Fireblast, Demon of Death's Gate. **Still open:** emerge (CR 702.119 — the sacrificed creature's mana value reduces the price), Firecat Blitz's "Sacrifice X Mountains" on a flashback, a tap price on flashback (Battle Screech, Prismatic Strands), and Cabal Therapy, whose front half needs a card-name choice at resolution.

- **Entering under an opponent's control** (#1759, [ADR 0102](decisions/0102-entering-under-another-players-control.md)) — "this enters under the control of an opponent of your choice" is a CR 614.1d replacement effect asked inside the entry window (`entry_controller`, CR 614.12a), ordered before every other entry effect (CR 616.1b), so the permanent lands under the chosen player and its enters triggers, "enters under your control" readers and summoning sickness are theirs. Captive Audience, Pendant of Prosperity and Abby, Merciless Soldier shipped with it. Xantcha, Sleeper Agent still waits on #1793 (any player may activate) and #1794 (can't attack its owner).

- **Rooms, and casting either half of a split card** (#1756, [ADR 0103](decisions/0103-rooms.md)): a Room's two doors are `Card.Unlocked`, the CR 709.5c designations. A Room spell enters with the door of the half that was cast unlocked (CR 709.5d) and "when you unlock this door" fires as it enters (CR 709.5h). A locked half has no name, mana cost or rules text on the battlefield, so a fully locked Room is nameless, colourless and mana value 0. The `unlock` special action (CR 709.5e, 116.2m) pays a locked door's cost at sorcery speed; its offer comes from the card, so an uncatalogued Room can be unlocked too. `UnlockDoorForEffect` and `LockDoorForEffect` are the instructions (CR 709.5f-g), and `EventRoomFullyUnlocked` is the full unlock (CR 709.5i). The designation gate reaches replacements, untap permissions and "no maximum hand size" as well, and `effects.Room` gates every ability of a door. Every split card now casts either half (CR 709.3) and is both halves combined off the stack (CR 709.4); an aftermath half casts only from a graveyard (CR 702.127a), and fuse casts both halves from hand (CR 702.102). Still open: the Room card files themselves (ADR 0103 PRs 3 and 4), manifest dread (four Rooms wait on it), and a duration copy of a Room keeping the whole card's name.

- **Keyword counters** (#1753, [ADR 0101](decisions/0101-keyword-counters.md)): a flying, first strike, double strike, deathtouch, haste, hexproof, indestructible, lifelink, menace, reach, shadow, trample or vigilance counter gives its object that keyword (CR 122.1b). The engine reads the counter itself, as a layer-6 effect at the counter's own CR 613.7c timestamp (`Card.CounterStampedAt`, stamped by the one counter door), so a "loses all abilities" older than the counter leaves the keyword and a newer one takes it, a "can't have" beats it, proliferate renews it, and a card in another zone with a keyword counter has the keyword too. `b24KeywordCounterGrant` is gone: Vraska Joins Up and Sorin of House Markov lost their caveats, and Tekuthal, Solphim and Drivnod no longer carry a static for their own counters. Perennation joined the catalog. Still open: decayed, exalted and "hexproof from" counters, which wait on those keywords.

- **Modes that haven't been chosen** (#1749, [ADR 0097](decisions/0097-modes-that-havent-been-chosen.md)): "choose one that hasn't been chosen [this turn]" is `ModeSpec.NotChosen`, written with `ChooseOneNotChosenThisTurn` / `ChooseOneNotChosen`. The engine keeps the memory per OBJECT and ability (`ObjectTallyKey` on `TurnTally.ModesChosen` for "this turn", `Card.ModesChosen` for "ever", which `MoveCard` clears with CR 400.7), records a mode when it is CHOSEN (a countered trigger has still used its mode, a copy records nothing), and keeps it across a change of control. `choosableModeOptionsLocked` drops the used modes before the no-legal-target filter, so the harvest check removes an exhausted trigger (CR 700.2b), the activation gate refuses a used mode with nothing paid, the enumerator never offers one, and simultaneous instances re-narrow each other's open `mode_pick` prompts. The picker shows used modes greyed out. Gala Greeters and Teval's Judgment moved to the real shape, Silent Hallcreeper lost its "not enforced" caveat and gained its copy bullet (`BecomeCopy`, #1593), and Monument to Endurance joined the catalog. Breeches, Demonic Pact, Kargan Intimidator and the rest of the ADR's list are PR 2's.

- **Gaining control of a spell** (#1745, [ADR 0104](decisions/0104-gaining-control-of-a-spell.md)) — a spell's control is layer 2 (CR 611.1, CR 613.1b), so a steal is a `setController` `ScopedEffect` pinned to the stack object (`AffectedObject.OnStack`/`Epoch`, `Duration.PinnedOnStack`). A stack step of the layer pass (`stackControlPassLocked`) materialises `StackItem.Controller` and the stack card's controller. At resolution the record is re-pinned to the permanent by its epoch, with `Card.BaseController` set to the caster (CR 110.2b, CR 400.7a). `GainControlOfSpellForEffect` and `ExchangeControlOfSpellAndPermanentForEffect` (CR 701.12, one timestamp); card-side `GainControlOfSpell` and `ExchangeControlOfSpellAnd` offer the new controller new targets. A departed player's control effects now end (CR 800.4a), which also fixed Act of Treason's reversion. Shipped on Invert Polarity, Aethersnatch, Commandeer (with `ExileFromHand` taking a count), Sudden Substitution and Perplexing Chimera. Still open: Chef's Kiss's random retarget (#1815).

- **Discarding or sacrificing as a permanent would enter** (#1744, [ADR 0098](decisions/0098-discard-as-a-permanent-would-enter.md)): "if this would enter, you may discard a land card instead … if you don't, put it into its owner's graveyard" and "sacrifice a Forest instead" are one CR 614.1a entry replacement with a choice inside it. #1198's `EntryHandReveal` is now `ReplacementEffect.EntryCardChoice` with an `Action` (reveal, discard, sacrifice), asked through `entry_discard_from_hand` and `entry_sacrifice` beside `entry_reveal_from_hand`. The discard is an effect's (`DiscardCauseEffect`, so Library of Leng and every discard payoff see it) and the sacrifice goes through `SacrificeAllThenForEffect`; either may pause, and the paused entry rides a frozen copy of the event. "If you do" means the card really left. A redirected entry is now moved to where its window sent it (`moveRedirectedEntryLocked`, inside `executeEntryToBattlefieldLocked`), fixing two latent bugs: three entry sites treated a redirect as a cancel (stranding a resolving permanent on the stack) and the stack-resolution and land-play sites put the card onto the battlefield anyway. A window that cannot ask now runs the decline of a question whose decline IS the replacement (`declineIsReplace`: a shockland or reveal-land enters tapped, a Mox goes to the graveyard) instead of skipping it, and the apply-loop and `ResolveReplacementOrder` share one question dispatcher (`offerOwnQuestionLocked`), which gives an ordered reveal-land its prompt back. **Cards:** Mox Diamond, Heart of Yavimaya, Kjeldoran Outpost, Lake of the Dead, Balduvian Trading Post, Soldevi Excavations, Lotus Vale and Scorched Ruins, all complete. Still open on the row: Indominus Rex, Alpha.

- **Delve, the payment** (#1732, [ADR 0100](decisions/0100-delve-either-or-and-variable-sacrifice-costs.md) sub-PR 1) — a spell with `Spec.Delve` may exile cards from its caster's graveyard to pay the generic mana in its total cost (CR 702.66a). The budget is `CastPrice.DelveBudget`, priced after the cost modifiers and the convoke taps and without the colours a "spend as though any colour" grant folded in, so the validator, the view, the auto-tap preview and the bot all read one number. The cards are exiled at CR 601.2h with the spell on the stack, a delved commander is asked about CR 903.9 first, and `PaidCost.Delved` records the objects that landed in exile. Still open: the readers of the cards "exiled with" a permanent (Murktide Regent, Soulflayer, CR 607.2q) and Teval's granted delve — see the Delve row.

- **Delve, the cards exiled with it and granted delve** (#1732, [ADR 0100](decisions/0100-delve-either-or-and-variable-sacrifice-costs.md) sub-PR 2) — a permanent whose spell delved is linked to the cards delve exiled to pay for it (CR 607.2q), and only while each is still in exile as that object (CR 400.7). `Game.DelvedCardsForEffect` applies that rule for every reader: `CastCounts.Delved` with `CountersPerDelved` is Murktide Regent's "enters with a +1/+1 counter on it for each instant and sorcery card exiled with it"; `CastProvenance.Delved` (`Card.Delved()`) carries the link onto the permanent and `PermanentInfo.Delved` onto its last-known information, which Soulflayer's layer-6 keywords (with `StaticAbility.DependsOnExile`, so a card leaving exile refreshes them) and Ethereal Forager's attack trigger (which still finds the cards after the Forager has left) read. `Spec.SpellsYouCastHaveDelve` is Teval, Arbiter of Virtue's "Spells you cast have delve", derived off the caster's permanents in the one `DelveForLocked` door, so the pricer, the view and the bot see it with no change. 5 cards shipped, Temporal Trespass among them now that extra turns exist. Still open: Tasigur, the Golden Fang, Afterlife from the Loam and Sorcerous Squall, held for their other text; see the Delve row.

- **Either/or additional costs** (#1732, [ADR 0100](decisions/0100-delve-either-or-and-variable-sacrifice-costs.md) sub-PR 3) — "As an additional cost to cast this spell, sacrifice an artifact or discard a card" is one mandatory `AdditionalCost` whose `Either` branches are ordinary costs with a `Key`. The caster announces the branch as `cost_branch` (CR 601.2b), required on a branched card and refused on any other, and the chosen branch becomes the plan's mandatory entry, so there is still one validator and one payer. A branch may carry mana (priced at CR 601.2f by `AdditionalCostMana`, next to the kicker's), a discard, a fixed sacrifice, a fixed life payment (`PayLife`, CR 119.4) or a blight. `AdditionalCostBranchPayableLocked` answers the view's `payable`, the bot's one-move-per-branch policy and `CastSpell` alike (CR 118.3, 601.2h). `PaidCost.CostBranch` and `PaidCost.Discarded` record the branch and the discarded cards (Grab the Prize, owner decision 6). 22 cards shipped. Still open: the branch components the cast path has no shape for — reveal, behold, tap an artifact, exile from the graveyard, forage — and an either/or ward; see the Either/or additional costs row.

- **Variable sacrifice costs on spells** (#1732, [ADR 0100](decisions/0100-delve-either-or-and-variable-sacrifice-costs.md) sub-PR 4) — "As an additional cost to cast this spell, sacrifice X lands" and "(you may) sacrifice any number of creatures" are two variable shapes of a cast's mandatory sacrifice clause: `SacrificeXCost` (the count is the announced X, CR 107.3a / 107.3i) and `SacrificeAnyNumberCost` (Min 0 / Max 0 — "you may" and "any number" mean the same thing, because sacrificing none is not paying). A cast's plan holds at most one variable clause and, with one, no other sacrifice, so the clause takes the whole flat `sacrifice_ids` list; `Register` enforces the rule across the mandatory slot, the either/or branches and `OptionalCosts`. The count is `PaidCost.Sacrificed`, and `CostQuery.Sacrificing` carries it to the per-sacrifice discount `CostsLessPerSacrificed` at CR 601.2f, so the preview, the bot and `CastSpell` charge one price. Bots are offered zero plus up to three counts, each priced on its own. 10 cards shipped. Still open: Corpse Cobble, which reads the sacrificed creatures' total power (last-known information for a list); see the Variable sacrifice costs on spells row.

- **Delayed trigger on a named player's turn** (#1538, CR 603.7 / 603.7d) — `game.DelayedTrigger.TurnOf` (and `effects.ScheduleDelayedTrigger.TurnOf`) names the one player whose turn the step must belong to, checked beside `ControllerTurnOnly` in `fireDelayedTriggersLocked`. It is the answer to "at the beginning of **that player's** next end step" without misstating CR 603.7d: the delayed ability is still controlled by whoever's effect created it, so it is their stack item and their APNAP slot, and only the moment it fires is the named player's. A trigger whose named player has left the game is dropped (CR 800.4a). The field is carried by the snapshot (`delayedTriggers[].turnOf`, additive within schema 7) and by clone. Proof card: The Eternal Wanderer's +1, which now returns the exiled card on its owner's end step (`exileTargetsThenReturnOnOwnersEndStep`, one delayed trigger per owner) and dropped its caveat. No seam row existed, because the gap earns one only when a second card waits on it.

- **Discover** (#1112, [ADR 0099](decisions/0099-discover.md)): "discover N" (CR 701.57) is `effects.Discover{N}` / `DiscoverN(n)` over `Game.DiscoverThenForEffect`, callable from a spell, a trigger or an activated ability, with `Spec.Discovers` declaring it for cards/coverage. It shares cascade's exile-until walk and `may_cast` prompt (now worded per keyword, with `may_cast_keyword` and the branch labels on the wire). Accepting stamps a `{0}` grant with `TimingFlash` (CR 608.2g) and a `MaxSpellManaValue` cap judged against the face being cast; the grant closes on the discoverer's next priority pass (`CastPermission.LapseOnPass`, miracle's rule) and the card goes to hand. `EventDiscover` fires when the card is settled — after the discovered spell's `EventCast`, so "whenever you discover" resolves first — and `WheneverYouDiscover` watches it. Cascade moved onto the same cap, timing and pass-closed window. Primordial Gnawer and Tecutlan, the Searing Rift ship with it; the other discover cards are the card PR's.

- **Extra combat and main phases** (#753, [ADR 0059](decisions/0059-turn-machinery.md) Decisions 3, 4 and 8 and its sub-PR 2b amendment): the rest of the turn is data. `Game.TurnPlan` is the steps still to come, `advanceCursorLocked` pops it, and an effect splices phases into it with `Game.AddPhasesForEffect` ("after this phase", "after this main phase") or a step with `Game.AddStepAfterCurrentForEffect` (CR 500.8 / 500.9), newest first. An added main phase is a postcombat main phase (CR 505.1a), so the Saga lore action and "at the beginning of your precombat main phase" happen once. #717's first-strike damage step is a plan entry of every combat and is still walked through when nobody has first or double strike. `Turn.PhaseID`, `PhaseOrdinal` and `StepOrdinal` answer "the first combat phase of the turn" and "the first end step of the turn", and `TurnTally.Attacks` answers "attacked this turn" and "attacks for the first time each turn" per object. A step-conditioned delayed trigger with a duration fires at every matching step ("at the beginning of each combat this turn", CR 603.7b). The plan clones and snapshots as plain data; the wire has `TurnView.phase_id` / `phase_ordinal` / `upcoming` and an `extra_phase` log line. Relentless Assault, Seize the Day, Aggravated Assault, Full Throttle, Karlach, Fury of Avernus, Hellkite Charger, Aurelia, the Warleader, Sphinx of the Second Sun and Éomer, Marshal of Rohan joined the catalog, and Y'shtola Rhul's additional end step is wired. Still open: an additional phase after "the second main phase" and a trigger bound to one added combat (World at War, Moraug, Fury of Akoum).

- **Extra turns** (#753, [ADR 0059](decisions/0059-turn-machinery.md) Decision 5 and its 2026-09-30 amendment): "take an extra turn after this one" (CR 500.7) is `effects.TakeExtraTurn`, over the one engine door `Game.TakeExtraTurnsForEffect`. The queue is a stack on `Game.ExtraTurns` popped by the single rotation seam, so the most recently created turn is taken first, normal rotation resumes from `Turn.OrderSeat`, `Turn.Round` never moves on an extra turn, and every per-turn reset runs (a planeswalker activates again, the land drop comes back). A queued turn of a player who has left is dropped as it would begin and still counts toward `TurnsBegun` (CR 800.4k / 800.4m). `DelayedTrigger.OnExtraTurn` binds "at the beginning of that turn's end step" to the turn, and a bound trigger whose turn is dropped or ends early is swept. The queue and the binding clone and snapshot as plain data; the wire has `TurnView.extra` / `extra_turns` and an `extra_turn` log line, and the board marks the turn. Time Warp, Time Stretch, Temporal Manipulation, Capture of Jingzhou, Temporal Mastery, Magistrate's Scepter, Final Fortune, Last Chance and Warrior's Oath joined the catalog, and Teferi, Master of Time's −10 and Avatar Kuruk's exhaust ability are wired. Still open: skipping a turn (CR 614.10 — Trouble in Pairs, Ugin's Nexus, Savor the Moment), and the extra combat and main phases of ADR 0059 Decision 3-4.

- **Shuffle after a replacement move** (#1735, [ADR 0013 §5ah](decisions/0013-replacement-effects.md#5ah-amendment-2026-09-29-a-redirected-library-destination-can-ask-for-a-shuffle)): Blightsteel Colossus's "reveal it and shuffle it into its owner's library instead" landed the card on TOP of the library, because the CR 614 pipeline could redirect a move's destination but had no hook to shuffle once the card actually got there — a real information leak (everyone at the table knows the top card). **`ReplacementEvent.ShuffleDestinationLibrary`** is the fix: a bool a replacement's `Replace` sets alongside the redirected `NewZone`, consumed once by whichever of the two exit primitives performs the physical landing — `executeBattlefieldLeaveLocked` (die/sacrifice/SBA) or `executeZoneRouteLocked` (discard, mill, exile, bounce, tuck, counter) — both of which now call `ShuffleLibraryForEffect` on the settled destination. A mill's redirect lands the card back in the SAME library it started in (`dstZone == src`, previously treated as a pure no-op); that branch shuffles too, since there is nowhere to move the card FROM but it still has to end up at a random position rather than exactly where it was. Blightsteel's own `AppliesTo` also grows `RepEventDiscard` alongside `RepEventMove` — a discard is its own `ReplacementEventKind` (§5g) under a different `Watches` key, so its "from anywhere" covered only move-shaped exits before this, and a discarded Blightsteel fell straight into the graveyard. Blightsteel moves to `CompletenessFull`. Checked the Eldrazi titans named alongside it in #1735 and found a different mechanic, not this seam: their printed clause triggers a shuffle of the whole graveyard AFTER the titan has already landed in it, rather than redirecting the titan's own move — recorded in the ADR rather than built, and gated on Annihilator (not implemented) regardless.

- **"Until your next turn" copy effects, and X-bound targets on an activated ability** (#1723, CR 611.2b, CR 602.2b, [ADR 0043 amendment 2026-09-28 (#1723)](decisions/0043-copy-effects.md), [ADR 0019 amendment 2026-09-28 (#1723)](decisions/0019-structured-targeting.md)). Two small seams #1720's builder found, closed together because both are one-field additions with no new engine machinery. **First:** `effects.BecomeCopy.Indefinite bool` covered only "until end of turn" and no stated duration; `Duration CopyDuration` is now a three-value enum adding `CopyUntilYourNextTurn`, which `Apply` turns into `game.UntilYourNextTurnDuration(ctx.Controller())` — an engine kind that already existed and that `durationExpiredLocked` already swept generically, so nothing there changed. **Second:** `TargetSpec.ManaValueAtMostX` ("X or less") gets an exact twin, `ManaValueEqualsX`, sharing the same `xBound` / `xBoundSet` bind-and-recheck machinery with `==` instead of `<=`; and `effects.Register`'s `checkNoXBound` now takes an `xAvailable bool` (`a.Cost.DemandsX()` for an activated ability's clause, still `false` for a trigger's) instead of refusing every non-spell owner outright — the announce and CR 608.2b re-check paths needed no change at all, since `ActivateCatalogAbility` and `itemAnnouncedClauses` already ran the same bind generically over "the announcement," ability items included. The one real piece of new logic is in the bot enumerator: "with mana value X" is NOT monotonic in X the way "or less" is, so `internal/legal/abilities.go` cannot pick the single largest affordable X the way every other `{X}` ability does — it tries every X from the floor up and keeps only the `(X, target)` pairs that are actually legal, the pairwise form of #544's rule. **Cards:** Shapesharer (new, `full`) uses the first half; Lazav, the Multifarious (new, `full`) uses the second (and the first, indefinitely, for its own copy). **Still open**, named in the ADR 0043 amendment (#1593) this narrows: Mizzium Transreliquat's second ability and Dimir Doppelganger and Lazav, Dimir Mastermind's indefinite copies were already shipped before this issue; last-known copiable values of a permanent that has left the battlefield remains unaddressed.

- **The monarch from a card effect** (#1722, CR 725, [ADR 0096](decisions/0096-the-monarch-from-a-card-effect.md)) — `Game.SetMonarch` took `g.mu` itself, so "you become the monarch" deadlocked from any `OnResolve` or trigger `Effect`, and no catalog card could hand out the crown. `becomeMonarchLocked` is now the one write to `Game.Monarch` — the card effect (`SetMonarchForEffect`, the `effect_api.go` convention), the CR 725.2 combat-damage steal, the CR 725.4 hand-on and the sandbox's manual `set_monarch` all go through it — and it emits **`EventMonarchChanged`** (Actor = new monarch, Target = previous) only on a real change of holder (CR 725.3), so "whenever you become the monarch" fires once per becoming. `layerVersionBump` invalidates on it, for "as long as you're the monarch" statics; `TurnTally.MonarchAtStart` (read through `Game.MonarchAsTurnBegan`) stamps the crown as each turn begins. Card side (`cards/effects/monarch.go`): `BecomeTheMonarch`, `WhenThisEntersYouBecomeTheMonarch`, `WheneverYouBecomeTheMonarch`, `AnOpponentBecameTheMonarch`, `YoureTheMonarch`, `AnOpponentIsTheMonarch`. Palace Jailer's "until an opponent becomes the monarch" is not an ADR 0063 duration — it is an event-conditioned delayed trigger (#663's shape) on `EventMonarchChanged` with the keyed condition `monarch/an-opponent-became` and an Indefinite duration. A test for CR 725.4's "next player in turn order" clause was added beside the existing active-player one. No wire change; the log gate lists the event as silent. **Cards:** Court of Grace, Court of Ambition, Court of Cunning, Court of Bounty, Court of Ire, Queen Marchesa, Entourage of Trest, Custodi Lich, Knights of the Black Rose, Regal Behemoth and Throne of the High City (all `full`), and Palace Jailer (`caveats`: if its controller leaves the game, CR 800.4a drops the release trigger before the CR 725.4 hand-on, so the creature stays exiled). **Still open:** Court of Locthwain — its monarch half is a single-use free cast from among cards exiled with a source, and a `{0}` cast permission over that set would let every card be cast free. Tracker [#883](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/883).

- **A target clause widened by an announced optional cost** (#1716, CR 601.2b / 601.2c / 608.2b, [ADR 0089 amendment 2026-09-28](decisions/0089-gift.md)) — "if this spell was kicked / cast using teamwork, instead … target …". The issue framed it as `TargetSpec.CardOK` being unable to read `item.Paid.OptionalCosts`; it does not need to. ADR 0089 §3's `AdditionalCost.Targets` already REPLACES the whole clause when that optional cost is paid, and `game.TargetSpecUnderOptionalCosts` is the one "which clause applies to this cast" answer: at announce (the result is stored on the stack item and is what the live CR 608.2b re-check reads), in `castTargetSpecForItem` for restore and copies (from `StackItem.Paid.OptionalCosts`), in the enumerator, and now in the view too — `viewOfOptionalCosts` asks it per offer instead of reading `oc.Targets` directly. Two changes made it reachable beyond gift: `effects.WhenPaid(cost, clause)`, `Gift.Instead`'s twin for kicker, teamwork and blight; and `checkTeamworkBlight` no longer counts a clause rewrite as a second payment component, which was the boot-time refusal #1703 hit on Cruel Alliance. No wire change: `optional_costs[i].target_mode` / `legal_targets` / `clauses` already existed and the client's `castTargetOverride` already takes the first ticked offer carrying one. **Cards (all `full`):** Too Evil to Stay Dead (caveat cleared), Cruel Alliance, Bloodchief's Thirst, Tear Asunder, Expel the Unworthy, Blood Beckoning and Divine Resilience. **Still open, each a different shape:** The Eagles Are Coming! (a delayed per-card token count), Waste Management (a player-targeted graveyard exile whose token count reads what was exiled), Galadriel's Dismissal (untargeted phasing of a target player's creatures) and Fight with Fire (a kicked divided "any number of targets"). Tracker [#887](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/887).

- **Turn-scoped block capacity, "blocks each attacking creature" and "blocks two or more"** (#1715, CR 509.1a–c, 509.3e and 802.2, [ADR 0045 amendment 2026-09-28 (#1715)](decisions/0045-combat-restrictions.md) Decisions 64–67). Closes the three items #1706 left open. "Can block an additional creature this turn" and "can block any number of creatures this turn" from a resolving spell or ability are two ADR 0041 layer-6 mod kinds, `addBlockCapacity` (`Amount`, at least 1) and `blockAnyNumber`, writing the same `Characteristic.AdditionalBlocks` / `BlocksAnyNumber` the statics write, so they add up and `game.BlockCapacity` stays the one reader; the record is pinned, ends at cleanup and rides undo and the snapshot (card side: `effects.BlockCapacityUntilEOT`). "It blocks each attacking creature this turn if able" is a sixth CR 509.1c kind, `blocksEach`, on the blocker and counted per attacker — Lure from the blocker's side — so the existing search makes the creature block every attacker it can within its capacity. "Target creature defending player controls" on a spell reads `Game.IsDefendingPlayerForEffect`: every opponent of the active player during the combat phase (CR 802.2). "Whenever this creature blocks two or more creatures" reads the numbered `EventBlock` (`Amount == 2`), so it fires once. **Coastline Chimera**, **Mounted Archers**, **Anurid Swarmsnapper**, **Luminous Guardian**, **Give No Ground**, **Valor Made Real**, **Act of Heroism**, **Yare**, **Blaze of Glory** and **Lairwatch Giant** are new and `full`. **Still open:** re-pointing a multi-blocker away from one of its attackers (a new action), and the bot's attack-side estimates that still assume one attacker per blocker.

- **Counter-placing costs join the CR 614 replacement window** (#1710, CR 614.16 / CR 606.4, [ADR 0073 amendment 2026-09-28](decisions/0073-optional-additional-costs-and-the-cast-gate.md)). Devoted Druid's "put a -1/-1 counter on this creature" cost and a planeswalker's loyalty cost (both the catalogued CR 602 path in `activated.go` and the `ActivateLoyalty` sandbox verb in `mutations.go`) used to write the counter-map mutation directly, on the reasoning that a counter-doubling replacement applies only to a counter placed by an EFFECT (CR 614.16) — true of Doubling Season, but it also skipped every replacement that names no effect at all, which CR 614.16 does not exclude. `game.payCostCounterLocked` (`counter_cost.go`) generalises `blightLocked`'s #1703 pattern off the blight-specific call: it opens the window with `ReplacementEvent.CounterFromCost` set and settles without pausing (a cost is paid in one indivisible step, CR 601.2h / 602.2b). Doubling Season's existing `CounterFromCost` gate still declines both; **Vizier of Remedies now reduces the Druid's cost to zero counters** (the long-standing infinite-mana combo, confirmed against Gatherer's Doubling Season ruling that a loyalty ability's cost is "put on as a cost, not as an effect" the same way), **Winding Constrictor adds one to the Druid's placement**, and **Vorinclex, Monstrous Raider doubles a `+1` loyalty ability to `+2`** — all three previously inert. The ability still resolves even when a replacement erases its counter entirely (CR 118.3 was already checked before payment); `PaidCost.CountersAdded` now records what actually landed instead of the printed number. A negative loyalty delta (a "-N" ability) is unaffected, since every registered placement replacement gates on a positive `CounterDelta` (CR 614.1 — a removal is not a placement). **Cards:** Vizier of Remedies (new, `full`) joins Winding Constrictor and Vorinclex, Monstrous Raider, both already catalogued.

- **A creature that blocks more than one attacker** (#1706, CR 509.1a/b and 510.1d, [ADR 0045 amendment 2026-09-28 (#1706)](decisions/0045-combat-restrictions.md) Decisions 60–63). "Can block an additional creature each combat", "an additional N creatures" and "can block any number of creatures" are a per-blocker capacity on the effective characteristic (`Characteristic.AdditionalBlocks` / `BlocksAnyNumber`, read by `game.BlockCapacity`), written by layer statics (`effects.CanBlockAdditional`, `effects.CanBlockAnyNumber`). A blocker maps to a set of attackers: `Card.BlockingTarget` stays the first and `Card.AlsoBlocking` holds the rest, and the block validator, the whole-combat limits and the CR 509.1c requirement search all read a blocker → attackers assignment. A declaration entry ADDS an attacker to a blocker with room, re-points an ordinary blocker as before, and is refused with `illegal_block` / `blocker_capacity` past the capacity; menace still needs two creatures, a limit counts the creature once, and "blocks each combat" is one requirement however many attackers it takes. A blocker still blocking two or more attackers divides its combat damage among them through the reused damage-assignment prompt (`blocker_divides`, free split, no trample). The lock-in announces one `EventBlock` per pair numbered in `Amount`, so "whenever this blocks" (CR 509.3a) fires once. The wire adds `CardView.blocking_targets`, `block_capacity` and `blocks_any_number`; the snapshot adds `alsoBlocking` and `announcedAlsoBlocks`, and an older file restores its single block. **Brave the Sands** and **Hundred-Handed One** lose their caveats and ship `full`; **Palace Guard**, **High Ground**, **Guardian of the Gateless**, **Two-Headed Giant of Foriys**, **Wall of Glare**, **Echo Circlet** and **Watcher in the Web** are new and `full`. **Still open:** "can block an additional creature this turn" and "can block any number of creatures this turn" from a resolving spell or ability (Coastline Chimera, Mounted Archers, Give No Ground, Valor Made Real) need an ADR 0041 data-record mod, and "blocks each attacking creature if able" (Blaze of Glory) a requirement kind. Conditional statics (Kemba's Legion, Cenn's Tactician, Entourage of Trest, Foriysian Totem) are one file each with the builders here, left for a card batch.

- **Teamwork and blight as optional costs** (#1703, CR 702.194a / CR 701.68a at CR 601.2h, [ADR 0073 amendment 2026-09-28](decisions/0073-optional-additional-costs-and-the-cast-gate.md)). Two components of `game.AdditionalCost`, announced by index like a kicker. **Teamwork** (`AdditionalCost.Teamwork`, `effects.Teamwork(n)`) is crew's sentence on a spell: the caster names the creatures on `cast_spell.teamwork_ids`, and `validateTeamworkLocked` checks what crew checks — untapped creatures they control, total effective power at least N, no summoning-sickness check (CR 302.6 is about a creature's own `{T}`). The taps land with the spell on the stack and the creatures are out of the auto-tapper's reach. **Blight** (`AdditionalCost.Blight`, `effects.OptionalBlight(n)`) names one creature the caster controls on `cast_spell.blight_ids`; a creature that dies of the counters still pays. The counters go through the CR 614 counter window marked `ReplacementEvent.CounterFromCost` and settle without pausing: Doubling Season's "if an effect would put" skips them (CR 614.16), while Winding Constrictor and Vizier of Remedies, which name no effect, apply. The hand card's `optional_costs[i]` carries `teamwork` / `teamwork_options` and `blight` / `blight_options` (present-and-empty when the board cannot pay), the client opens the crew picker or a one-creature picker after the add-on toggle, and the enumerator makes one payment per announced set (`crewPayment` for teamwork, a survivor first for blight), checked for affordability with those creatures excluded from the plan. "Cast using teamwork" is `ctx.UsedTeamwork()` and the `TeamworkUsed` mode condition; "if this spell's additional cost was paid" is `ctx.BlightPaid()` and `BlightUsed`. **Cards:** HULK SMASH!, Go Nuts!, Atlantis Attacks, Murdock's Crusade, Widow's Bite, Pyrrhic Strike, Repulsor Blast, Cinder Strike and Requiting Hex, all `full`. **Still open:** "blight N or pay {M}" and "blight X" as mandatory costs (Wild Unraveling, Bogslither's Embrace, Soul Immolation), blight on an activated ability's cost (Dawnhand Dissident, Gristle Glutton, Sting-Slinger), "you may blight N. If you do" as an effect (Sourbread Auntie, Dream Seizer), "becomes tapped to pay a teamwork cost" (Agent Maria Hill) and "cast a spell using teamwork" (Virtual Assistant), which no event names. Devoted Druid's and the loyalty cost's counters used to skip the window entirely; #1710 (below) moves them onto it too.

- **Monstrosity** (#1700, CR 701.37, [ADR 0071 amendment 2026-09-28](decisions/0071-designations-that-switch-abilities-on.md)) — a sixth designation with Harnessed's lifecycle: `Card.Monstrous`, not copiable, cleared at both CR 400.7 sites, carried by clone and the snapshot (additive to the v7 shape). `DesignationMonstrous` / `game.Monstrous()` gates "as long as this creature is monstrous" lines through the four ability accessors with no other change. `Game.MonstrosityForEffect(cardID, n)` is the keyword action: it does nothing for a permanent that is already monstrous, checked on resolution so a second activation in response resolves to nothing; otherwise it puts N +1/+1 counters on through the CR 614 counter pipeline (Doubling Season and Hardened Scales apply, and a CR 616 ordering prompt pauses it), and as the placement's continuation sets the flag and emits `EventBecameMonstrous` with **Amount = the announced N**, not the counters that landed, so a doubled placement leaves a trigger's X alone. The event bumps the layer version (load-bearing at X = 0, where no counter event does) and is silent on the log; `CardView.monstrous` carries the badge. Card side (`cards/effects/monstrosity.go`): `Monstrosity(cost, n)`, `MonstrosityX(cost)`, `WhenBecomesMonstrous(label, effect)`, `MonstrosityXOf(item)`, `MonstrousKeywords(kws...)`. Polukranos's "X damage divided" needs no new divide shape: its `TargetsFrom` reads X off the trigger context and declares a fixed `Divide(X)` with at most X targets. **8 cards:** Polukranos, World Eater, Stormbreath Dragon, Hydra Broodmaster, Ember Swallower, Arbor Colossus, Fleecemane Lion and Domesticated Hydra ship `full`; Hundred-Handed One ships with a caveat (it blocks one creature, not a hundred — Brave the Sands' gap). **Still open:** Alpha Deathclaw and Protector of the Wastes ("enters or becomes monstrous", expressible, not attempted), cost-reduced monstrosity (Grim Giganotosaurus, Nemesis of Mortals), and an N that is not announced (Clay Golem's die roll, Maester Seymour).

- **Colour in the leaves-the-battlefield last-known information** (#1689, CR 603.10a, [ADR 0027 amendment 2026-09-28, #1689](decisions/0027-attack-triggers.md)). Teysa, Orzhov Scion's "whenever another black creature you control dies" asked `dead.HasColor("B")` of the card in the graveyard, where no effect applies any more, so a creature painted black only by Darkest Hour, a black-making static or an Aura was not counted — and one an effect had painted another colour still was, because the printed colour is the graveyard fallback. `EventLTB` now also carries `Event.LastKnownColors`, stamped by the same `exitLKI` as the #1675 types, #1679 subtypes and #1682 supertypes/controller; `Event.WasColor` reads it with `Card.HasColor`'s exact-match semantics. Card side, `leftAsColor(ev, c, color)` is the one question; Teysa is the only catalog card that tested a departed permanent's colour, so it is the only switch. This closes the last-known-information family this ADR opened: card types, subtypes, supertypes, controller and now colour are all read as the permanent last existed on the battlefield rather than as the card sits in its new zone.

- **Provoke-style "blocks it" and filtered Lures** (#1684, CR 509.1c and CR 702.39, [ADR 0045 amendment 2026-09-28 (#1684)](decisions/0045-combat-restrictions.md) Decision 59). Closes two of the "still open" items #1597 left. A fifth requirement kind, **`blocksAttacker`**, sits on the would-be blocker and names ONE attacking object — instance and epoch, carried on the `addBlockRequirement` record in the new `Mod.Objects` and on the characteristic as `BlockRequirement.Attacker` — so only blocking that object obeys it, and a creature that left and came back is not it (CR 400.7). A Lure may carry a registered blocker **filter key** (`BlockRequirement.Filter`; `wall`, `flying`, `game.RegisterBlockerFilter`), never a closure, so the characteristic stays plain comparable data (ADR 0041's ratchet); a blocker the filter does not match carries no requirement from it. Both ride the existing search unchanged in shape: each blocker → attacker arc is now weighted by `blockPairWeight`, the requirements that one pair obeys, and the witness is still counted exactly and re-checked by the ordinary validator, so a provoked creature that can't legally block the provoker owes nothing. Card side: `effects.Provoke()` (a triggered ability, not a `PrintedKeywords` token, for Exalted's reason), `BlocksAttackerUntilEOT`, `TargetBlocksTheAttacker`, `TargetBlocksThisCreature` and `FilteredLure(key)`. **Goblin Grappler**, **Grappling Hook**, **Turntimber Basilisk**, **Marble Priest**, **Talruum Piper**, **Alluring Scent**, **Bloodscent**, **Elvish Bard**, **Taunting Elf**, **Canopy Stalker**, **Watchdog** (with its "creatures attacking you get −1/−0" static) and **Nacatl War-Pride** (with its token-copy attack trigger) are new and `full`. **Still open:** blocking costs (CR 509.1d) and "blocks each attacking creature". Crested Craghorn, Lowland Tracker and Brontotherium (printed keywords plus `Provoke()`) and Hunt Down's "target creature blocks target creature" (`BlocksAttackerUntilEOT`) need no new machinery and wait only for a card batch.

- **Supertypes and controller in the leaves-the-battlefield last-known information** (#1682, CR 603.10a / 108.4, [ADR 0027 amendment 2026-09-28, #1682](decisions/0027-attack-triggers.md)). Rakdos Joins Up asked `isLegendary` of the card in the graveyard, so a Clone that died a copy of a legend was not counted; and every "a creature you control dies" clause read `dead.Controller` off the moved card, which names no one in the rules (a card in a graveyard has no controller) and is right today only because the move leaves the field alone. `EventLTB` now also carries `Event.LastKnownSupertypes` and `Event.LastKnownController`, stamped by the same `exitLKI` as the #1675 types and #1679 subtypes; `Event.WasSupertype` and `Event.LeftUnderControlOf` read them. Card side, `leftAsSupertype(ev, c, supertype)` and `leftUnderControlOf(ev, c)` are the questions: Rakdos Joins Up asks the first, and 43 "you control" / "an opponent controls" dies and leaves-the-battlefield conditions (Zulaport Cutthroat, Midnight Reaper, Massacre Wurm, Death Tyrant, The Ozolith, Scrap Trawler and the rest) ask the second; the per-turn `CreaturesDied` tally credits the stamped controller too. Owner clauses are unchanged. A stolen creature sacrificed by its thief triggers the thief's Zulaport and not the owner's. ~~**Still open:** colour as it last existed (Teysa, Orzhov Scion's "black creature").~~ **Closed by #1689.**

- **Subtypes in the leaves-the-battlefield last-known information** (#1679, CR 603.10a, [ADR 0027 amendment 2026-09-28, #1679](decisions/0027-attack-triggers.md)). The tribal dies-triggers ("whenever another Zombie you control dies") asked `HasSubtype` of the card in the graveyard, where no grant applies, so a creature that was a Zombie only under Maskwood Nexus, a changeling grant or a lord's type grant was not counted — and a Zombie an effect had made an Elk still was. `EventLTB` now also carries `Event.LastKnownSubtypes` and `Event.LastKnownAllCreatureTypes` (one flag, not ~345 subtypes), stamped by the same `exitLKI` as the #1675 card types; `Event.WasSubtype` answers with `Card.HasSubtype`'s semantics. Card side, `leftAsSubtype(ev, c, subtype)` is the one question, and every tribal dies condition asks it. **Cards:** Undead Augur, Headless Rider, Diregraf Captain, Plague Belcher, Pashalik Mons, Crossway Troublemakers, Bishop of Wings, Tegwyll, Atla Palani and Omnath, Locus of Rage — prose corrected, all already full. **Still open:** supertypes and controller as they last existed.

- **Card types in the leaves-the-battlefield last-known information** (#1675, CR 603.10a, [ADR 0027 amendment 2026-09-28, #1675](decisions/0027-attack-triggers.md)). `diedCreature(ev, g)` — the reader behind every "whenever a creature dies" card — tested `IsCreature()` on the card in the graveyard, where no continuous effect applies any more, so a crewed Vehicle, an animated manland or an animated artifact that died triggered nothing, and a creature an effect had made a noncreature still did. `EventLTB` now carries `Event.LastKnownTypes`, the leaving permanent's post-layer card types, read by the same `battlefieldExitLocked` look-back (`exitLKI`, was `combatLKI`) that stamps the #1661 combat state, so every exit route carries them with no new call site; `Event.WasType` reads them and says whether the event knows. Card side, `leftAsType(ev, c, cardType)` is the one question, and `diedCreature` plus every other dies / leaves-the-battlefield condition that tests a card type (artifact, land, artifact-or-creature, creature-you-control-left, The Ozolith, Cruel Celebrant) asks it. No new game state: the field rides the event log. **Cards:** Mahadi, Emporium Master's caveat was stale (the per-turn tally already read the CR 603.10 snapshot) and is cleared. **Still open:** subtypes and supertypes as they last existed ("whenever a Zombie you control dies" misses a granted Zombie), and controller as it last existed.

- **Miracle: reveal the first card drawn each turn and cast it for its miracle cost** (#1665, CR 702.94, [ADR 0066 amendment 2026-09-28, #1665](decisions/0066-granted-cast-and-play-permissions.md)). A card declares `AlternativeCosts: []game.AlternativeCost{Miracle("{W}")}`. That is a printed offer gated by `AlternativeCost.RequiresGrant`, and `buildDef` grows the keyword's trigger from it. The trigger watches `EventDrawCard` from the hand (`TriggeredAbility.Zones` now accepts `ZoneHand`) and applies only to the first card its owner drew this turn (`Game.DrawnThisTurn`), on any player's turn. Its `OptionalPrompt` asks "Reveal X for its miracle cost?". A "yes" reveals the card and stacks a keyed `miracle/offer` item. When that item resolves, it grants a per-object `CastPermission{Zone: hand, AltCostKey: miracle, Timing: flash}`, provided the revealed object is still in the hand (CR 702.94b). `CastPermission.ForClaim` scopes a hand permission to its own claim, so the printed cost keeps sorcery timing. The holder passing priority closes the window. **Cards:** Terminus, Thunderous Wrath, Devastation Tide, Reforge the Soul, Entreat the Angels, Banishing Stroke; Blessings of Nature's caveat cleared. **Still open:** Temporal Mastery (extra turns), Revenge of the Hunted (not built; its blocking requirement shipped in #1597), Bonfire of the Damned ("target player or planeswalker" with a sweep that follows it), and the CR 117.3b response window before the cast on another player's turn (madness has the same gap).

- **Every P/T counter kind changes power and toughness** (#1664, CR 122.1a / CR 613.4c / CR 704.5q, [ADR 0008 amendment 2026-09-28](decisions/0008-counter-mechanics.md)) — `Card.PowerForComparison` and `Card.CurrentToughness` read only `+1/+1` and `-1/-1`, so a -2/-1 (Contagion), a +1/+0 (Dwarven Armorer) or a -0/-1 (Wall of Roots) was stored, drawn as a pip, and changed nothing. `game.ParsePTCounter` is now the one parser (closed grammar `[+-]N/[+-]M`, both signs, 1–3 digits a side, so "loyalty", "1/1" and homebrew names are never stat changes) and `game.PTCounterDelta` sums a counter map through it; the two helpers read the sum, so the view, combat, the SBAs and every "its power" reader follow. The catalog's last-known P/T readers sum every kind off the event log (`b13LastKnownPTDelta`), and proliferate's automatic pick reads a P/T kind as harmful when it takes more than it gives. **Unchanged on purpose:** CR 704.5q annihilation is still +1/+1 against -1/-1 only, and every printed "+1/+1 counters" reader (Hardened Scales, Branching Evolution, Conclave Mentor) still names that one kind; Doubling Season doubles any kind, as printed. The client pip shows a P/T kind's whole name. Shipped on Contagion, Wall of Roots (the first printed mana ability whose cost adds a counter), Dwarven Armorer, Armor Thrull and Lightning Serpent, all `full`.

- **Combat state in the leaves-the-battlefield last-known information** (#1661, CR 603.10a / 506.4, [ADR 0027 amendment 2026-09-28](decisions/0027-attack-triggers.md)). A permanent leaving the battlefield is removed from combat, and the exit cleared `Card.AttackingTarget` / `BlockingTarget` before any watcher's trigger condition ran, so only the dying card's own triggers had a snapshot and that snapshot carried no combat state. `EventLTB` now carries it: `Event.AttackingTarget` (the player, planeswalker or battle it was attacking), `Event.BlockingTarget` (the attacker it was blocking) and `Event.Blocked` (it was a blocked attacker, CR 509.1h). `battlefieldExitLocked`, which every battlefield exit runs, reads them before anything clears them and hands them back for the three `EventLTB` emit sites to stamp, so combat damage, a removal spell mid-combat, a sacrifice, a bounce and the sandbox move all report the same facts; a creature removed from combat first (a control change) reports none. No new game state: the facts live on the event log, which Clone and the snapshot already carry. Card-side reads are `diedWhileAttacking(ev, g)` and `diedWhileBlocking(ev, g)`. **Cards:** Kardur, Doomscourge is now `full`; Garna, Bloodfist of Keld moved off an event-log walk that missed attackers put onto the battlefield attacking; Death Tyrant and Ares, God of War are new. **Still open, and a different shape:** "attacking or blocking ALONE" (Thijarian Witness) needs the combat's other participants, and "goaded attacking or blocking creature" (Baeloth Barrityl) waits on a static goad.

- **A divided amount read off the board, and "up to" division** (#1657, CR 601.2d, [ADR 0065 amendment 2026-09-28 (b)](decisions/0065-modal-and-multi-target-clauses.md)). `game.DivideSpec` gains `AmountKey`, a registered amount rule (`game.RegisterDivideAmount`, declared with `effects.DivideBy(rule)`), and `UpTo`, "distribute UP TO that many" (`effects.UpTo(…)`). The rule is a key, not a func, so the ADR 0041 closure ratchet gains no route; it reads the announcing player, the source, a trigger's last-known source (CR 603.10) and the claimed alternative cost, and answers in the existing data shapes (`Total`, or `FromX`), so the announced X still applies and the client never sees the rule. `bindDivideAmountsLocked` reads it ONCE, at announce — the cast (with the claimed cost), the activation, and the moment a trigger's `pick_target` walk opens — into the steps' clause copies, so the prompt, its wire projection, the enumerator and `settleDistribution` read one fixed number and a land or a life gain after that changes nothing (Ureni's and Lathiel's rulings). `UpTo` relaxes the gate's sum to "at most the amount"; each chosen target still gets at least 1. The view resolves the rule for the viewer and for each `alternative_costs[]` entry, `divide` gains `up_to`, and `activated_abilities[].legal_targets` now carries `divide` for a single-clause divided ability (Mogg Mob had shipped without it). The client's divide modal and banner say "up to", and the X prompt opens for an alternative cost with its own `{X}` (Avacyn's Judgment's madness `{X}{R}`). **Cards:** Lathiel, the Bounteous Dawn is now `full`; Ureni, the Song Unending, Orca, Siege Demon and Avacyn's Judgment are new. **Still open:** Polukranos, World Eater, which needs monstrosity (CR 701.37) and a "becomes monstrous" event before its divided X can be read; divided prevention (Remedy).

- **A mode count that reads the announced optional costs, and one that forces the higher count** (#1655, CR 601.2b / CR 603.3c / CR 700.2, [ADR 0065 amendment 2026-09-28](decisions/0065-modal-and-multi-target-clauses.md)). #1590's conditional count could only raise the maximum, off the board. `ModeSpec` now carries `RaisedMin` beside `RaisedMax`: `InsteadIf(n, cond)` is "choose both instead" with no "may", so one bullet stops being an answer; `AnyNumberIf(cond)` is "choose any number / one or more instead"; `OrUpToIf` is unchanged. The one reader is `(*Game).modeBoundsLocked` (`ModeBoundsForEffect` outside the package), which returns both bounds for a `ModeCountQuery`: the chooser, plus the optional costs announced with the choice. The cast gate validates the modes after the optional-cost choice, since CR 601.2b announces both in one step. A trigger's `mode_pick` reads the spell's own `PaidCost` while it is on the stack (a "when you cast this spell" trigger) and the permanent's `CastProvenance` after that. `game.ModeConditionOnAnnouncement` registers a key-named predicate over the query (ADR 0041: a key, not a closure); `effects.WasKicked` is the first one. For a trigger, a forced minimum asks for no more bullets than are on offer (CR 603.3c: an untargetable bullet "can't be chosen"). The hand card's `modes.min` / `modes.max` are the caster's bounds, and `modes.if_optional_paid` carries the bounds with the optional costs announced, which the client's picker switches to when a kicker is ticked. The public copy shows the printed bounds. The enumerator bounds each optional-cost announcement's mode sets by the same accessor (#544). `Register` refuses a raised minimum outside `Min..RaisedMax`. **Cards:** Inscription of Ruin, Inscription of Abundance, Depth Defiler, Prophetic Titan, Let's Play a Game and Wail of the Forgotten, all `full`. **Still open, and not this seam:** teamwork (HULK SMASH!, Go Nuts!, Atlantis Attacks, Murdock's Crusade, Widow's Bite) and blight (Pyrrhic Strike) have no optional-cost component to announce. Their mode count is one `InsteadIf` away once the cost exists.

- **A turn-scoped hexproof waiver, and "can't have" a keyword** (#1651, CR 702.11 / CR 101.2, [ADR 0038 amendment 2026-09-28 (#1651)](decisions/0038-protection-style-keywords.md)). Detection Tower's "until end of turn, your opponents and creatures your opponents control with hexproof can be the targets of spells and abilities you control as though they didn't have hexproof" is a new ADR 0041 data record, the `waiveHexproof` mod under the live `opponentsAndTheirCreatures` scope. The same two readers as the static `Spec.HexproofBypasses` check it at the targeting choke point, and only the record's controller benefits. Undo, the snapshot and cleanup treat it like every other scoped record. "Loses <keyword> and can't have <keyword>" is a strip after the layer-6 bucket. An effect records the tokens on `Characteristic.CantHave`, using `effects.LoseAndCantHave` for the static form and the `cantHaveKeywords` mod for the scoped one. The strip then removes them from the ability list, so a grant with a later timestamp cannot put them back. **Detection Tower**, **Arcane Lighthouse**, **Archetype of Endurance**, **Archetype of Imagination** and **Archetype of Finality** ship `full`. **Archetype of Aggression** and **Archetype of Courage** lose their caveats.

- **Mass "can't block / can't be blocked this turn" reads a live set** (#1650, CR 611.2c, [ADR 0045 amendment 2026-09-28 (#1650)](decisions/0045-combat-restrictions.md) Decision 54). CR 611.2c locks the affected set of an effect from a resolving spell or ability only when the effect changes characteristics or control, and a restriction does neither. So `RestrictUntilEOT`'s mass form no longer takes a `Match` snapshot. It takes a `game.AffectedScope` and registers an ADR 0041 data record whose set is a rule read until cleanup. The record reaches a creature that arrives, or loses flying, after the spell resolved. The single-`Target` form is unchanged. Live-rule restriction records are applied after the last layer (`foldRuleScopedRestrictionsLocked`), so "without flying" reads the finished characteristics. There are two new scopes, `yourCreatures` and `creaturesWithoutFlying`, and `ScopedEffectFor` refuses a `Match` over `addRestrictions`. `BoostUntilEOT` and `GrantKeywordUntilEOT` keep their snapshot, correctly. **Glaring Spotlight**, **Falter**, **Magmatic Chasm**, **Seismic Stomp**, **Cosmotronic Wave** and **Hazardous Blast** ship `full`.

- **A gated TriggerDoubler** (#1647, CR 603.2d, ADR 0071 amendment). `game.TriggerDoubler` gets its own `ActiveWhen game.Designation` field: a `TriggerDoubler` is not an ability-list entry (`TriggersForCard` / `StaticAbilitiesForCard` never see one), so the ADR 0071 gate that switches Frostcliff Siege's lines on and off cannot reach it through those accessors. `triggerDoublersLocked` checks the gate directly against the doubler's own candidate card before consulting `Applies`, so an inactive doubler (the unchosen word, or an unanswered as-enters prompt — `DesignationChosenOption` never matches an empty `Card.ChosenOption`) contributes nothing, in the engine and at the wire alike. Card side: `DoublerWhenChosen(word, doubler)`, `choose_option.go`'s `WhenChosen` / `StaticWhenChosen` one door over. `specDesignations` and its two callers (Register's door guard, `TestEveryAnchorWordGateIsOffered`) walk the new slot too, so a card that gates a doubler on a word its own prompt never offers still fails at boot. **Windcrag Siege** ships `full` with both modes — Mardu is Isshin, Two Heavens as One's `DoublesAttacking(Creature())` gated to the word, Jeskai is Legion Warboss's token shape with lifelink and haste instead of an attack requirement — closing the gap #1572's entry left. **Glacierwood Siege**, **Hollowmurk Siege** and **Monastery Siege** ship `full` alongside it: a target-reading `Targeting()` trigger plus a `GatedCastPermissions` standing graveyard land-play permission, an any-kind `EventCounterPlaced` watcher (once each turn) plus an `OncePerBatch` "whenever you attack" trigger, and a draw-step `lootOne` plus a target-reading `CostModifier` (`TargetsYouOrYourPermanent`), respectively — no new machinery for any of the three.

- **Goad remembers every goader** (#1598, CR 701.15a / 701.15c, [ADR 0045 amendment 2026-09-28 (#1598)](decisions/0045-combat-restrictions.md) Decision 53). A creature goaded by several players carries every goad. `Card.Goads` has one entry per goader, and each entry ends as its own goader's next turn begins. The stamp is `Player.TurnsBegun + 1`, the same one `UntilYourNextTurn` uses, and `sweepExpiredGoadsLocked` runs at turn start. A re-goad refreshes the goader's entry. `attackRequirementsOfLocked` adds goad's pair per entry, so CR 508.1d's counting makes the creature attack a player who goaded it neither time when it can, and any goader when every opponent goaded it. The snapshot adds `goads` beside `goadedBy`, which now holds the latest goader, and a pre-#1598 `goadedBy`-only file restores as a one-goader set. The wire adds `CardView.goaders` beside the unchanged `goaded_by`. **Alela, Cunning Conqueror** loses her last caveat and ships `full`; this retires the "one goader per creature" caveat that #1571's entry left.

- **Blocking requirements** (#1597, CR 509.1c, [ADR 0045 amendment 2026-09-28 (#1597)](decisions/0045-combat-restrictions.md) Decisions 55–58). "Blocks each combat if able", Lure's "all creatures able to block this creature do so", "must be blocked if able" and "must be blocked by exactly one creature if able" are enforced over each defending player's whole declaration, which must obey the most requirements obeyable without breaking a restriction — menace, flying and the other evasion, "can't block", block-count bounds and the whole-combat limits. `Characteristic.BlockRequirements` carries them on either side (the blocker for `blocks`, the attacker for the other three), written by layer statics and the new `addBlockRequirement` data record. `blockRequirementReachLocked` is the requirements a staged assignment obeys plus the best legal addition, searched as a min-cost flow per combination of attacker modes (a minimum is 0-or-at-least-m, not a capacity) and re-checked by the ordinary validator, so its witness is always a declaration the verb takes. The block validator refuses a declaration that lowers a pending defender's reach; the defender's pass, `finish_blocks` and `AdvanceStep` are the checkpoint. The option generator offers the witness as one `BlockOption.Required`, which the enumerator marks `AlwaysLegal` while it withholds the pass, so a declining bot blocks as required and the #328 signal never auto-completes a defender past a requirement. Refusals are `illegal_block` / `block_requirement`, and the wire adds `CardView.must_block`. **Grand Melee** loses its block-half caveat and ships `full`; **Lure**, **Prized Unicorn**, **Gaea's Protector**, **Razorgrass Screen**, **Irresistible Prey** and **Taunting Challenge** are new and `full`. **Still open:** provoke and "target creature blocks it this turn if able" (a requirement naming one attacker), predicated Lures (Marble Priest's Walls), blocking costs (CR 509.1d) and "blocks each attacking creature".

- **Computed life cost on an activated ability** (#1594, CR 601.2f–g via CR 602.2b, CR 119.4, [ADR 0020 amendment 2026-09-28](decisions/0020-activated-abilities.md), Decisions 47–48). "Pay life equal to the number of colors in your commanders' color identity" (War Room) and "Pay half your life, rounded up" (Murderous Betrayal, Lurking Evil) are now expressible. The new component is `AbilityCost.LifeFrom`, a `game.LifeCostCount`: a KEY into a registry that `game.LifeCount(key, fn)` fills at init, the same shape as `ModeCountCondition`. It is not a func, so the ADR 0041 closure ratchet gains no route, and the cost survives clone and snapshot as data. The card side is `effects.PayLifeCount(effects.LifeEqualToCommanderColors)` or `(effects.LifeHalfYoursRoundedUp)`, composed with `Plus`. `(*Game).AbilityLifeCostLocked` is the single reader, and three callers use it: the engine reads the count ONCE at announce, checks it against CR 119.4 / 119.8, charges exactly that amount and records it on `PaidCost.LifePaid`, so a commander that changes afterwards changes nothing; the legal enumerator drops an activation whose computed amount the seat cannot pay and puts that amount on `Move.Cost.Life` for the bot; and the view stamps it onto `activated_abilities[i].life_cost`, re-priced per snapshot. War Room's count is `CommanderIdentityColorCountForEffect`, the union over every commander the activator owns (partners and backgrounds), with 0 for no commander or a colourless one. **3 cards**, all `full`: War Room (caveat removed), Murderous Betrayal, Lurking Evil. **Still open:** "Pay X life" on an activated ability (Krumar Initiate, Decision 48). Its life total is a ceiling on X, which the ability enumerator's X search would have to solve, as the cast path does for Toxic Deluge. The card also needs endure. Tracker [#887](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/887).

- **Duration copy effects — "becomes a copy of X until end of turn"** (#1593, CR 707.2, CR 611.2, CR 613.1a, CR 613.7, [ADR 0043 amendment 2026-09-28 (#1593)](decisions/0043-copy-effects.md) Decisions 21-25). A copy effect with a duration and a timestamp of its own, on a permanent already on the battlefield, is an ADR 0041 `ScopedEffect` carrying one **`becomeCopy`** mod, and the mod carries the copied `PrintedValues` BY VALUE (`Mod.Copy`) — never a reference, because the copied card may be in a graveyard and may leave it. It is layer 1 by materialisation, not by a layer-pass effect: `materialiseDurationCopiesLocked` (`game/duration_copy.go`) writes the winning copy — the latest timestamp, entry copy underneath — onto the flat printed fields at the top of every recompute, when the sweep drops a record, and when one is registered, so the oracle ID every catalog hook keys on switches with the copy and switches back. A new carried field, `Card.DurationCopyBase`, is the baseline under the duration copies: the entry copy for a Clone (a Cytoshaped Clone of Grizzly Bears is Grizzly Bears again at cleanup, not Clone), the card's own values otherwise. "As this enters … until end of turn" (Cursed Mirror) lands as an entry copy before any event and is re-filed as a duration copy once the permanent has its entry stamp. Counters, damage and status are untouched (CR 707.2); a snapshot or undo carries the record and the baseline; a restore refuses a copy mod with no values or a granted bundle it does not know. Card side: `effects.BecomeCopy`, `EntersAsCopyOfUntilEndOfTurn`, `PrintedValues.AddKeyword`, `game.OwnPrintedValues`. **Shifting Woodland** and **Cursed Mirror** drop their caveats; **Mirage Mirror**, **Cytoshape**, **Mirrorweave**, **Lazav, Dimir Mastermind** and **Dimir Doppelganger** are new and `full`; **Unstable Shapeshifter** is new with one caveat (a copy of a creature that was itself a copy and left before the trigger resolved copies the card, because the engine keeps no last-known copiable values for a permanent). **Still open:** Shapesharer (a two-target clause with different specs), Mizzium Transreliquat's second ability and the other Lazavs need no new machinery and wait only for a card batch; last-known copiable values of a permanent that has left the battlefield.

- **Phyrexian symbols keep their life option under spend grants** (#1589, CR 107.4f, [ADR 0066's 2026-09-28 amendment](decisions/0066-granted-cast-and-play-permissions.md)). Under "spend mana as though it were mana of any color" (Breeches) or "mana of any type" (Hostage Taker, Gonti), `asAnyColorCost` and `asAnyTypeCost` used to fold a Phyrexian symbol into generic mana, so its "or 2 life" half was lost. The view still stamped `phyrexian_symbols` from the printed cost, so the client offered a life payment the engine refused. Both folds now keep the slot Phyrexian and mark it `ColorRequirement.AnyMana`: the life half stays, and any mana pays the mana half. Hybrid Phyrexian symbols work the same way. Every payment path asks one predicate, `ColorRequirement.Admits`, so the hand payment, the auto-tapper, the preview, the bot enumerator and `cast_prices` agree. A widened slot is paid last, so it can't take the token a narrower slot needed. `{C}` under the any-colour grant still needs colorless mana. Tracker [#887](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/887).

- **Divided damage** (#1563, CR 601.2d / CR 700.2i, [ADR 0065 amendment 2026-09-28](decisions/0065-modal-and-multi-target-clauses.md)). "N damage divided as you choose among …" is now a clause property: `TargetSpec.Divide` (`game.DivideSpec`: a fixed `Total`, or `FromX`, with `DoubleFromX` for Shatterskull Smashing's "twice X if X is 6 or more"), declared with `.Dividing(Divide(4))`, `DivideX()` or `DivideXDoublingFrom(6)`. It is data, not a func, so the ADR 0041 closure ratchet gains no route. The caster announces the split with the targets, keyed by target id, on `cast_spell`, `activate_ability` or the `pick_target` answer to `resolve_choice`. One gate, `settleDistribution`, runs after the target gate at all three points (CR 601.2d / 602.2b / 603.3d): each target at least 1, the shares summing to the amount under the announced X, never more targets than the amount, and a lone target handed the whole amount unasked. The settled map is `StackItem.Distribution`, which undo, snapshot, copy (CR 707.10) and retarget (CR 115.7c) already carried and which tests now pin. `effects.DealDividedDamage` / `PutDividedCounters` deal each surviving target exactly its share; a departed target's share is lost, not redistributed (CR 608.2b). The view ships the amount as `LegalTargetsView.divide`, the client asks for a share per target once a divided step has two or more picks, and the bot announces `game.EvenDistribution` (remainder to the earliest targets), never offering a set the gate refuses (#544). **Cards:** Fury, Dragonlord Atarka, Shatterskull Smashing and Abzan Charm are now `full`; Inferno Titan, Bogardan Hellkite, Fire Covenant and Mogg Mob are new. **Still open, and a different shape:** an amount read off the board or a paid cost rather than a constant or the announced X (Lathiel's "up to that many", Ureni, Orca, Avacyn's Judgment's madness X), and divided prevention (Remedy).

- **A conditional mode count** (#1590, CR 601.2b / CR 700.2, [ADR 0065 amendment 2026-09-27](decisions/0065-modal-and-multi-target-clauses.md)). "Choose one. If you control a commander as you cast this spell, you may choose both instead" is now `ModeSpec.RaisedMax` + `RaiseMaxIf`, declared with `ChooseOne(…).OrUpToIf(2, YouControlACommander)`. `RaiseMaxIf` is a registered key (`game.ModeCondition`), not a func, so the ADR 0041 closure ratchet gains no route. `(*Game).modeMaxLocked` (`ModeMaxForEffect` outside the package) is the one reader. It returns the raised bound while the named condition holds for the chooser and the printed `Max` otherwise; `Min` never moves. It is read once, when the choice is made: at announce for a spell, at activation for an activated ability, and as a trigger's `mode_pick` prompt is queued. The result feeds `validateModes`. Nothing re-asks it, so a commander that leaves in response does not take a chosen bullet back. The view stamps the same bound per caster on `modes.max`, so the picker offers "both" exactly when the gate accepts it; a bystander's public copy shows the printed bound. The bot enumerator widens its selections by the same accessor (#544). `Register` refuses a half-declared, non-raising or over-wide count. `YouControlACommander` registers the free-spell cycle's condition: a commander permanent the player controls, anybody's. **Cards:** Jeska's Will, Akroma's Will, Drown in Dreams, Will of the Mardu, Will of the Abzan and Flame of Anor (whose condition is `YouControlAWizard`) are now `full`. Will of the Sultai is new. The four whose bodies read `item.Targets[0]` now read each bullet's own target group through `OptionTargets`, in printed order. **Still open, and a different shape:** "choose both instead" driven by the caster's own optional cost (teamwork, kicker: HULK SMASH!, Inscription of Ruin, Depth Defiler, Pyrrhic Strike) and board conditions that FORCE the higher count (Prophetic Titan). Both need `Min` to move and, for the first, a bound that reads the announced optional costs.

- **A chosen named option as the permanent enters** (#1572, CR 614.12, [ADR 0071 amendment 2026-09-27](decisions/0071-designations-that-switch-abilities-on.md)) — "As this enchantment enters, choose Khans or Dragons. • Khans — … • Dragons — …", the Siege cycles' anchor words. `Card.ChosenOption` is the as-enters family's fifth member (after `NamedTribe`, `ChosenColor`, `ChosenPlayer` and `ChosenName`) with their lifecycle verbatim: per instance, cleared at both CR 400.7 sites, carried by clone and the snapshot, not copiable. It is written by `Game.QueueChooseOptionAsEntersForEffect`, an ordinary `option_pick` with one option per printed word, so the gate, the enumerator, the wire and the client modal needed nothing. What reads it is ADR 0071's gate, not card code: `DesignationChosenOption` (`game.ChosenOptionIs(word)`, card side `ChosenIs` / `WhenChosen` / `StaticWhenChosen`) drops the unchosen line from the four ability accessors, and an unanswered prompt matches neither word. `EventOptionChosen` bumps the layer version and is narrated as `choose_option`; `CardView.chosen_option` carries the word. Card side: `ChooseOptionAsEnters(label, options...)`. **6 cards, all `full`:** Frostcliff Siege, Palace Siege, Citadel Siege, Barrensteppe Siege, Outpost Siege, Frontier Siege. Windcrag Siege, whose Mardu line is a trigger doubler, waited on `game.TriggerDoubler` having no designation gate — closed by #1647.

- **Hexproof waivers and ward that doesn't trigger** (#1560, CR 702.11 / CR 702.21, [ADR 0038 amendment 2026-09-27](decisions/0038-protection-style-keywords.md)). "Can be the targets of spells and abilities [you control] as though they didn't have hexproof" is now `Spec.HexproofBypasses`. It is read live at the targeting choke point, and only once hexproof alone would refuse the target, so shroud and protection are untouched. The announce gate and the CR 608.2b re-check share that reader. `YoursOnly` separates Nowhere to Run's "spells and abilities" from Kaya's "you control", and a `Player` half waives a player's hexproof. "Ward abilities of those creatures don't trigger" is `Spec.WardSuppressions`. It is read by `effects.WardGranted`, the trigger condition every ward in the catalog uses, and asked about the warded creature when the ability would trigger. **Nowhere to Run** and **Kaya, Bane of the Dead** now ship `full`. Detection Tower's turn-scoped waiver is still missing. Glaring Spotlight, which also needed a live-set "can't be blocked this turn", shipped with #1650.

- **Uncopyable abilities and the as-this-transforms-into hook** (#1574, CR 707.10 / CR 712.18, [ADR 0043 amendment 2026-09-24](decisions/0043-copy-effects.md) Decision 20, [ADR 0079 amendment 2026-09-24](decisions/0079-transforming-a-permanent.md) Decision 9) — "This ability can't be copied" is `ActivatedAbility.Uncopyable`, stamped onto the stack item at activation (`StackItem.Uncopyable`, carried by Clone and the snapshot) and refused by `CopyAbilityForEffect`, the one door every ability copy comes through, so Lithoform Engine, Strionic Resonator, Rings of Brighthearth and another Gogo copy nothing. Like "can't be countered", it narrows no target clause. `Spec.AsTransformsInto` is a face's "As this permanent transforms into <face>" clause; the in-place transform verb runs it for the face now up whatever effect asked for the transform, reading `CatalogAbilityKey` so an ability removal still applies (CR 712.18), and "exile it, then return it transformed" does not run it. **Gogo, Master of Mimicry** and **Sephiroth, One-Winged Angel** now ship `full`.

- **Exile-and-cast: a face-down exile the permission holder may look at, and mana of any type** (#1573, CR 406.3 / 106.1b, [ADR 0066's 2026-09-24 amendment](decisions/0066-granted-cast-and-play-permissions.md)). There are two gaps in the "exile it and you may cast it" family.

  **1. A face-down exile the holder may look at.** `FaceDownPermitted` (`"permitted"`) is an exile-side face-down kind. Its viewer is the holder of the cast permission over the card. `GrantCastPermissionForEffect` stamps the look on such a card and on no other face-down kind, so Necropotence's `exiled` card stays unreadable. `Game.ExileTopFaceDownWithPermissionForEffect` picks the top N cards up front and routes each one through the shared exit primitive. It grants from the continuation, so a commander whose owner declines the command zone is still exiled and granted. The existing non-knower redaction handles the rest: the owner and the other seats get a card back with no `exile_play` and no cast surface.

  **2. Mana of any type.** `CastPermission.AnyType` means "mana of any type can be spent". Colorless is a type and not a color, so it folds `{C}` requirements into generic as well as the colored ones. `spendAsThoughAny` applies the fold in the one pricer, so the hand payment, the auto-tapper, the preview, the bot enumerator and `cast_prices` all agree. On the wire it appears as `exile_play.any_type`, which is set together with `any_color`.

  **Cards now `full`:** Hostage Taker, Gonti, Night Minister and Outrageous Robbery. Tracker [#885](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/885); deck tracker [#1565](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/1565).

- **Attack requirements** (#1571, CR 508.1d / 701.15b, [ADR 0045 amendment 2026-09-24 (#1571)](decisions/0045-combat-restrictions.md) Decisions 48–52). "Attacks each combat if able", "creatures your opponents control attack this turn if able" and goad's two requirements are enforced over the whole declaration, which must obey the most requirements obeyable without breaking a restriction or paying a cost. `Characteristic.AttackRequirements` carries the requirements layers write, and `Card.GoadedBy` carries goad's pair. `attackRequirementReachLocked` is the requirements a staged declaration obeys plus the best free addition, solved exactly under attack-limit capacities by a small min-cost flow. `DeclareAttackerWith` / `DeclareAttackersWith` refuse a declaration that lowers reach. The active player's pass in declare_attackers (and `AdvanceStep`) is the checkpoint, run once per combat (`Game.attacksDeclared`): it is refused while a free addition would obey another requirement, so declaring nothing is refused only when a requirement can be met. The enumerator offers only what the verbs accept, withholds the pass while it would be refused, and marks the answering attacks `AlwaysLegal`; a declining bot takes that answer. Refusals are `illegal_attack` / `attack_requirement`, and the wire adds `CardView.must_attack`. Floating requirements are ADR 0041 data: the `addAttackRequirement` mod, with `ScopedEffect.Scope` `opponentsCreatures` for CR 611.2c's live set. **Bident of Thassa** and **The Akroan War** ship `full`; **Alela, Cunning Conqueror**'s goad is enforced, with one caveat left (one goader per creature).

- **Target-set legality** (#1559, CR 601.2c / 608.2b, [ADR 0019 amendment 2026-09-24](decisions/0019-structured-targeting.md)) — a rule over the chosen SET of a clause's targets, which `TargetSpec.CardOK` judging each candidate alone could never say. `TargetSpec.Different *TargetDifference{Label, Key}` is a pairwise-distinct key (`game/target_set.go`), built with `effects.EachDifferentManaValue()`, `EachDifferentController()` and `EachDifferentName()` and attached with `.EachDifferent(…)`. **Announce** refuses a set with a repeated key as `ErrIllegalTarget` carrying the printed rule; **resolution** (CR 608.2b) re-judges the survivors — a pick that left is dropped alone, and two survivors that now share a key are both illegal (the weaker reading; neither is preferred); **fillability** (CR 603.3d, the cast offer) counts keys rather than candidates; a **retarget** (CR 115.7c) may not move a slot onto a key another slot holds. The **enumerator** prunes colliding sets inside its expansion, cast and `pick_target` alike, and the **wire** ships `legal_targets.different {label, keys}` so the client picker greys and refuses a colliding candidate and says the rule in its banner. The same PR added `TargetSpec.ManaValueAtMostX` — "with mana value X or less", bound to the announced X at cast and again from `StackItem.XValue` at resolution, shipped with `mana_values` for the client to narrow by — because Agadeem's Awakening prints both halves. **Cards:** Agadeem's Awakening and Behold the Sinister Six! are new and `full`; Run Away Together drops its caveat and is `full`; Eerie Ultimatum and Windgrace's Judgment each lose their resolution-time dedup and keep one narrower caveat (untargeted-in-print, and the per-opponent binding, respectively). **Triaged out:** Molten Primordial's "for each opponent, up to one target creature that player controls" is one clause per opponent binding its pick to that player, built with the existing `TriggeredAbility.TargetsFrom`; it ships `full` in the same PR. **Still open:** a set rule on a COST (Transmutation Font's sacrifice of differently named tokens), on the set-of-sacrificed-permanents row.

- **Conditional and per-player combat limits** (#1534, CR 508.1c / 509.1b, [ADR 0045 amendment 2026-09-24 (#1534)](decisions/0045-combat-restrictions.md) Decision 47). These are the three variants #1507 left for their cards, each one field read by the existing check. **`game.AttackLimit.While`** is a condition gate: `activeAttackLimitsLocked` skips a limit whose condition is false, read live, so a condition that turns true after the declaration unmakes nothing. **`AttackLimitAttackingThis`** is a third scope that counts only attacks naming the limit's own source permanent; its controller and their other planeswalkers stay open. **`BlockRule.LimitPerDefender`** makes `blockLimitRefusalLocked` group the counted blockers by controller and judge each defending player on their own. Mirri's trigger registers the per-defender rule through `effects.EachOpponentCantBlockWithMoreThanN` into `TurnScopedBlockRules`, with no snapshot, so a creature an opponent flashes in later is bound too. "This combat" is the turn's combat until extra combats exist (#753). The enumerator, the block option generator and the #328 signal run the same checks, so they needed no change. The constructors are `effects.AsLongAs` + `ThisIsTapped` and `NoMoreThanNCanAttackThisEachCombat`. **Mirri, Weatherlight Duelist** ships `full`. **The Eternal Wanderer** ships with one caveat, on its +1: the exiled card returns at the next end step rather than its owner's, because a delayed trigger cannot wait for a third player's turn. That gap is a `DelayedTrigger` field, not a combat seam.

- **Split second read from the card** (#1519, CR 702.61 / 702.61b / 708.4 / 707.2, [ADR 0007 amendment 2026-09-24](decisions/0007-stack-foundation.md) Decisions 11–12) — the rule was built in S13.1 and every consumer asked `Game.SplitSecondActive`, but the only writer was the sandbox `split_second` cast flag no client sends, so a Krosan Grip could be answered. Split second is now a `canonicalKeywords` token: the deck importer keeps Scryfall's "Split second", and `castHasSplitSecond` (`game/split_second.go`) stamps `StackItem.SplitSecond` at announce from `HasKeyword` on the announce copy, ORed with the flag (one writer) and refused for a face-down cast. `CastTimingOpenLocked` and `ActivationTimingOpenLocked` refuse under split second, so `castable_here` and an instant-speed row's `timing_closed` agree with the enumerator; mana abilities, triggers, foretell and turning face up still work. Krosan Grip ships `full`; Sudden Shock, Sudden Death, Sudden Edict and Sudden Spoiling join the catalog. Angel's Grace (#749) declares the keyword and drops its split-second caveat.
