The importer confirms the token against a bare keyword line (`narrowVariantKeywords`), so The Fearsome Flock's "split second level up" is not stamped on the creature.

- **Combat-wide attack and block limits** (#1507, CR 508.1c / 509.1b, [ADR 0045 amendment 2026-09-24 (#1507)](decisions/0045-combat-restrictions.md) Decisions 43–45) — "no more than N creatures can attack / block each combat" and "no more than N creatures can attack you each combat". **Block half:** `game.BlockRule.Limit`, the whole-combat bound Decision 12 reserved, is a third set check in `checkBlockDeclarationLocked` (`blockLimitRefusalLocked`, `game/block_rules.go`), counted over every block stored in the combat, whichever attacker or defender, and refused as `illegal_block` / `declaration_limit` (the token Decision 8 reserved) with the printing card named; the option generator, the enumerator and the #328 signal stop offering a block once it is used, because they all run the validator. **Attack half:** `game.AttackLimit{Scope, Max}` (`game/attack_limits.go`), catalog-contributed through `CardDef.AttackLimits` / `CatalogAttackLimits` keyed by `CatalogAbilityKey`, with a structural "you" (`AttackLimitAttackingYou`, the zero value — the player, not their planeswalkers) or `AttackLimitEachCombat`. One check, `attackLimitRefusalLocked`, is run by `DeclareAttackerWith`, by `DeclareAttackersWith` (all or nothing, like the attack tax) and by `legal/combat.go` for every candidate move, before the CR 508.1a tax is priced; refused as the new `illegal_attack` / `attack_limit` frame. Both halves refuse only a declaration that RAISES the count, so a limit arriving late unmakes nothing. `effects.NoMoreThanNCanBlockEachCombat`, `NoMoreThanNCanAttackEachCombat` and `NoMoreThanNCanAttackYouEachCombat` are the constructors. **Silent Arbiter**, **Dueling Grounds**, **Crawlspace** and **Judoon Enforcers** ship `full`; **Caverns of Despair** ships with only the world-rule caveat. Mirri, Weatherlight Duelist's conditional / per-opponent variants and The Eternal Wanderer's per-planeswalker scope were built later with their cards (#1534).

- **An ability copy's new targets are judged against its departed source as it last existed** (#1449, CR 707.10c / 115.7c / 608.2h / 400.7 / 702.16b, [ADR 0072 amendment 2026-09-24 (#1449)](decisions/0072-protection.md)) — the "still open" clause of the #1429 entry below. The CR 707.10c "you may choose new targets for the copy" prompt judged the new targets against `abilitySourceCardLocked`, the source's current-zone card. Once the source had left, that was its graveyard card, so the copy of a painted-red source's ability could be pointed past protection from red, and the copy of a bleached red source's ability could not take a pro-red target it was allowed. `copyTargetSourceLocked` (`game/spell_copy.go`) is now the `TargetSource` for the offer, the #809 refresh of the open prompt (`game/trigger_target_timing.go`) and the answer. For an ability whose source `departedAbilitySourceLocked` finds a record for, it snapshots that record, which is the read #1429 gave the original, from the `SourceObject` the copy carries (#1418). Spell copies and live sources are unchanged. Also fixed: the refresh withdrew an ability copy's emptied prompt through the spell builder, which put a spell copy of the source permanent's card on the stack. It now uses `createCopyLocked`. No caveat named the gap. The proof is #1429's Murderous Redcap turned blue by Cerulean Wisps and killed, then copied with Strionic Resonator: the copy may now take a pro-red creature and may no longer take a pro-blue one. Trackers [#881](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/881), [#883](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/883).

- **The primitives that act on "this" ask for the object, not the card** (#1432, CR 400.7 / 603.7c / 611.2b / 701.12b, [ADR 0018 amendment 2026-09-24](decisions/0018-triggers-on-the-stack.md) Decisions 17-19; the follow-on to #1418, entry below). About a hundred catalog files resolve with `Target: item.SourceCardID` (`AddCounter` 64 literals, `SacrificePermanent` 13, `UntapTarget` 12, the until-EOT pumps 9, bounce, exile, transform, crew's zero target) or call a game mutator on the source directly, and a source that left and came back under the same instance ID while its ability waited was acted on as the new object: stronger than printed. **One check, at the primitive:** `Game.AbilitySourceIsNewObjectForEffect(item)` / `effects.Context.isNewSourceObject(target)` (`effects/source_object_guard.go`) — the target is the item's own source, the item is a stamped ability, and the card is on the battlefield as an object other than `StackItem.SourceObject` that this resolution did not put there. Every permanent-acting primitive asks it (the list is in Decision 17), as do `b09SourceStillOnBattlefield`, the "for as long as ~" duration builders, fight, the ~20 direct-mutator bodies, and the engine's evoke sacrifice trigger. **The card-following rule** (Decision 18): off the battlefield the primitive follows the card ("when this dies, return it", "return this card from your graveyard"), and a move this resolution made itself is followed (CR 400.7: unearth's haste; `resolvingItem.sourceEpoch` tells the two apart). Delayed and reflexive triggers follow a source their creator put onto the battlefield (`followedSourceObjectLocked`, Decision 19), so unearth's end-step exile still finds its creature. Two regression scans fail a new primitive, or a new direct-mutator body, that does not ask. Proof cards: **Bartolomé del Presidio**, **Underworld Breach**, **Devoted Druid**, evoked **Mulldrifter**, and **Krenko, Tin Street Kingpin**, which ships `full` (last-known-power Goblins, no counter on a Krenko that is not the attacker). The "each creature you control" loop that reached its own returned source and skipped it (weaker than printed) is closed by #1463 (Decision 20): a loop over a set read off the board applies with `ctx.asGroupMember()`, sites that name the source by construction ask `isNewSourceObjectAsThis`, and a third scan fails an "each" loop that forgets; proof cards Steel Overseer, Mazirek, Kalonian Hydra. Still open, stated in the amendment: hand-built durations; damage to "this". Trackers [#881](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/881).

- **The CR 608.2b target re-check for an ability whose source has left** (#1429, CR 608.2b / 608.2h / 400.7 / 702.16b, [ADR 0072 amendment 2026-09-24 (#1429)](decisions/0072-protection.md), [ADR 0018 note 2026-09-24](decisions/0018-triggers-on-the-stack.md)) — the "still open" clause of the #1417 entry below. `stackItemSourceLocked` (`game/targets.go`) is the `TargetSource` the resolution re-check and a retarget are judged under. For an ability whose source had left the battlefield it looked the source up in its current zone and got the graveyard card, a new object with printed colours. Its comment said the value was source-less, which was wrong. So protection was tested against the wrong object: a painted-red source that died kept a pro-red target, and a bleached red source that died lost one and fizzled. It now returns a snapshot of the source's `lastKnownPermanents` record, found by `departedAbilitySourceLocked` from the item's `SourceObject` (#1418): the live object while it is on the battlefield, its record once it has left, and the card where it is for an object that was never a permanent. So a graveyard trigger from a card that died earlier this turn is judged as the graveyard card, and an activation that sacrificed its own source is judged as that source even after the card comes back. Unstamped items from pre-#1418 snapshots keep ADR 0056 Decision 10's instance-ID rule. Spells never consult the record, and an ability with no record keeps the lookup. No caveat named the gap. The proof is #1417's Murderous Redcap turned blue by Cerulean Wisps and then killed, with Mother of Runes giving its target protection in response: protection from red no longer fizzles the trigger, and protection from blue now does. ~~Still open: an ability copy's re-target prompt (`game/ability_copy.go`, `abilitySourceCardLocked`) still reads the current-zone card (#1449)~~ — **closed by #1449**, the entry above. Trackers [#881](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/881), [#883](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/883).

- **Variable-count tap-others costs** (#1421, CR 107.3 / 118.3 / 602.2b, [ADR 0073 amendment 2026-09-24](decisions/0073-optional-additional-costs-and-the-cast-gate.md) Decisions 8–10) — `TapOthersCost.Filter.CountFromX` makes "Tap X untapped … you control" an announce-time cost: the shared bounds helper turns X into the exact payment width, `AbilityCost.DemandsX` exposes the prompt even without `{X}` mana, and the validator refuses a different number of `tap_ids` before anything taps. The wire reuses `LegalTargetsView.count_from_x`; the client picker supplies both the IDs and their count as `x_value`; bots offer the first three useful nested payments. Registration refuses a second claimant on the same X and refuses the shape on a mana ability, which has no X announcement (CR 605.3b). **Secluded Starforge** and **Apothecary White** now ship `full` as the two proof cards.

- **The block declaration's completion point, and "attacks and isn't blocked"** (#1279, CR 509.1 / 509.2 / 509.3 / 117.3a, [ADR 0045 amendment 2026-09-24 (#1279)](decisions/0045-combat-restrictions.md) Decisions 38-39) — the gap #1227 inherited and declared on `UnblockedAttackerForEffect`: nothing marked a defender's CR 509.1 declaration DONE, so "has not declared yet" and "declared no blocks" were the same empty record. `Game.blocksDeclared` (per combat, carried by undo and the snapshot) is written by `completeBlockDeclarationLocked` at four points — the step's start for a defender with no legal block, `finish_blocks` (new, player-scoped, not priority-gated), the defender's own `pass_priority` in the step, and the step ending for anyone still pending — and completing announces: that defender's staged blocks lock in (the #830 lock-in now waits for its blocker's controller to finish) and then **`EventBlockersDeclared`** (Actor = the defender, Amount = blockers, 0 = "declared, none"). The last completion by pass or `finish_blocks` hands the ACTIVE player priority, so the attacker gets its post-block window instead of the defender's pass ending the step. Consumers moved onto it: `UnblockedAttackerForEffect` (ninjutsu waits for the defender), the #328 `block_decision_seats` signal and the enumerator's block moves (a declared defender is asked nothing more), the bot's block grace (reads the signal off the frame), and the client (a "Done blocking" / "No blocks" control on `block_pending_seats`). Wire: `block_pending_seats` / `blocks_declared_seats`, action `finish_blocks`. `effects.WhenAttacksAndIsNotBlocked` is the new trigger shape. **4 cards, all `full`:** Swamp Mosquito, Guiltfeeder, Abyssal Nightstalker, Eternal of Harsh Truths. **Still open, by choice:** a late block by hand after completion is still accepted (sandbox allowance), and the active player still receives priority on the step's entry before anyone has declared.

- **Emblems that change when you can activate** (#1275, CR 114.3 / 602.5d / 606.3 / 101.1, [ADR 0066 amendment 2026-09-24](decisions/0066-granted-cast-and-play-permissions.md), [ADR 0064 note 2026-09-24](decisions/0064-emblems.md)) — #1208's per-player activation timing had one home, derived from the BATTLEFIELD, and an emblem lives in the command zone, so "you may activate loyalty abilities of planeswalkers you control on any player's turn any time you could cast an instant" had nowhere to be said. It is now the derived home one zone over, and nothing is stored: an emblem leaves only with its owner (CR 800.4a), so its presence is the duration. **Two lines, both the ones the issue named.** `effects.EmblemSpec.ActivationTimings` is the `Spec.ActivationTimings` slot on an emblem, projected by `buildEmblemDef` into the emblem's `CardDef` and checked at boot by the same guard (`checkActivationTimings`, now shared by both homes). `Game.activationTimingVerdictLocked` (`game/activation_timing.go`) walks every seat's `Player.Emblems` after the battlefield — the fifth emblem walk, beside the layer pass's, the trigger harvest's and the two turn-based-action gathers. `Covers` receives the emblem as `q.Source`, so "you" is its owner. **No reader changed**: the engine (`ActivateCatalogAbility`), the bot enumerator, the view's `timing_closed` and the sandbox `ActivateLoyalty` verb all read `ActivationTimingOpenLocked` already, which is the test that they did not have to learn the walk. CR 606.3's other half is untouched: the emblem opens the window and each planeswalker still activates one loyalty ability per turn. **Constructor:** `effects.LoyaltyAbilitiesOfYourPlaneswalkersAtInstantSpeed` — Teferi, Master of Time's clause with the self-reference widened to a control test. **Cards:** Teferi, Temporal Archmage ships `full` (its +1 is `LookAtTopThenTakeOneRestOnBottom(2)`, which #952 had built in the meantime; its −1 untaps up to four target permanents). Teferi's Talent ships `caveats`: Enchant planeswalker (new `EnchantPlaneswalker`) and the draw trigger work, and the granted −12 is the "Abilities granted to other permanents" row above, not this one. Trackers [#883](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/883), [#885](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/885).

- **Tap N untapped permanents you control as an ability cost** (#758, CR 118.3 / 302.6 / 602.2b) — one fixed-count `game.TapOthersCost` with a count, non-targeting `TargetSpec` filter, printed-word `ExcludeSource` and prompt label, shared by `AbilityCost` and `ManaAbilityShape`. Both activation paths validate every named permanent before paying anything, refuse duplicates, tapped or uncontrolled permanents and a source already spent by `{T}`, then emit one tap event per payment. Both wire views expose `tap_others_label` / `tap_others_options`; both actions accept `tap_ids`; the legal enumerator expands the same candidate walk and the heuristic prices the lost attack/block; the client reuses the sacrifice modal with the verb "Tap". The auto-tapper excludes named payers and never plans a mana source whose own activation needs this choice. **Cards:** the station family and The Shire already exercised the ordinary activated path; Scene of the Crime, Survivors' Encampment, Springleaf Drum, Jaspera Sentinel, Heritage Druid, Relic of Legends and Holdout Settlement exercise the mana path and are `full`. The variable-count follow-up is closed separately above; fixed-count catalog adoption continues in the owning batch issues rather than reopening this engine seam.

- **Conditional blocking restrictions** (#750, CR 509.1b / 702.111b, [ADR 0045 addendum](decisions/0045-combat-restrictions.md) Decisions 11–13 and its amendment of 2026-09-24, Decisions 40–42) — the card half. The engine half (slot 4 of `Game.BlockPairRefusalLocked`, `blockerBoundsLocked`, `DeclareBlockers` refusing an illegal count, `CatalogBlockRules` and `Game.TurnScopedBlockRules`) had no way for a card to declare a rule. `effects.Spec.BlockRules` and a token template's `BlockRules` slot now feed `CardDef.BlockRules`, which carddef.go publishes through `CatalogBlockRules`, keyed by `CatalogAbilityKey` so a permanent that loses its abilities imposes nothing. `effects/block_rules.go` pairs a SCOPE (`OnSelf`, `OnAttached`, `ControlledBySourceController`, `OnMatching`, `PowerLessThanSource`) with a rule (`CantBeBlockedExceptBy`, `CantBeBlockedBy`, `CantBeBlockedWhile`, `CantBlockAttackers`, `CantBlockOrBeBlockedBy`, `MaxBlockers`, `MinBlockers`), and `BlockRuleUntilEOT` registers any of them for a snapshotted set until end of turn. **Prowler's Helm**, **Legolas Greenleaf**, **Champion of Lambholt**, **Gingerbrute**, **Thieves' Tools**, **Vorrac Battlehorns** and **Rampaging Ceratops** ship `full`; **Departed Deckhand** and **Canopy Cover** lose their block-rule caveats and ship `full`; **Avatar Kuruk**'s Spirit token carries "can't block or be blocked by non-Spirit creatures" and the card keeps only its extra-turn caveat (#753). Still open, split out as #1507: `BlockRule.Limit` and the attack-side twin (Silent Arbiter, Crawlspace).

- **Winning the game by an effect, and "can't lose" / "can't win"** (#749, CR 104.2b / 104.3 / 104.4a / 614.17, [ADR 0057](decisions/0057-win-and-lose-by-effect.md) and its 2026-09-24 amendment) — the engine names a result. `game/game_end.go` gives every departure a `LossCause` (`life`, `empty_draw`, `poison`, `commander_damage`, `effect`, `concede`), splits elimination into `loseGameLocked` (reads the gate) / `leaveGameLocked` (CR 800.4a, unchanged) / `checkGameOverLocked` (the last player standing wins, CR 104.2a, no gate read; everyone gone is a draw, CR 104.4a), and records `Game.Outcome` (`{win|draw, winner, last_standing|effect|all_lost, source}`) through one writer, `endGameLocked`, which also clears every prompt. **`WinTheGameForEffect`** is immediate (the Jace ruling) and eliminates nobody — a four-seat game won by Felidar Sovereign ends with three seats standing; a prevented win emits `EventWinPrevented` once. **`LoseTheGameForEffect`** is now immediate too (CR 104.3e has no SBA clause); an active player who loses mid-resolution leaves at once and the rotation waits for the next SBA pass (`Game.ActiveSeatLeftPending`, ADR 0059 Decision 6). `effects.WinTheGame` / `effects.LoseTheGame` return `game.ErrStopResolution` when the resolution must stop, which `EmitEvent` drops rather than logging as an effect error. The **SBA loss pass** is collect-then-apply (CR 704.3, CR 104.3f) and consumes the CR 704.5b empty-draw flag on every pass, gated or not. **Gates** (`game/game_end_gates.go`): derived from the battlefield through `Spec.GameEndGates` → `CatalogGameEndGates` keyed by `CatalogAbilityKey` (Platinum Angel, Herald of Eternal Dawn, Abyssal Persecutor), and granted for a CR 611.2 duration as a fifth `PlayerStatic` payload (Angel's Grace) — the amendment's change from the ADR's separate registry. Concession is never gated. **Wire:** `GameView.outcome`, `PlayerView.cant_lose` / `cant_win` / `end_gates`, log `cause` plus `game_over` and `win_prevented` lines, and one line per concession. **Client:** the banner reads the winner and cause from `outcome` (survivors only as a fallback), and a seat shows `CAN'T LOSE` / `CAN'T WIN` badges. **Room:** once a game has ended only the admin can undo. **Bots:** a seat that can't lose to its life total is never a lethal target, never hopeless, and its danger penalty is clamped at 0. **7 cards:** Herald of Eternal Dawn (#1306), Platinum Angel, Abyssal Persecutor, Felidar Sovereign, Laboratory Maniac and Thassa's Oracle complete; Angel's Grace with one declared caveat (its life floor is a separate damage-result replacement; its second, split second, was cleared by #1519). **Still open, each its own seam:** a "would lose the game" replacement (The Golden Throne), granting a non-keyword static (Cloudsteel Kirin, The Book of Exalted Deeds), emblem gates (Gideon of the Trials), a `Causes`-narrowed gate's first card (Phyrexian Unlife, Pact Weapon), the `outcome` column on ADR 0051's `games` row (#1520 — the user database shipped before this, so it is now a migration; `winner_seat` is written from the outcome already), and the rest of the ADR's first wave, which no longer wait on this seam and are checked for other blockers in their own card PRs (Test of Endurance, Revel in Riches, Simic Ascendancy, Helix Pinnacle, Jace, Wielder of Mysteries, Strixhaven Stadium and the others ADR 0057's question 3 lists). Tracker [#883](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/883).

- **Infect, wither and toxic** (#748, CR 702.90 / 702.80 / 702.164 / 120.3b / 120.3d / 120.3g, [ADR 0056](decisions/0056-infect-wither-toxic.md) PR 2) — the damage tail (`game/damage_tail.go`) now gives damage its CR 120.3 result. The tail snapshots `result` (infect, wither, the CR 702.164b toxic total) and `controller` beside deathtouch and lifelink, through one reader (`damageSourceTraitsOfCard`, and `damageSourceTraitsOfAbilities` over a departed object's #1396 record); a spell on the stack is read too (CR 702.80c / 702.90e — Puncture Blast), and the CR 510.1c frame caches the three as `SourceInfect` / `SourceWither` / `SourceToxic`. Damage to a creature from an infect or wither source is -1/-1 counters instead of marked damage; infect damage to a player is poison instead of life loss; toxic adds its total in poison on combat damage to a player; infect plus toxic is ONE placement. The counters are a RESULT, placed through the CR 614 counter window as a separate event (`placeDamageResultCountersLocked`) by the source's controller (`CounterPlacer`) and flagged `CounterFromCombatDamage` in combat, so Doubling Season leaves combat counters alone and a Vizier-of-Remedies-shaped replacement sees them; the damage tail does not wait on that window. `infect`, `wither` and the `toxic` family joined `canonicalKeywords`, so the ~30 keyword-only infect / wither / toxic creatures import fully automated; toxic is minted as `toxic N` from the oracle line, and the catalog's keyword grants append through `game.AppendKeywordAbility`, which keeps every toxic instance. Shipped on Plague Myr, Blighted Agent, Ichor Rats, Puncture Blast, Tainted Strike, Triumph of the Hordes, Karumonix, the Rat King and Bloated Contaminator. Still to come under #748, and not rules seams: ADR 0056 PR 3 (the "as poison" / "as -1/-1 counters" log suffixes, a poison log line, the `N/10` chip, one `TOX N` badge) and PR 4 (the bots valuing poison). Grafted Exoskeleton, once listed here, waits on a "whenever this becomes unattached" trigger, not on this seam.

- **Prowess, and a name for keyword triggers** (#706 and #1258, CR 702.108 / 707.10 / 400.7 / 603.3b, [ADR 0014 amendment 2026-09-24](decisions/0014-combat-keywords.md)) — prowess is a `canonicalKeywords` token and the table's first TRIGGERED keyword. `TriggersForCard` (`game/designations.go`) asks `keywordTriggersFor` (`game/prowess.go`) for one trigger per prowess token on the effective ability list, so a printed prowess stamped by the deck importer (no catalog entry), a token's `Keywords` and a layer-6 grant all fire, a "loses all abilities" silences it, and a face-down creature has none. It watches `EventCast` for a noncreature spell its controller cast, so copies never trigger it (CR 707.10), and resolves as a layer-7c +1/+1 until end of turn pinned to the object after the #1432 new-object check. It is cumulative in `AppendKeywordAbility` beside toxic (CR 702.108b). `game.TriggeredAbility.Keyword` names a keyword trigger (`Cascade()`, `GrantsCascade()`, `Storm()`, prowess), and `cards/coverage` has exact `cascade`, `storm` and `prowess` probes. The bot prices prowess per instance. Proof cards: Ty Lee, Chi Blocker (to full, deck #1306), Monastery Mentor, Sokka, Tenacious Tactician (grant; batch 49's #754 skip), Elemental Eruption (token + storm copies; batch 53), Cori-Steel Cutter (batch 40's prowess skip). Still weaker than printed: a card that prints "Prowess, prowess" imports with one instance, because Scryfall's keyword array is a set.

- **A triggered ability names its source OBJECT, not only its card** (#1418, CR 400.7 / 113.7a / 603.7d / 603.12, [ADR 0018 amendment 2026-09-24](decisions/0018-triggers-on-the-stack.md) Decisions 14-16). A triggered item carried `SourceCardID` and nothing else about its source. `StackItem.SourceEpoch` was stamped by the two activation paths only. So "this" in a trigger whose event was not about its source (an upkeep, draw-step or cast trigger) read the object the card was NOW, and a source flickered in response was judged as the new object. **`StackItem.SourceObject ObjectRef`** is now stamped on every ability item where it is made: the trigger dispatch after `Build` (`stampTriggerSource`, from the dispatch's value copy of the source, or from the event's own snapshot when the event is about the source, so a dies trigger names the permanent that died), `queueHarvestedTriggerLocked` for any item without one, both activation paths (read before costs, so a source sacrificed to its own ability is still named), delayed triggers (`DelayedTrigger.SourceObject`, inherited from the resolving creator, CR 603.7d), reflexive triggers (the parent's, CR 603.12) and ability copies. Clone and the snapshot carry it (`carried` in `snapshot_drift_test.go`). `Game.SourceObjectForEffect` is the read, and `effects.Context.SourceRef()` / `SourcePermanent()` are the card-side spellings. `SourcePermanent()` is `PermanentForEffect` on that ref: live while the object is there, its record once it has gone. `AbilitySourceGoneForEffect` now asks the object of every stamped item, triggers included. An unstamped item from an older snapshot keeps its old rule. Proof cards: **Mana Vault** ships `full` (its last caveat was this issue); **Batterskull** (living weapon), **Hero's Blade** and **Uthros Research Craft** no longer attach or counter a new object that their card became while the trigger waited. Still open, stated in the amendment: a spell item carries no source object (nothing asks yet). Trackers [#881](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/881).

- **A departed damage source's colour, type and controller for protection and "a red source you control"** (#1417, CR 608.2h / 400.7 / 702.16e, [ADR 0056 amendment 2026-09-24 (follow-up)](decisions/0056-infect-wither-toxic.md) Decision 12, [ADR 0072 amendment 2026-09-24](decisions/0072-protection.md)) — the last named piece of the #1396 entry below. The damage event's `SourceLKI`, which CR 702.16e protection reads, was snapshotted from the source's current zone, so a creature that had left before its damage event (a dies trigger, a creature killed in response to its own ability) was judged by its graveyard card's printed colour. `effectDamageTailLocked` (`game/damage_tail.go`) now takes it from the same `lastKnownPermanents` record `departedDamageSourceLocked` already returns for lifelink and deathtouch, by object when a ref is given and by the "current epoch = recorded + 1" rule for an instance ID. Spells on the stack, live permanents and cards that have moved on keep the current-zone read (#662). Torbran, Thane of Red Fell, Ojer Axonil, Deepest Might and Mechanized Warfare read the same snapshot through the new `effects.damageSourceCharacteristics` instead of looking the card up. No caveat named the gap. The proof is Murderous Redcap turned blue by Cerulean Wisps and then killed: protection from blue prevents its damage, and none of the three red-source cards raise it. ~~Still open: the CR 608.2b target re-check reads a departed ability source's graveyard card (#1429)~~ — **closed by #1429**, the entry above. **#1430 closed the other half**, [ADR 0056 amendment 2026-09-24 (follow-up 2)](decisions/0056-infect-wither-toxic.md) Decision 13: the four catalog damage replacements that test only the source's controller or type (Angrath's Marauders and its three siblings, Fated Firepower, Gratuitous Violence, Inquisitor's Flail) now read `effects.damageSourceCharacteristics` too, instead of a fresh `LookupCardForEffect` at apply time. Proved directly against each registered `Spec`'s `AppliesTo` closure (`departed_source_matcher_test.go`), the same technique `TestOjerAxonilLeavesCombatDamageAlone` uses. Still open: `EventDealDamage` **triggers** still look the source up — `game.Event` carries no snapshot at all, so that half needs a `SourceLKI` on the event first (#1430). Trackers [#881](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/881), [#883](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/883).

- **A commander moved to pay a COST is offered CR 903.9** (#1397, CR 903.9 / 601.2h / 602.2b / 605.3a, [ADR 0013 §5af](decisions/0013-replacement-effects.md#5af-amendment-2026-09-24-a-commander-paid-as-a-cost-is-asked-before-the-payment-not-during-it)) — not a catalog seam but an engine gap every cost component shared, in two opposite directions. The discard, return-to-hand and exile payers (Thrill of Possibility, cycling, ninjutsu, Quirion Ranger, Cadaverous Bloom, Grim Lavamancer, scavenge, the Spirit Guides) settled with `MustSettleNow` and never asked the owner; the sacrifice and alternative-cost payers (Viscera Seer, Ashnod's Altar, Village Rites, a Force of Will pitch, escape) paused half way through the payment, leaving the commander spendable a second time. **Now every announcement asks first**: `ActivateCatalogAbility`, `CastSpell` and `ActivateManaAbility` pass `askCostCommanderLocked` (`game/cost_commander_choice.go`) after validating and before paying; a commander among the cards the payment would move parks the announcement on the existing `optional_replacement` prompt, addressed to its OWNER (an opponent, for a stolen commander), and the answer re-makes the announcement with the answer carried onto each move (`zoneRoute.commanderAnswer` → `ReplacementEvent.commanderAnswer`, read by the gather). The payment still settles in one step. No wire change; the enumerator and bots answer the prompt as any other yes/no. **#1445** adds the other half. A card whose exit an EFFECT has paused (a destroyed commander waiting on its owner) is refused by every one of those announcements with `ErrChoicePending` (`refusePausedCostCardsLocked`), and the auto-tapper neither plans nor spends it (ADR 0013 §5af addendum). **#1427** extends that refusal to a cost that only TAPS the card — its own `{T}`, crew, tap-another, convoke and waterbend — through the same gate (second addendum). **#1474** closes the last two uses: casting or playing the paused card itself (CR 601.2a moves it before any cost, so it was in no cost list), and activating any ability of it or naming it as a counter-removal source. Both go through the same gate, and `castable_here` agrees (third addendum). Not closed: the auto-tapper's own cost moves (no commander carries a self-costed mana ability), and a CR 616 ordering between two OTHER replacements on a sacrificed or alternative-cost card, which can still pause those two payers ([#1420](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/1420)).

- **Effect-reachable RNG / die roll** (#744, [ADR 0054](decisions/0054-dice-rolls-and-coin-flips.md)) — closed on the 2026-09-24 re-triage (#1386): the row was fully stale. Its text, as it stood:

    > **What was missing:** **Core delivered for #744** ([ADR 0054](decisions/0054-dice-rolls-and-coin-flips.md)): keyed, rewindable `RollDiceForEffect`, `FlipCoinsForEffect`, `ChooseAtRandomForEffect`, and resumable called flips with heads/tails/stop; per-instruction public logs, client cues, legal moves and bot policy. Stable source ordinals survive clone and snapshot. Eight representative cards are registered; Urza's Bauble now uses the keyed stream. Remaining work is the additional card wave and its card-specific dependencies, not the basic random-effect APIs.
    >
    > **Cards waiting:** Remaining: Wyll's Reversal (spell retargeting); Clown Car (ETB X provenance); Ancient Silver Dragon (permanent hand-size grant); Goblin Archaeologist; Fiery Gambit; Game of Chaos (continuation and life-change ordering); Yusri (temporary free-cast permission)

- **Layer invalidation on hand / life / attack / graveyard / spells-cast state** (#74, #1117, #1218, #1325) — closed on the 2026-09-24 re-triage (#1386): the row was fully stale. Its text, as it stood:

    > **What was missing:** **Hand, life and graveyard delivered** — hand size with #74, life total and graveyard contents with #1117. `StaticAbility.DependsOnLifeTotal` is `DependsOnHandSize`'s sibling: an opt-in invalidation hint, gated on the same battlefield walk, so a table holding nothing that reads a life total pays nothing for the many life changes a game of Commander makes. Its bump rides the WRITE to a life total (`Game.invalidateLayersForLifeChangeLocked`, three call sites) rather than an event, because damage to a player changes a life total without emitting `EventChangeLife` at all and emits `EventDealDamage` on OPPOSITE sides of the write on the combat and non-combat routes — a listener arm alone would recache the stale answer on the commonest route there is. The graveyard half is deliberately NOT gated on a flag: it widens the existing zone-keyed condition so any event whose `OldZone` / `NewZone` crosses a graveyard boundary bumps, arrivals and departures alike. The statics that read a graveyard are a pre-existing family of about ten (Tarmogoyf, Lord of Extinction, Nighthowler, Nighthawk Scavenger, Consuming Aberration, Jarad, Multani, Wight of the Reliquary, Elvish Reclaimer, The Warring Triad) that could not have declared a flag they predate, several of them built by shared helpers, and no test can catch a Lhurgoyf that forgets one; measured, the widening is one atomic add at emit and one 46us recompute at the next read. It was invisible until now because a creature DYING is a battlefield move and already bumped — only the mill, the discard and the resolving spell did not. **Attack-declaration invalidation is delivered too (#1218)** — the quarter #1117 left open. `StaticAbility.DependsOnAttackingStatus` is the third `DependsOnHandSize` sibling: the same opt-in walk, so a table with nothing reading "is this creature attacking" pays nothing for a declare-attackers step. The bump has THREE call sites because attacking status has three exits and only one of them is an event: `layerVersionBump.OnEvent`'s `EventAttack` arm covers the DECLARE half (the lock-in stamps `Card.AttackingTarget` before the event fires), and `Game.invalidateLayersForAttackChangeLocked` is called directly from `removeFromCombatLocked` (a control change pulling a permanent out mid-declaration, CR 506.4) and from `clearCombatLocked` (combat ending, CR 511.3) — neither mutates through an event that names every affected card, the same reason turn advance bumps from `onTurnBeganLocked` rather than a listener arm. **A fifth quarter, spells-cast invalidation, is delivered too (#1325)** — a dimension none of the first four rows named, found when Stoic Sphinx's "hexproof as long as you haven't cast a spell this turn" needed it. `StaticAbility.DependsOnSpellsCast` is the fourth `DependsOnHandSize` sibling, gated on the same opt-in walk; its bump is a single unconditional-shaped arm, `layerVersionBump.OnEvent`'s `EventCast` case, because the tally it reads (`Game.SpellsCastThisTurn`) has exactly one write site and that site already bumps the tally before the event fires (mutations.go), so there is no "removed mid-turn" exit the way attacking status has. All five quarters of this row are now closed.
    >
    > **Cards waiting:** Wonder (#300) — shipped with #1221, which closed "Statics read from a non-battlefield zone" (see Closed seams); its static lives in a graveyard and this row's graveyard bump is what keeps it fresh. Shipped: Psychosis Crawler (#74); Serra Ascendant (#1117), and with it four caveats the same change made false — Bloodghast's conditional haste, Righteous Valkyrie's +2/+2, and the graveyard-staleness notes on Lord of Extinction (now full) and Wight of the Reliquary; Ohran Frostfang (#1218); Stoic Sphinx (#1325)

- **Pick-from-exile / pick-from-graveyard prompt with continuation** (no issue) — closed on the 2026-09-24 re-triage (#1386): the row was fully stale. Its text, as it stood:

    > **What was missing:** prompt: a spell-side pick from exile or from a graveyard with a continuation, distinct from the sacrifice/discard pickers
    >
    > **Cards waiting:** Lord of the Void (#395); Necromantic Selection (#395)

- **Post-departure LKI / "exiled with this" record** (#1218) — closed on the 2026-09-24 re-triage (#1386): the row was fully stale. Its text, as it stood:

    > **What was missing:** **Closed for two of the three by #1218, and it turned out to need less than this row said.** The "exiled with this" record already existed by the time anyone looked again — `b27ExiledWith` (batch 27, after this row was written) reads it back off the event log for Duplicant, Angel of Serenity, Chrome Mox, Bag of Holding and Ossification — so Valakut Exploration and Currency Converter needed no new primitive at all: `b27ExiledWith` plus `game.CastPermission{Duration: WhileInZoneDuration()}` (the airbend primitive, exile_play.go) plus `PutCardsIntoGraveyardThenForEffect` (a new continuation sibling of `PutIntoGraveyardForEffect`, needed only so "deal that much damage" counts what actually landed after a CR 903.9 pause rather than before it, ADR 0013 §5t) covers both cards outright. The genuine gap was narrower than "no record of what a permanent exiled": it was **counters at departure**, which is not an exile question at all — `Characteristic` (the CR 603.10 LKI snapshot every leaves-the-battlefield trigger reads) deliberately excludes Counters ("belongs to other engine subsystems", characteristic.go), and `MoveCard`'s exit cleanup zeroes them a line after the snapshot is taken. `Game.lastKnownCounters` (triggers.go's `snapshotLKILocked`, alongside `lastKnownBattlefield`) and `LastKnownCountersForEffect` are the fix, kept on the GAME for the same reason the sibling maps are — the departing `Card` value is about to be overwritten out from under whatever holds a copy of it. It answers for a BYSTANDER's trigger too (The Ozolith is not the permanent that left), which its own `sourceLKI` could never have.
    >
    > **Cards waiting:** The Ozolith (#295) — counters-at-departure LKI, the actual new primitive. Shipped with no new primitive: Valakut Exploration (#307); Currency Converter (#387)

    The row is quoted from `docs/engine-seams.md` as of #1244, because it had already left the open table by the time #1402 moved the table into the registry; the two entries that follow this one (#1379 and #1396) closed what it still listed.

- **Post-departure LKI / "exiled with this" record — the last piece: a departed damage source's lifelink and deathtouch** (#1396, CR 608.2h / 400.7 / 702.15b / 702.2b, [ADR 0056 amendment 2026-09-24](decisions/0056-infect-wither-toxic.md) Decisions 9-11, closing its Decision 2 step 4). The row had three parts. #1218 closed two: the "exiled with this" record already existed (`b27ExiledWith`), and counters at departure became `Game.lastKnownCounters` (The Ozolith; Valakut Exploration and Currency Converter shipped with no new primitive). #1379 then added durable resolution-time LKI (`Game.lastKnownPermanents`, the entry below). The one piece left was the damage tail: `effectDamageTailLocked` read lifelink and deathtouch off the battlefield only, so a source that had left dealt plain damage. It now reads the departed object's record (`departedDamageSourceLocked` in `game/damage_tail.go`): deathtouch from its recorded abilities, and lifelink credited to its recorded controller. A damage call names its source two ways. **By object**, the new `Game.DealDamageFromObjectForEffect(ObjectRef, …)` / `effects.DealDamage{SourceObject: &ref}`, reads that object's record and never a new object the card has become since. **By instance ID**, every existing caller, reads the last object the card was, and only while its epoch is exactly one past the record's (it has not moved since it left), or when the card is a token that has ceased to exist. So every "when this dies, it deals damage" card gets the departed creature's keywords with no change to its file. `PermanentInfo` also gains `Tapped` (ADR 0056 Decision 11) for Mana Vault's resolution-time "if this artifact is tapped". The combat tail is unchanged (a combat source is on the battlefield or has a CR 510.1c frame). Proof cards: Warstorm Surge ships `full`; Murderous Redcap keeps only its persist caveat; Mana Vault's caveat narrows to a Vault that leaves and comes back before its draw-step trigger resolves (a triggered item does not carry its source's object epoch, #1418 — closed, entry above). Still open, stated in the amendment: ~~the damage event's `sourceLKI` (CR 702.16e protection colour) still reads the card in its current zone (#1417)~~ closed, entry above; infect, wither and toxic join the same reader when ADR 0056 PR 2 wires them into the tail. Trackers [#881](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/881).

- **Resolution-time last-known information for a permanent that has left the battlefield** (#1379, CR 608.2h / 400.7, [ADR 0018 amendment 2026-09-24](decisions/0018-triggers-on-the-stack.md) Decisions 12-13). The CR 603.10 store (`lastKnownBattlefield`, `lastKnownCounters`) is deleted when the exit's trigger harvest ends. An ability that read a permanent at RESOLUTION ("where X is that creature's power") found the card in its new zone, with printed power and no counters. `Game.lastKnownPermanents` (`server/internal/game/permanent_lki.go`) is the second store. `battlefieldExitLocked` writes it at the same moment as the CR 603.10 snapshot: one `PermanentInfo` per departed OBJECT, keyed by instance ID and told apart by `Card.ObjectEpoch`. Each record holds post-layer characteristics, power and toughness with counters, the counters, the controller and the attachment. It lasts for the rest of the turn (cleared with `lastKnownStack`) and is carried by Clone, RestoreFrom and the persisted snapshot. `PermanentForEffect(ObjectRef)` returns the live permanent while that object is still on the battlefield, and the record once it has gone. It never returns a new object that has the same card. `ObjectSnapshot` gains `Epoch`, the battlefield epoch for a departed object, so `effects.Context.TriggeringPermanent()` gives the ref to any trigger. Proof cards: Cream of the Crop, Tribute to the World Tree and Claustrophobia now ship `full`. Warstorm Surge and Murderous Redcap now deal the last-known damage, and their caveats were narrowed to the damage-source keywords, which #1396 then closed (next entry).

- **An activated ability's OWN cost clause, and a price that reads the ability's target** (#1296, CR 602.2b / 601.2c / 601.2f / 113.6, [ADR 0020 amendment 2026-09-24](decisions/0020-activated-abilities.md) Decisions 41-44) — narrows the open "Cost modification for activated abilities" row. `ActivatedAbilityShape.CostModifiers` (card side `ActivatedAbility.CostModifiers`) is "This ability costs {N} less to activate …": the activation twin of a spell's `SelfCostModifiers`, joined to the same CR 601.2f pass beside the board's activation-scoped modifiers, bound to the ability's source with the ACTIVATOR as its controller, and read from the query rather than the battlefield — so it works wherever the ability does. A clause with `ReadsTargets` is priced after the targets (`Game.AbilityManaCostForTargetsForEffect`, which `ActivateCatalogAbility` pays through with the targets it has just validated); `Game.AbilityPriceReadsTargetsForEffect` tells the enumerator to price each target set on its own (the cast path's §14 rule) and the view to ship `activated_abilities[i].target_charged_mana_costs`. `effects.CostsLessForTheCardItTargets` / `ColorsOf` / `CountersOf` and `ChannelDiscountPerLegendaryCreature` are the card vocabulary; `Register` refuses a clause on an ability with no mana cost, a `CostFloor`, `SpecialActions` or a designation gate. **Cards:** Dragonfire Blade (the equip discount ships; hexproof-from-monocolored stays caveated), Ghostfire Blade and Warrior's Blades (new, `full`), and Takenuma, Otawara and Boseiju (`full` — Takenuma's discount was a board modifier that the battlefield scan never found from the hand, so its "applies when you pay" caveat was false). **Not this seam, fixed in the same PR:** the reported "equip costs were not paid" was the client never sending `strict` / `auto_tap` on `activate_ability`, so every activation took the paper path. **Still open:** Belt of Giant Strength ("{X} less, where X is the power of the creature it targets") is expressible with `CostsLessForTheCardItTargets` and was not added; Arm-Mounted Anchor, Crown of Gondor, Plate Armor and the rest of the non-target "costs {N} less to activate" family are plain `CostsLessEach` / `CostsLess` clauses on the ability and are ordinary card work now.

- **"Exile this <permanent>" as a CR 602 cost, paid from the battlefield** (#1404, CR 602.2b / 406 / 903.9, [ADR 0020 amendment 2026-09-24](decisions/0020-activated-abilities.md) Decisions 45-46). This seam had no row of its own. #1381 found it and pinned Perpetual Timepiece in `knownOracleMismatches`. `AbilityCost.ExileSelf` now follows the ability's zone. `game.ExileSelfZoneSupported` (graveyard or battlefield) is the one predicate both `effects.Register` and `validateExileSelfCostLocked` read. From the battlefield the source leaves through the exit primitive's battlefield arm. Leaves-the-battlefield watchers fire; dies and sacrifice watchers do not. The source is gone before the ability resolves, and its leaves-triggers go on the stack above the ability. A commander's owner is asked about the command zone before anything is paid, through #1397's ask-first gate (ADR 0013 §5af), which already lists the source whenever the cost has `ExileSelf`. `AbilityAutoTapExclusions` excludes the exiled source. Cards: Perpetual Timepiece (caveat and pin removed), Hanged Executioner, Nyx Weaver, Feldon's Cane. Still open: a cost that exiles this AND other permanents (Mechtitan Core, craft).

- **Hideaway (CR 702.75): a face-down exile the controller may look at, linked to its source, with a free play** (#1331, CR 702.75a / 406.3 / 406.3a / 607.2a, [ADR 0091](decisions/0091-hideaway.md)) — the last named hideaway item on the face-down row. **A seventh exile-side kind**, `FaceDownHidden` (`"hideaway"`), whose viewer is the one rule no other kind has: the controller of ANOTHER object, the permanent that exiled the card. `faceDownViewersLocked` reads it through `Card.HiddenBy`, and `hideawayKnowersSweepLocked` (with the state checks, never "fired") adds the current controller when the permanent changes hands — CR 406.3 keeps anyone who has already looked. **The link** is `Card.HiddenBy`, the permanent OBJECT {instance, epoch}, stamped by the exile route before the knowers are computed (`zoneRoute.HiddenBy`, `ExileHiddenForEffect`) and cleared by `MoveCard` on every move; `HiddenCardsForEffect(ref)` is CR 607.2a's "the exiled card". Card-carried rather than the effects package's event-log exiled-with record (`b27ExiledWith`) because the viewer rule is answered inside the engine about one `Card`; it is exact in the same way that record is since #1239 — the epoch pins the incarnation, so a hideaway land bounced and replayed has no claim on its old card. **The free play of a hidden spell** is an ADR 0066 grant over the linked card written by the card's own ability (`effects.PlayHiddenCard`): `{0}`, not cast-only (hideaway says *play*), `TimingFlash`, until end of turn — the cascade / Malcolm grant-not-inline posture, declared. The link is read off the activation's `SourceEpoch` (`HiddenRefOfActivation`) or captured in a trigger's Build, so a hideaway land destroyed in response still plays its card. `effects.Hideaway(name, n)` is CR 702.75a whole: look (controller only), a mandatory one-of-N `choose_cards` pick, the face-down exile, the rest to the bottom in a random order. **4 cards**, all `full`: Rabble Rousing (#1306's card), Mosswort Bridge (#294), Windbrisk Heights (#298), Spinerock Knoll (#299). **A hidden land is played during the resolution** (ADR 0091 decision 5): `Game.PlayLandDuringResolutionForEffect` is a land play through the one land entry finisher — it spends a land drop (CR 305.2a), needs no main phase or empty stack, and is ignored with no drop left or on another player's turn (CR 305.2b / 305.3) — offered through cascade's `may_cast` prompt; cascade and Malcolm can reuse it for a land. **Still open:** Evercoat Ursine's two hideaways and "one of them"; Watcher for Tomorrow's leaves-the-battlefield read of the link (the LTB needs the departed object's epoch captured at trigger time); and the other hideaway cards, each on its own condition. Trackers [#886](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/886) and [#889](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/889).

- **"Exile N cards from your graveyard / hand" as an activated ability's cost** (#1297, CR 602.2b / 118.3 / 406, [ADR 0020 amendment 2026-09-23](decisions/0020-activated-abilities.md) Decisions 35-37, [ADR 0073 amendment 2026-09-23](decisions/0073-optional-additional-costs-and-the-cast-gate.md) Decision 7) — the open remainder the #1242 / #1283 / #1285 entry below named. `AbilityCost.ExileCards` is #1283's `game.ExileCost` with a SECOND OWNER, not a second component: the candidate walk (`ExileCostOptionsForEffect`), the validator (refusing overlap with the discards and the source) and the payer (the one exit primitive with `MustSettleNow`) are the mana ability's, now called from `ActivateCatalogAbility`. The component grew `From` (`ZoneHand` / `ZoneGraveyard`; zero reads as the hand, so every #1283 declaration is unchanged), because the printed CR 602 form is overwhelmingly the graveyard and the two piles are one rule read against two of the activator's own zones; `effects.Register` refuses any other zone and a zero count on both owners through one check. `PaidCost.Exiled` records the exiled cards, which is how "the card exiled this way" (Holistic Wisdom, Dread Defiler) is answered at resolution. The hand form joins `AbilityAutoTapExclusions`, so a Spirit Guide named to the cost is never also exiled for the mana. Wire: `activate_ability`'s `exile_ids`, and `exile_cost_n` / `_label` / `_options` / `_zone` on the ability view (the mana view gained `_zone`). The enumerator offers ONE payment out of the engine's own walk, cheapest fuel first when the policy has an opinion (`Options.OrderCostFuel`, escape's price); the heuristic prices the exiled cards with the same `fuelValue`. Client: Board's announce chain asks after the discard, through `DiscardCostModal` with the verb changed and the pile named (`exileCost.ts`). Proof cards, all `full`: **Mines of Moria** (its caveat named this seam), **Grim Lavamancer**, **Moorland Haunt**, **Tome Shredder** (the "instant or sorcery card" predicate) and **Holistic Wisdom** (the hand form and the paid record). Still open: the variable forms — "Exile X cards" (Necropolis Fiend, Taigam), "one or more" (Corpseweft) and "any number … with total mana value 30 or greater" (The Capitoline Triad) — which are an ANNOUNCED count, the #1213 variable-sacrifice question one component over; craft's "exile a creature you control or a creature card from your graveyard", which spans two zones in one clause; and a MANA ability that exiles from the graveyard (Molt Tender, Titans' Nest), which the component now expresses (`ExileCardsFromGraveyard`) but which no catalog card declares yet.

- **A land entry does not say whether it was PLAYED (CR 305.4)** (#1326, CR 305.1 / 305.4, [ADR 0061 amendment 2026-09-23](decisions/0061-token-creation-and-discard-are-replaceable-events.md)) — the *Aang is so flashy* deck triage (#1306) filed it against Deep Gnome Terramancer's "whenever one or more lands enter under an opponent's control **without being played**", which cannot be written against a guess. `Event.Played` is stamped by `announceEntryLocked` (the shared entry finisher above) from the settled entry's `landPlay` flag — the one the land-drop tally already reads — true for the land branch of `CastSpell` (a play from hand, an impulse-exile grant or a graveyard permission, whichever zone the branch draws from) and unset (the zero value) for every "put" path and for a token. The land branch's own push had never actually been migrated to call the shared finisher — unlike the stack-resolution site #653 fixed — so it duplicated the move, tally bump and events inline and would have stamped `Played` only on a PAUSED land; it now calls `executeEntryToBattlefieldLocked` and the duplicate is gone. Three catalog readers that used to GUESS the distinction from the entry's origin zone and the land-drop tally's arithmetic (`b20LandPlayed`, `b10AnotherLandPlayedByYou`) now read the field directly, closing the caveats on Horn of Greed, Prosper, Tome-Bound and City of Traitors — plus a fourth pair #1360 declared while this was in flight (a land `PutFromHandOntoBattlefieldForEffect` puts from hand still drew a card on Horn of Greed and The Endstone, stronger than printed), whose pinned test flips to assert no draw. **Cards:** Deep Gnome Terramancer (new, complete); Horn of Greed, Prosper, Tome-Bound, City of Traitors and The Endstone lose their land-play-guess caveat and are complete. Trackers [#884](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/884) and [#882](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/882).

- **Station (CR 702.184, 721)** (#759, [ADR 0071 addendum 2026-09-23](decisions/0071-designations-that-switch-abilities-on.md)) — the station ABILITY, on #758's tap-another cost. The threshold half shipped with ADR 0071 (#969); what closed here is "Tap another untapped creature you control: Put charge counters equal to its power on this permanent. Activate only as a sorcery." `effects.Station()` is the whole keyword: `TapAnotherUntapped` (a `TapOthersCost` of one, a creature filter, `ExcludeSource` for "another"), `SorcerySpeed`, and no `ActiveWhen` gate (CR 721.4). **Which power** is CR 608.2h's answer, confirmed by the Edge of Eternities release notes: the tapped creature's power AS THE ABILITY RESOLVES, or as it last existed on the battlefield if it left — not the power it was tapped with, which is what ADR 0071 had assumed. So the payment record (`PaidCost.TappedOthers []PaidTap`) carries the tapped object's ID and epoch, `Context.TappedPower()` reads it live through `Game.PaidTapPowerForEffect`, and the one battlefield-exit choke point (`battlefieldExitLocked`) freezes the last-known power onto any stack item that tapped the leaving object. Zero or negative power puts nothing on and takes nothing off; a source that left or was replayed as a new object gets nothing (`AbilitySourceGoneForEffect`). The wire carries the cost's options (`tap_others_label` / `tap_others_options`), the enumerator offers one move per creature, the heuristic prices the tapped creature's attack before combat, and the client reuses the sacrifice picker with the verb "Tap". **Cards:** The Seriema (#337) goes Full; Galvanizing Sawship, Uthros Research Craft (a gated trigger) and Adagia, Windswept Bastion (a Planet — a land with station) ship Full. Still open, stated: CR 702.184c modifiers (Tapestry Warden) and CR 721.2c (no P/T outside the battlefield).

- **A creature blocks only what its controller is defending against** (#1339, CR 802.4a / 509.1a, [ADR 0045 amendment 2026-09-23](decisions/0045-combat-restrictions.md), Decision 34) — no row of its own: a rules bug found while testing #1329, not a missing mechanic. `DeclareBlockers` took any pairing whose blocker the caller controlled, so at a four-seat table seat 1 could block the creature attacking seat 2; the option generator (`blockOptionsLocked`) already offered each seat only its own attackers, so bots were right and a human client could send what no bot was offered. **The check** is `blockDefenderRefusalLocked` (`game/block_declaration.go`), one per-entry line in Decision 13's validator reading the same `defendingPlayerForAttackLocked` the generator reads — the player attacked, a planeswalker's controller, a battle's protector — and refusing with the reserved token `not_defending`. A pairing already standing is not re-judged, so an attacker reselected after it was blocked (CR 508.7a, #1329) keeps its blockers. **The wire** stamps `defending_player` on every attacker (`protocol/view.go` `stampCombatTargets`), and the client's four block pickers read it through `attackersDefendedBy` (`client/src/lib/attackTargets.ts`); the click paths now also offer the attacks on a planeswalker the viewer controls or a battle they protect. **Closed since (#1364, Decision 35):** CR 506.4c's "it may be blocked" for an attacker whose planeswalker or battle has left. `Game.attackDefenders` records the defending player whenever an attack is pointed (`setAttackTargetLocked` in `game/attack_target.go` — the two declaration verbs, an entry "attacking", a CR 508.7 reselect), and the block path — generator, verb, refusal sentence, landwalk, the enumerator's pre-filter and the wire's `defending_player` — reads `defendingPlayerForAttackerLocked`, which falls back to it once the live target is gone. Damage still goes nowhere (CR 510.1b). **Closed since (#1376, Decision 36):** an attacked planeswalker or battle removed from combat WITHOUT leaving the battlefield — a control change (`materialiseControlLocked`) or phasing out (`phaseOutLocked`) — takes its attackers with it: `removeAttackedFromCombatLocked` (`game/attack_target.go`) re-points every announced attacker at the reserved `game.AttackingNothing` id, so they still attack, deal no damage to it or its new controller, and are blocked by the recorded defender. **Closed since (#1387, Decision 37):** the type clause — an attacked permanent that stops being a planeswalker or battle is removed from combat for good. `recomputeLayersLocked` (`game/layers.go`) calls `removeTypeLostAttackTargetsLocked` (`game/attack_target.go`) after each layer pass; it checks only the permanents announced attackers name and re-points the attackers of any that is no longer a planeswalker or battle at `AttackingNothing`, so a type that comes back later in the same combat does not bring the attack back. No proof cards: this is a legality gate every block crosses, not a card's missing shape. Tracker [#880](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/880).

- **Waterbend as an activated ability's cost, and as a ward's payment** (#1310 / #1311, CR 701.67a, [ADR 0020 amendment 2026-09-23](decisions/0020-activated-abilities.md) and [ADR 0073 amendment 2026-09-23](decisions/0073-optional-additional-costs-and-the-cast-gate.md)) — S22's `game.TapPermanentsCost` gained two owners rather than a sibling: `AbilityCost.Waterbend` ("Waterbend {N}:") and `CounterUnlessPaidPrompt.Waterbend` ("Ward—Waterbend {N}"). On both, the waterbend mana is the cost itself, so it stays in the ordinary mana component — X detection, the CR 601.2f cost-modifier pass, the view's cost chip and every affordability check read it unchanged — and the clause's `Extra` names the part the taps may cover (CR 701.67b). One budget (`game.WaterbendBudget`: the clause's generic, capped by what the priced cost still charges), one reduction (`game.WaterbendReduced`, applied after the modifier pass) and one walk (`Game.WaterbendOptionsForEffect`) are shared by the engine, the view and the enumerator (#544). Activation: `waterbend_ids` on `activate_ability`, validated with every other component, excluded from the auto-tapper (CR 118.3), tapped with the ability on the stack; not the {T} symbol, so a summoning-sick creature — and a source whose cost prints no {T} — may pay (CR 302.6). Prompt: `PendingChoiceView.tap_cost` and `resolve_choice { apply: true, tap_ids }` through `Game.ResolvePayUnlessWithTaps`; the mana remainder is settled first and nothing taps if it cannot be, and a malformed tap list is refused with the prompt left open. `effects.WaterbendCost` / `effects.WardWaterbend` build them; `Plus` sums a waterbend's mana with another mana component and no longer drops `TapOthers`. The enumerator offers one payment (free artifacts, then creatures, then mana sources; the largest reachable X), and the client reuses TapCostModal for the ability and lists waterbenders in the pay-unless modal. **Cards:** Aang, Swift Savior (caveat dropped, `full`); Katara, Water Tribe's Hope (`full`); The Unagi of Kyoshi Island (`full`); Avatar Kuruk's exhaust ability now waits on extra turns (#753) alone. Still open: CR 701.67c "whenever a player waterbends" (no card asks), waterbend on a mana ability (none printed), and convoke on an activated ability.

- **A "put onto the battlefield" batch that can ask, from more than one zone, and an exile return that reports what arrived** (#1322, #1324, #1327, CR 614.12a / 614.12b / 603.6a / 400.7, [ADR 0061 amendment 2026-09-23](decisions/0061-token-creation-and-discard-are-replaceable-events.md)) — three entry-path gaps the *Aang is so flashy* deck triage (#1306) filed together because they share one body. **The batch is resumable** (`game/entry_batch.go`): `putOntoBattlefieldFromZoneLocked` was the last effect-side entry built without `entryResumable`, because its only possible resume landed one card after its siblings had been announced. It now walks its cards' CR 614 windows one at a time and carries itself across a question on `entryTail.batch`; the resume hands each settled event back to the batch instead of landing it, and the batch lands, announces and runs hooks for every card once the last answer is in. CR 614.12b follows from the order (a payment made for the first card shrinks what the second can pay). **One entry from two zones**: `Game.PutOntoBattlefieldTogetherThenForEffect([]BatchEntry{{CardID, From}}, opts, then)` with `From` hand, library or exile (an exiled card returns as a new object). **The exile return has a continuation**: `ReturnFromExileToBattlefieldThenForEffect` / `effects.ReturnFromExile.Then`, told the new object's ID once the entry is complete. The finisher `executeEntryToBattlefieldLocked` is split into `landEntryLocked` / `announceEntryLocked` / `runEntryHooksLocked` so the batch runs the same landing the single entry does — which also gives a Clone put by Genesis Wave its copy choice. Two fixes the batch exposed: a face-down entry (manifest) no longer applies the entering card's own replacements (CR 708.2a), and the copy prompt's resume goes through the shared finisher so a cancel after it cannot strand a batch. **Cards:** Sword of Hearth and Home and Phelia, Exuberant Shepherd registered complete; every shockland (10), pay-life / enters-tapped / Pathway MDFC land back (60) and reveal-land (10) loses its "a spell PUTS it onto the battlefield" caveat and is complete. Still unresumable: the sandbox `move_card` verb only.

- **A spell exiled from the stack takes its stack record with it; "exile target spell"; plot's effect half; and the cause of a zone change** (#1318, #1320, CR 400.7 / CR 701.6 / CR 702.170c-d / CR 603.2c, [ADR 0013 §5ad](decisions/0013-replacement-effects.md), [ADR 0066 amendment 2026-09-23](decisions/0066-granted-cast-and-play-permissions.md)) — no row of its own: both were filed straight from the *Aang is so flashy* deck triage (#1306). **#1318** was a live bug on the deck's commander. Aang, Swift Savior airbends "a creature or SPELL" through the plain exile route, which moved the card and retired the `StackMeta` record only when the route said `DropStackMeta` — and only the counter / return-to-hand body and the sandbox move did. The spell's record stayed behind and the table wedged. The flag is gone: `executeZoneRouteLocked` retires the record, and `routeCardToZoneLocked` takes the CR 608.2h last-known record, whenever the card came FROM the stack, whatever verb asked. `Game.ExileSpellForEffect` / `ExileSpellThenForEffect` (`effects.ExileTargetSpell`) is the third verb over `exitSpellFromStackLocked` — not a counter, refuses a non-spell, and its `Then` hears whether the card really reached exile. `Game.PlotExiledCardForEffect` (`effects.PlotExiled`, `game/plot.go`) is CR 702.170d as an ADR 0066 permission with a new `TimingPlot` that no flash grant widens; the plot KEYWORD's hand special action followed in #1342 (see "Special actions from the hand" below). **#1320:** a routed zone change now carries `Event.Cause` / `CauseController` / `CauseItem` — `effect` inferred from the resolving item (`Game.resolving`), and `cost`, `special_action`, `rule` and `manual` named by the routes that know it, which is what keeps a stale resolving slot from claiming a cost. `game.ExiledBySpellOrAbilityOf` reads it. **#1341, closed:** two foretells in one priority window used to share one event batch, so `OncePerBatch` counted one occurrence where CR 603.2c counts two (each special action is its own event); `PerformSpecialAction` now opens its own batch (`event_batch.go`), the same boundary a resolution or a step entry opens. Proof cards: Aang, Swift Savior airbending a spell (still caveated for waterbend, #1310); Aven Interrupter (`full`); **Ranar the Ever-Watchful is now `full`** — the foretell discount (#1319) and the one-event-batch gap (#1341) were its last two caveats and both are closed. Still open, as a general engine limitation rather than a per-card caveat: the destroy / SBA exit carries no cause, so a Rest in Peace exile is not "a spell you control exiled it"; a cost is recorded but not counted as "a spell or ability exiles".

- **Preparation cards (CR 722): the prepared designation and casting the prepare spell as a copy** (#1328, CR 722.2-722.3c / 707.12 / 704.5e, [ADR 0090](decisions/0090-preparation-cards.md)) — the seam had no row; ADR 0034 recorded the mechanic as out of scope ("needs spell-copying, which does not exist") and `deck/validate.go` warned "preparing isn't implemented yet". **The designation** is `Card.Prepared`, beside ADR 0071's level and solved marker and under their rules (not copiable, cleared by CR 400.7, kept through phasing, carried by the snapshot), with one writer that refuses what CR 722.3a refuses — no prepare spell, or already prepared — as a no-op. It is not a `DesignationKind`: it switches a cast on, not a printed ability. **The copy** is a real object created in place in exile from the permanent's copiable values (so a Clone of a preparation card has its prepare spell, CR 722.2b), face 1 active so it resolves through the `"#1"` catalog entry, with the front face's card-level keywords dropped (a flash creature must not hand its sorcery flash). `Card.PrepareCopy` marks it not a card and keeps `MoveCard`'s CR 712.8a front-face reset off it; `Card.PreparedBy` names the permanent OBJECT {instance, epoch} and is cleared on every move. **The permission is derived** (`prepareCopyPermissionLocked`), consulted by `CastPermissionForLocked`, `CastPermissionOnCardForEffect` and `AnyCastPermissionsForEffect`, so the cast path, the bot enumerator and the wire's `exile_play` agree and CR 722.3c's "for as long as" holds by construction; a prepare copy answers ONLY through it. **The cast** is the ordinary exile cast plus two lines: `StackItem.IsCopy` (CR 707.10's exits end it) and the unprepare at CR 601.2i just before `EventCast`. **One sweep**, `prepareCopySweepSBALocked` beside the CR 704.5d token sweep, is CR 704.5e with CR 722.3c's exception; `phaseInLocked` makes a fresh copy for a permanent that "phases in prepared". "Enters prepared" is `effects.SelfEntersPrepared()` (CR 614.1d, `ReplacementEvent.EntersPrepared`, applied by all three battlefield landings before `EventETB`); "becomes prepared" is `effects.BecomePrepared`. Wire: `CardView.prepared` and a "PREPARED" designation badge; the copy rides the existing `exile_play`. **4 cards**, all `full`: Skycoach Conductor // All Aboard (#1306's card), Landscape Painter // Vibrant Idea, Encouraging Aviator // Jump, Skycoach Waypoint. **Still open:** a Clone entering as a copy of a preparation card does not pick up the copied card's "enters prepared" (the entry's replacements are gathered before the copy applies), CR 722.3d (a copy of a prepare SPELL is a prepare spell) and CR 722.5 (naming a prepare spell's name) have no reader, and #1340 — the spell copies CR 704.5e also covers, which `PrepareCopy` was deliberately not widened to. Trackers [#889](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/889) and [#885](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/885).

- **Ending the combat phase, and reselecting an attack** (#1317, #1329, CR 724.2 / CR 508.7, [ADR 0045 amendment 2026-09-23](decisions/0045-combat-restrictions.md), Decisions 32-33) — two effects that reach into a combat already under way, neither with a row of its own (each had one waiting card before the *Aang is so flashy* triage, #1306). **`EndCombatPhaseForEffect`** (`game/end_combat.go`) is CR 724.2 as its printed procedure: a no-op outside combat (724.2g); `PendingTriggers` emptied (724.2a); every stack object exiled, the resolving one included, spells through the shared stack-exit door `exitSpellFromStackLocked` so each `StackMeta` record leaves with its card (the #1318 wedge — this uses the unexported door because #1318's exported verb had not merged), copies ceasing to exist, ability items deleted rather than countered (724.2b); `clearCombatLocked` and a cursor walk through `advanceCursorLocked` that enters no skipped step, so the end of combat step is never announced and "at end of combat" triggers never fire (724.2d/e). 724.2c's SBA check is folded into the resolution frame's own check, which runs next with nobody having had priority. **`ReselectAttackTargetForEffect`** (`game/attack_reselect.go`) writes `Card.AttackingTarget` and nothing else — no `EventAttack`, no removal from combat, the announcement and blocked records untouched (508.7a), no restriction, tap or tax (508.7b) — validated by `canAttackTargetLocked` against the attacking creature's controller (508.7c); 508.7d does not apply in Commander (CR 903.2). Its prompt, **`QueueReselectAttackForEffect`**, is an `option_pick` with "keep" first (the always-legal branch) and an answer read off the picked option's subject through a new `optionPickFrame.thenSubject`, #994's `thenSeat` generalised to seats-and-permanents, so a seat pruned mid-question cannot shift the answer. No wire change. **4 cards:** Misleading Signpost (the deck card), Portal Mage and Windshaper Planetar `full`; Mandate of Peace `caveats` — its "your opponents can't cast spells this turn" is #1316, a separate PR. **Still open:** Capricopian, whose reselect is activated by "the player this creature is attacking" — an activator that is not the controller, which `ActivatedAbility` cannot say; and extra combat phases (the Open row "Extra combat and main phases"), without which "the next phase" is always the postcombat main phase. Trackers [#880](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/880) and [#884](https://github.com/krakenhavoc/cmd_and_ctrl/issues/884).

- **`ReturnFromGraveyard` has no "enters tapped" option** (#1284) — the last piece of "Ability activatable from a non-battlefield zone" that #1221's activated half left on the row for Reassembling Skeleton and Drownyard Temple, both of which print "Return this card from your graveyard to the battlefield tapped" behind the closed graveyard activation. `ReturnFromGraveyard.Tapped` is `SearchLibrary.TappedOnEntry`'s shape one primitive over: it threads through a new `Game.ReturnFromGraveyardTappedForEffect` to `returnFromGraveyardLocked`'s existing (previously unexposed) `tapped` parameter, which already stamped `EntersTapped` on the CR 614 entry event — the same field `ReturnToBattlefieldForEffect`'s exile-return path has used since #1178, just never reachable from the graveyard-under-control path. A new function rather than a fourth parameter on `ReturnFromGraveyardUnderControlForEffect`, which has call sites across the catalog and the engine's own tests all meaning "untapped": a bare trailing bool is the kind of change a diff reviews past. Both cards are `full`; the shared body (`returnThisFromGraveyardTapped`) lives in `reassembling_skeleton.go` and is called from `drownyard_temple.go`. **Still open on this row**: the per-instance exile GRANT (Greater Gargadon while suspended) named below has no shape yet.

- **Gift (CR 702.174)** (#1267, CR 702.174a-m — gift is **702.174** in the pinned August 2026 edition, not the 702.170 the issue cites, [ADR 0089](decisions/0089-gift.md)) — the seam had no row of its own; it was filed while re-triaging roadmap batch 02 (#295), where **Dawn's Truce** (`edhrec_rank` ~360) was waiting, and six catalog cards carried "the gift can't be promised" caveats. **Gift is an ADR 0073 optional cost** whose payment names a player: `AdditionalCost.ChoosesOpponent`, keyed `game.GiftKey`, announced as its index in `optional_costs` plus `CastSpellParams.GiftOpponent` (`cast_spell.gift_opponent`), validated before the targets against `GiftOpponentsLocked` (other seats still in the game), and recorded as `PaidCost.GiftOpponent` — copied by CR 707.10, carried onto the permanent as `CastProvenance.GiftOpponent` by CR 400.7d, shown as `StackItemView.gift_to`. **A paid optional cost may now rewrite the target clause** (`AdditionalCost.Targets`, `game.TargetSpecUnderOptionalCosts`, applied after the alternative-cost rewrite at announce, resolution, the enumerator and the view) — CR 702.174m's "instead … target …", including a clause only the promise has. **Cards declare `Spec.Gift`** (`GiftACard` / `GiftAFood` / `GiftATappedFish` / `GiftATreasure`, `.Instead(clause)`), and `buildDef` grows the cost, the before-`OnResolve` gift for an instant or sorcery (CR 702.174j) and the intervening-if entry trigger for a permanent (CR 702.174b); the card reads `ctx.GiftPromised()`. The bot is offered the unpromised cast and one promise per opponent. Adopted on Dawn's Truce, Into the Flood Maw, Long River's Pull, Wear Down and Peerless Recycling (all now `full`), plus Scrapshooter (the permanent half) and Valley Rally (a Food, and a target only the promise has). **Still open:** Starfall Invocation (its promised branch needs a pick among the cards its own wipe moved — pinned in `coverage/caveats_test.go` under the new `gift` probe), gift an extra turn (no extra-turn primitive), gift an Octopus, and CR 702.174c's "whenever a player gives a gift" (no reader).

- **The auto-tapper cracks a sacrifice-ONLY source, an exile-a-card cost on a mana ability, and a preview that names every source** (#1242, #1283, #1285, [ADR 0011](decisions/0011-mana-pool-and-auto-tapper.md) amendment 2026-09-23) — closes the "Still open" item both earlier ADR 0011 amendments carried (#1215's and #1228's) and the `ManaAbilityCost.ExileCards` item #1228's entry below named. **#1242:** a Gold token, an Eldrazi Spawn and an Eldrazi Scion print "Sacrifice this: Add …" with no `{T}`, and `autoTapAbilityFor` demanded a `{T}`, so a board of them read as "missing {3}" to the cast preview, the strict gate and every bot. The picker now demands that the ability cost the source SOMETHING (a `{T}` or itself); the tapped check moved after the pick and asks only of a `{T}` (`manaSourceTappedOut`, shared by planner and executor), so a tapped Gold is still a source; and the executor pays the tap as a component — a cracked Spawn is never tapped, emits no `EventTapCard`, is not "tapped for mana" (CR 106.12a) and fires no triggered mana ability. Nothing new rides the plan: both halves read it off the picked ability. The creature question is answered as an ORDER — `tapSource.SacrificesCreature`, a sub-tier inside the sacrifice tier, `Wanted → LeavesHand → Sacrifices → SacrificesCreature → Frozen` — not as #758's exclusion, because a Spawn's own text names it a mana source. Making Spawn plannable made a CR 118.3 gap common: `game.CastAutoTapExclusions` / `AbilityAutoTapExclusions` keep the planner off anything the announcement already named (a Spawn offered to Village Rites, a Spirit Guide offered to Thrill of Possibility), read by `CastSpell`, `ActivateCatalogAbility`, the `/autotap` preview and the enumerator's per-payment re-check. **#1283:** `ManaAbilityShape.ExileCards` / `game.ExileCost` is "Exile a card from your hand" — `DiscardCost`'s shape, NOT a discard (one exit primitive, no discard event, madness does not see it) — with its own wire triple (`exile_cost_*`, answered with `exile_ids`), the discard modal with the verb changed, and the enumerator's one-payment arm; the auto-tapper never plans it. **#1285:** the preview's `plan` IDs gain `sources`, each `{card_id, name, zone, tap, sacrifice, exile}` read through the executor's own pickers, and the client names every card leaving the hand ("Taps 2 permanents and exiles Simian Spirit Guide from your hand"). Proof cards: Gold, Eldrazi Spawn and Eldrazi Scion paying a cast through auto-tap; Deadly Dispute refusing to crack the Spawn it sacrifices; Cadaverous Bloom (`full`); the Simian Spirit Guide preview. ~~Still open: `AbilityCost.ExileCards`, the CR 602 owner, mostly the GRAVEYARD form (Grim Lavamancer, Scarblade Elite, Moorland Haunt — #1297).~~ (closed by #1297, above)

- **Targeting an activated or triggered ability on the stack** (#1211, CR 115.4 / CR 701.6a, [ADR 0019 amendment 2026-09-23](decisions/0019-structured-targeting.md), [ADR 0043 amendment 2026-09-23](decisions/0043-copy-effects.md)) — the row three closed seams above left open in their "still open" clause (`Stack-item retarget`, `Ability-copy`, `Modal and multi-target clauses`) and ADR 0065 named first: every target clause walked ZONES, the stack zone holds spell cards, and an activated or triggered ability is a `StackMeta` entry with a synthetic id and no card anywhere — so "counter target activated or triggered ability" and the "or ability" half of "target spell or ability" were unwritable. **The gap was never the rule.** The engine has been able to COUNTER an ability since S13.1 (`counterAbilityLocked`); what it could not do was NAME one. #1223 built the half it needed for Strionic Resonator (`TargetSpec.Abilities` + `AbilityOK`, enumerating `StackMeta` by `Seq`), and generalising it to the whole catalog is one constructor: **`TargetSpellOrAbility` sets `Zones: {ZoneStack}` AND `Abilities: true` on one spec**, so the zone walk finds the spell cards, `abilityItemsBySeqLocked` finds the ability items, both append to `LegalTargets.Cards` and both answer an ordinary `TargetRef{Kind: TargetCard}` — no fifth `TargetRefKind`, because the wire, the picker, `internal/legal` and the CR 608.2b re-check all key on "a uuid that has to still be there", which is as true of an item as of a card. The two id spaces overlap by design (a spell item's id IS its card's instance id) and `specMatchLocked` resolves it by checking `StackMeta` first and excluding `StackItemSpell` there. **The predicate vocabulary is over the ITEM**, not the card: Bolt Bend's "with a single target" is a fact about the announcement and a spell and an ability have the same announcement record, so `StackItemPredicate` narrows both halves at once (`ItemHasASingleTarget`, `AnAbilityItem`, `ASpellItem(Legendary())`, `AnyStackItem`) — which is how Tale's End says "activated ability, triggered ability, or legendary spell" in one clause. **A mana ability is unreachable for free**: CR 605.3b keeps it off the stack, so the printed "(Mana abilities can't be targeted.)" needs no predicate, no check and no caveat. **The CR 702 keyword gate stays off** — an ability is neither a permanent nor a player, so nothing about it can have hexproof — and **CR 701.6a is a deletion, not a zone change**: no `routeCardToZoneLocked`, no CR 903.9 window, no destination to redirect, and the source permanent does not move. Two bugs on that path are fixed rather than worked around: `CounterAbility` (the public entry) was a second copy of the deletion, the #529 shape that let the two counter paths drift, and now delegates; and the emitted `EventCounterSpell` put the COUNTERED ability's own source in `Source`, which `events.go` reserves for the counter and the game log reads as "who countered it" — so the line came out as *"Llanowar Elves countered `<uuid>`"*, the victim in the attacker's place and a raw id in the victim's.
